imprisonment). <L 1996-07-10/42, art. 3, 018; En vigueur : 11-08-1996> If it has not resulted in prejudice to the King's freedom, and if it has not caused him bloodshed, injury or illness, the attack on his person shall be punished (by imprisonment from twenty to thirty years). <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 17, 040; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 102.

<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 18, 040; En vigueur : 13-03-2003> An attack on the life of the presumptive heir to the crown shall be punished by life imprisonment.

An attack on his person shall be punished by imprisonment from twenty years to thirty years. If it has not resulted in prejudice to his freedom and if it has not caused him bloodshed, injury or illness, the attack on his person shall be punished by imprisonment from fifteen years to twenty years.

###### Art. 103. (See NOTE under TITLE) An attack on the life of the queen, of the king's

relatives and in-laws in direct line, of the king's brothers, having Belgian nationality, on the life of the regent, or on the life of ministers exercising, in the cases provided for by the Constitution, the constitutional powers of the King, shall always be punished as a completed act.

(An attack on their person shall be punished by imprisonment from ten years to fifteen years; it shall be punished by imprisonment from five years to ten years, if it has not resulted in prejudice to their freedom and if it has not caused them bloodshed, injury or illness.) <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 19, 040; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 104. (See NOTE under TITLE) An attack the purpose of which is either to destroy

or to change the form of government or the order of succession to the throne, or to make citizens or inhabitants take up arms against the royal authority, the legislative Chambers or one of them, shall be punished by detention (from twenty years to thirty years). <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 20, 040; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 105. An attack exists as soon as there is a punishable attempt.

###### Art. 106. (See NOTE under TITLE) Conspiracy against the life or against the person

of the King shall be punished by fifteen years to twenty years of (imprisonment), if it has been followed by an act committed to prepare its execution, and by ten years to fifteen years of the same penalty, in the contrary case. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 21, 040; En vigueur : 13-03-

###### Art. 107. (See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 22, 040; En vigueur : 13- 03-2003> Conspiracy against the life or against the person of the heir presumptive to the crown shall be punished by ten years to fifteen years of imprisonment, if it has been followed by an act committed to prepare its execution, and by five years to ten years of imprisonment, in the contrary case.

###### Art. 108. (See NOTE under TITLE) Conspiracy against the life or against the person,

either of members of the royal family enumerated in article 103, or of the regent, or of ministers exercising the constitutional powers of the King, shall be punished by (imprisonment of five years to ten years). <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 23, 040; En vigueur : 13-

###### Art. 109. (See NOTE under TITLE) Conspiracy formed to achieve one of the purposes

mentioned in article 104 shall be punished by ten years to fifteen years of detention, if any act has been committed to prepare its execution, and by five years to ten years of the same penalty, in the contrary case.

###### Art. 110. There is conspiracy as soon as the resolution to act has been agreed between several

persons.

###### Art. 111. The proposal made and not accepted to form a conspiracy against the life

or against the person of the King, of the heir presumptive to the crown, of members of the Royal Family enumerated in article 103, of the regent, or of ministers exercising the constitutional powers of the King, shall be punished by imprisonment of one year to five years.

The guilty party (...) may, moreover, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33. <L 09-04-1930, art. 32>

###### Art. 112. (See NOTE under TITLE) Whoever shall have formed alone the resolution to

commit an attack against the life or against the person of the King, of the heir presumptive to the crown, of members of the Royal Family enumerated in article 103, of the regent or of ministers exercising the constitutional powers of the King, shall be punished by (imprisonment of five years to ten years), when he shall have committed an act to prepare its execution. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 23, 040; En vigueur : 13-03-

##### CHAPITRE II. - CRIMES AND OFFENCES AGAINST THE EXTERNAL SECURITY OF

THE STATE.

###### Art. 113. (Any Belgian who shall have borne arms against Belgium shall be punished (by

detention for life).) <AL 11-10-1916, art. 1> <L 1996-07-10/42, art. 15, 018; En vigueur : 11-08-1996>

(For the application of this provision, constitutes the act of bearing arms against Belgium, that of knowingly performing for the enemy tasks of combat, transport, work or surveillance, which normally fall to enemy armies or their services.) <AL 17-12-1942, art. 1>

###### Art. 114.

<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 24, 040; En vigueur : 13-03-2003> Whoever shall have engaged in machinations or maintained intelligence with a foreign power or with any person acting in the interest of a foreign power, to induce that power to undertake war against Belgium, or to provide it with the means, shall be punished by detention of twenty years to thirty years. If hostilities have ensued, he shall be punished by detention for life.

###### Art. 115.

§ 1. (Shall be punished (by detention for life) : <L 1996-07-10/42, art.

15, 018; En vigueur : 11-08-1996>

Whoever shall have facilitated the entry of enemies of the State onto the territory of the kingdom; Whoever shall have delivered to them cities, fortresses, stores, arsenals, vessels or buildings belonging to Belgium;

Whoever shall have provided them with assistance in soldiers, men, money, provisions, arms or munitions;

Whoever shall have aided the progress of their arms on the territory of the kingdom or against Belgian land or sea forces, by shaking the loyalty of officers, soldiers, sailors or other citizens towards the King and the State.

In the above cases, the punishable attempt shall be assimilated to the crime itself. Conspiracy having for its purpose one of these crimes shall be punished by (detention of twenty years to thirty years), if it has been followed by an act committed to prepare its execution, and by detention of five years to ten years, in the contrary case.) <L 10-12-1937, art. unique, 2°> <L 1996-07-10/42, art. 3, 018; En vigueur : 11-08-1996> <L 2003-01- 23/42, art. 25, 040; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

###### § 2. (The provision of paragraph 4 of § 1 is applicable to a person residing in

territory occupied by the enemy, only:

1° If, either directly, or through an intermediary or in such capacity, he has provided to enemies of the State assistance in soldiers, men, money, provisions intended for the supply of the enemy, offensive or defensive war material, war munitions properly so-called, spare parts intended for the manufacture of such material or such munitions, clothing or equipment items which he knew to be for military use, or if, for them, he has organized or directed a works enterprise for the establishment, fitting out or camouflage of fortifications, aerodromes or any other constructions or installations for military purposes; raw materials, materials or products which he knew to be intended for the manufacture of such material, such munitions or such items, or for the execution of such works, unless he made such supplies using all means at his disposal to resist the execution of orders from enemies of the State; raw or manufactured materials, products, commodities or animals, where such supply has been consecutive to solicitations or approaches made to them or to intermediaries acting on their behalf or where it has required the creation, transformation or expansion of the enterprise or the modification of its nature or its methods of operation, or where production has been maintained or brought to an abnormal level to satisfy their orders, or where the supplier has had recourse to their assistance to settle social conflicts or has organized counter-sabotage services;

4° If he has placed his activity at their service with a view to assembling, on their behalf, the raw or manufactured materials, products, commodities or animals referred to in 1°, 2° and 3° above.) <AL 25-05-1945, art. 1>

###### Art. 116.

<L 19-07-1934, art. 1> Whoever shall have knowingly delivered or communicated in whole or in part, in original or in reproduction, to an enemy power or to any person acting in the interest of an enemy power, objects, plans, writings, documents or information the secrecy of which vis-à-vis the enemy concerns the defense of the territory or the security of the State, shall be punished (by detention for life). <L 1996-07-10/42, art. 15, 018; En vigueur

###### Art. 117. (The penalties expressed in articles 113, 115 and 116 shall be the same,

whether the crimes provided for by these articles have been committed against Belgium, or whether they have been committed against allies of Belgium acting against the common enemy.) <AL 11-10-1916, art. 1>

(For the application of this provision is an ally of Belgium, any State which, even independently of a treaty of alliance, prosecutes war against a State with which Belgium itself is at war.) <AL 17-12-1942, art. 2>

###### Art. 118. (See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 19-07-1934, art. 1> Whoever shall have knowingly delivered or communicated, in whole or in part, in original or in reproduction, to a foreign power or to any person acting in the interest of a foreign power, objects, plans, writings, documents or information, the secrecy of which concerns the defense of the territory or the external security of the State, shall be punished with (detention of ten to fifteen years.)

If the offender was vested with a public function or mandate or if he fulfilled a mission or performed work entrusted to him by the Government, he shall be punished with (detention of fifteen years to twenty years). <L 2003-01- 23/42, art. 26, 040; In force: 13-03-2003>

Art. 118bis. <AL 17-12-1942, art. 3> Shall be punished (with detention for life), whoever shall have participated in the transformation by the enemy of legal institutions or organizations, undermined in time of war the loyalty of citizens toward the King and the State, or who shall have knowingly served the policy or designs of the enemy. <L 1996-07-10/42, art. 15, 018; In force: 11-08-1996>

Shall likewise be punished (with detention for life), whoever shall have knowingly directed, practiced by any means whatsoever, provoked, aided or favored propaganda directed against resistance to the enemy or its allies or tending to the acts enumerated in the preceding paragraph. <L 1996-07-10/42, art. 15, 018; In force

###### Art. 119.

<L 19-07-1934, art. 1> Whoever shall have knowingly delivered or communicated, in whole or in part, in original or in reproduction, to any person not qualified to take delivery or knowledge thereof, objects, plans, writings, documents or information referred to in article 118, shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of 500 to 5,000 [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.

Shall be punished with the same penalties, whoever, without authorization from the competent authority, shall have reproduced, published or disclosed, in whole or in part, by any means whatsoever, objects, plans, writings, documents or information referred to in article 118.

###### Art. 120.

<L 19-07-1934, art. 1> Whoever, without qualification to take delivery or knowledge thereof, shall have procured, in whole or in part, in original or in reproduction, objects, plans, writings, documents or information referred to in article 118 or shall have received them voluntarily, shall be punished with imprisonment of one month to five years and a fine of 100 to 5,000 [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42,

###### art. 2

, In force: 01-01-2002>.

Art. 120bis. <L 19-07-1934, art. 1> Shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of 500 to 5,000 [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>:

1° Whoever, under a disguise or by concealing his identity, profession, status or nationality, or by means of a maneuver aimed at deceiving the agents assigned to guard or at thwarting their surveillance, shall have entered either into a fort, any defense work, a post, a vessel of the State, or a vessel requisitioned or chartered by it, a military, maritime or aeronautical establishment, a depot, a military magazine or park, or into a workshop, a yard or a laboratory where work concerning the defense of the territory is carried out for the State;

2° Whoever, by one of the means provided for in the preceding paragraph, shall have drawn up a plan, surveyed communication routes, means of correspondence or remote transmission or collected information concerning the defense of the territory or the external security of the State;

3° Whoever, with a view to collecting or transmitting information concerning the defense of the territory or the external security of the State and without having the qualification to do so, shall have organized or used any means whatsoever of correspondence or remote transmission.

Art. 120ter. <L 19-07-1934, art. 1> Shall be punished with imprisonment of eight days to (one year) and a fine of 26 to 100 [euros]: <L 10-12-1937, art. unique, 4°> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

1° Whoever, without authorization from the military, maritime or aeronautical authority, shall have carried out by any means whatsoever surveys or topographical operations within a radius of one myriameter or within any other radius that shall subsequently be fixed by the Minister of National Defense, around a fortified place, a defense work, a post, a military or maritime establishment, an aeronautical establishment other than an aerodrome or airport terminal, a depot, military magazine or park, from the advanced works, or shall have taken photographs of one of these places, works or establishments, published, displayed, sold or distributed reproductions of these views;

2° Whoever, without authorization, shall have scaled or crossed either the facings or slopes of fortifications, or the walls, barriers, railings, palisades, hedges or other enclosures, established on military ground or shall have entered into a fort or one of the other establishments referred to in article 120bis, 1°.

Art. 120quater. <L 19-07-1934, art. 1> The attempt of one of the offenses provided for by articles 116, 119, 120 to 120ter is considered as the offense itself.

Art. 120quinquies. <L 19-07-1934, art. 1> Shall be punished with imprisonment of one month to one year and a fine of 100 to 1,000 [euros], whoever, contrary to the regulations, shall have moved or held objects, plans, writings or documents referred to in article 118, or whoever, through negligence or non-observance of the regulations, shall have allowed to be destroyed, removed or taken away, even momentarily, all or part of these objects, plans, writings or documents which have been entrusted to him or of which he has had knowledge by reason of his functions, status, profession, a mission, a mandate or shall have allowed knowledge, copy or reproduction to be taken by any means whatsoever, in whole or in part. <L 2000-06-26/42,

###### art. 2

, In force: 01-01-2002>

Art. 120sexies. <L 19-07-1934, art. 1> If they have been committed in time of war:

The offenses provided for by articles 118, 119, 120 and 120bis shall be punished with (detention for life); <L 1996-07-10/42, art. 15, 018; In force: 11-08- The offenses provided for by article 120ter shall be punished with (detention of fifteen years to twenty years); <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 27, 040; In force: 13-03-2003> The offenses provided for by article 120quinquies shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of 500 to 5,000 [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.

Art. 120septies. <L 19-07-1934, art. 1> Without prejudice to the application of articles 66 and 67, shall be punished with imprisonment of eight days to six months and a fine of 26 to 500 [euros], whoever, knowing the intentions of the perpetrators of one of the offenses provided for by articles 120 or 120bis or of the attempt of one of these offenses, shall have provided them with lodging, place of retreat or meeting, shall have either received or transmitted their correspondence, or concealed the objects or instruments having served or intended to serve to commit the offense. <L 2000-06- 26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

Art. 120octies. <L 19-03-1956, art. unique> The penalties expressed in articles 118, 119, 120 to 120septies shall be the same whether the offenses provided for by these articles have been committed against Belgium, or whether they have been committed against a State with which Belgium is united by a regional agreement with a view to a common defense.

###### Art. 121.

<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 28, 040; En vigueur : 13-03-2003> Whoever shall have harboured or caused to be harboured spies or enemy soldiers sent on reconnaissance, and whom he shall have known as such, shall be punished with imprisonment for life. Whoever shall have harboured or caused to be harboured enemy agents or soldiers, able-bodied or wounded, or who shall have aided them to enable them to evade the authorities shall be punished with imprisonment of fifteen to twenty years. In a state of siege, the offence shall be punished with imprisonment for life.

Whoever shall have harboured or caused to be harboured a subject of an enemy power or allied to the enemy or who shall have aided him to enable him to evade the authorities, shall be punished with imprisonment of five to ten years. In a state of siege, the offence shall be punished with imprisonment of ten to fifteen years. Whoever shall have harboured or caused to be harboured persons whom he knew to be prosecuted or convicted for one of the offences provided for in Chapter II, of Title 1, of

## Book II

of the Penal Code and in articles 17 and 18 of the law of 27 May 1870 containing the Military Penal Code, or who shall have aided them to enable them to evade the action of justice, shall be punished with the penalty provided for such offence, without however the sentence imposed being able to exceed fifteen years of imprisonment or detention.

Excepted from the provision set out in the preceding paragraph are the ascendants or descendants, spouses, even divorced, brothers or sisters and relatives in the same degrees of the perpetrators or accomplices of the offences in question. Art. 121bis.<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 29, 040; En vigueur : 13-03-2003> Shall be punished with imprisonment of five to ten years, whoever shall have knowingly, by the denunciation of a real or imaginary fact, exposed any person to the investigations, prosecutions or rigours of the enemy.

He shall be punished with imprisonment of ten to fifteen years if the denunciation has resulted, for any person and without the intervention of a new denunciation, in a deprivation of liberty of more than one month.

He shall be punished with imprisonment for life if, following detention or treatment undergone, the denunciation has had as a consequence for any person and without the intervention of a new denunciation, either death, or an apparently incurable disease, or [1incapacity for personal work of more than four months]1, or the loss of the absolute use of an organ, or serious mutilation. (1)<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 20, 114; En vigueur : 29-02-2016>

###### Art. 122.

<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 30, 040; En vigueur : 13-03-2003> When objects have been set on fire or destroyed by any means whatsoever, with the intention of favouring the enemy, the penalties imposed for such acts by

##### Chapter III, of Title IX shall be replaced:

imprisonment, by imprisonment of ten to fifteen years; imprisonment of five to ten years, by imprisonment of fifteen to twenty years; imprisonment of ten to fifteen years, by imprisonment of twenty to thirty years; imprisonment of fifteen years and more, by imprisonment for life; the attempt to set fire or destroy shall be considered as the crime itself.

Art. 122bis. <AL 31-12-1939, art. 1> Without prejudice to the application of more severe provisions, shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of one hundred to five thousand [euros], whoever shall have established or operated a military intelligence service operating on the territory of the kingdom in the interest and to the detriment of foreign powers, whoever shall have carried out any activity in such service, in particular either by recruiting collaborators or agents for it, or by delivering or knowingly communicating to it, in whole or in part, in original or in reproduction, objects, plans, writings, documents or information not manifestly public concerning the military organisation or the defence system of a foreign power, the supply of food, arms or ammunition to its land, sea or air forces or the equipment in use there, or by transmitting the said objects, plans, writings, documents or information to another foreign power or to a person acting in the interest thereof. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2,En vigueur

###### Art. 123. Whoever, by hostile actions not approved by the

government, shall have exposed the State to hostilities on the part of a foreign power, shall be punished with detention of five to ten years, and if hostilities have ensued, with detention of ten to fifteen years.

Art. 123bis. <L 19-07-1934, art. 1> Without prejudice to the application of article 1 of the law of 7 July 1875, of articles 66 and 67 of this Code, shall be punished with imprisonment of eight days to (three years) and a fine of 50 to 1,000 [euros]: <L 10-12-1937, art. unique, 5°> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-
1° The offer or proposal to commit one of the offences provided for by articles 113 to 120bis, 121 to 123;

2° The acceptance of such offer or such proposal.

Art. 123ter. (If the offences provided for by articles 115 to 120quater, 120sexies to 123bis, have been committed out of a desire for profit, (the sums, goods or any direct or indirect advantages which constitute the profit resulting from the activity of the guilty party, or, when they have not been seized, the amount of their value), shall be declared forfeited to the Treasury.) <L 19-07-1934, art. 1> <L 07-06-1948, art. 1> (In the same case, the penalties of imprisonment provided for by articles 119 and 120 shall be replaced by imprisonment of five to ten years and temporary detention by temporary imprisonment of the same duration.) <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 31, 040; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

(If there are mitigating circumstances, (imprisonment for life) shall be replaced in accordance with article 80.) <L 19-07-1934, art. 1> <L 1996-07-10/42,

###### art. 15

, 018; In force: 11-08-1996>

Art. 123quater. (See NOTE under TITLE) <L 19-07-1934, art. 1> Without prejudice to the application of more severe provisions, shall be punished by the penalties provided for in article 123bis, the conspiracy to commit a crime or an offence against persons or property, formed with the purpose of hindering, in time of war, either the defence of the territory, or the mobilization, or the supply of food, arms or ammunition to the army.

If the conspiracy is formed in time of war, it shall be punished by (imprisonment from five years to ten years). <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 31, 040; In force: 13-03-2003>

Art. 123quinquies.<L 19-07-1934, art. 1> The confiscation of things which have been used or which have been intended to commit the offence shall always be pronounced, as well as the confiscation of plans, maps, writings, documents, copies, surveys, photographs, views, reproductions and all other things obtained by the offence.

(In the cases provided for in articles 119, 120, 120bis, 121bis, 122bis and 123quater, the guilty parties sentenced to imprisonment may be sentenced to perpetual or temporary prohibition of the rights listed in [1 article 31, paragraph 1]1.) <AL 31-12-1939, art. 2>

(1)<L 2009-04-14/01, art. 8, 073; In force: 15-04-2009>

Art. 123sexies. <L 30-06-1961, art. 1> § 1. (By derogation from articles 31 and 32, the judgments or rulings of conviction to life imprisonment or life detention, to imprisonment from ten years to fifteen years or a higher term or detention from twenty years to thirty years or from fifteen years to twenty years for offence or attempted offence provided for in Chapter II, of Title 1, of Book II of the Criminal Code, committed in time of war, shall not pronounce against the convicted persons the prohibition of the rights referred to therein, but shall entail by operation of law perpetual disqualification:) <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 33, 040; In force: 13- 1° from the rights listed in the said article 31 including the rights to vote, to elect, to stand for election;

2° from the right to be registered on one of the rolls of the bar association, on a list of honorary lawyers or on a list of trainee lawyers;

3° from the right to participate in any capacity whatsoever in teaching given in a public or private establishment;

4° from the right to be remunerated by the State as a minister of a religion;

5° from the right to be a leader of a political association;

6° from the right to participate in any capacity whatsoever in the operation, administration, editing, printing or distribution of a newspaper or any publication in cases where such participation has a political character; 7° from the right to participate in the management or administration of any cultural, philanthropic and sports event or any public entertainment in cases where such participation has a political character;

8° from the right to participate in the operation, administration or in any manner in the activity of any enterprise whose object is theatrical performances, cinematography or broadcasting in cases where such participation has a political character;

9° from the right to participate in any capacity in the administration, management or direction of a professional association or a non-profit association.

###### § 2. By derogation from articles 32 and 33, the judgments or rulings of

conviction to other criminal penalties or to correctional penalties for offence or attempted offence provided for in chapter II of title I of book II of the Criminal Code, committed in time of war, may pronounce not the prohibition of rights provided for in the said articles but the temporary disqualification from the rights listed in the preceding paragraph.

The disqualifications set out in the electoral laws, including (articles 6 and 7 of the Electoral Code), are in any case applicable. <L 05-07-1976, art. 145> (The disqualifications may be pronounced for a period of ten years to twenty years if the penalty is imprisonment from five years to ten years or detention from five years to ten years or from ten years to fifteen years and for a period of five years to ten years if the penalty is correctional). The duration of the disqualifications fixed by the judgment or ruling of conviction shall run from the day on which the adversarial or default conviction has acquired the force of res judicata. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 33, 040; In force: 13- Art. 123septies. (See NOTE under TITLE) <L 30-06-1961, art. 1> § 1. Convicted persons subject to disqualification by application of article 123sexies may request restitution of the rights listed under 6° to 9° on condition:

1° that they are not detained in execution of the sentence, nor fugitives or absconders; 2° that they have discharged the pecuniary penalties pronounced against them and have discharged the restitutions, damages and costs to which they have been sentenced; however, the court may exempt from this condition the convicted person who proves to have been unable to discharge them, either by reason of his indigence, or by reason of any other cause not attributable to him; 3° that, since the day on which the disqualification took effect, a period of twenty years has elapsed if the convicted person has been subject to perpetual disqualification, ten years if he has been subject to disqualification from ten to twenty years following a conviction to (imprisonment from five years to ten years or to detention from five years to ten years or from ten years to fifteen years), and five years if he has been subject to a disqualification from five to ten years following a conviction to a correctional penalty. <L 2003-01-23/42,

###### art. 34

, 040; In force: 13-03-2003>

###### § 2. The application is sent by registered letter to the King's prosecutor of the

domicile or residence of the interested party and, if the latter has in Belgium neither domicile nor certain residence, to that of the district of Brussels.

The King's prosecutor obtains all information he deems necessary and brings the application before the Court of First Instance.

The interested party appears before the court sitting in chambers either in person, or through a solicitor or through a lawyer bearing the documents, upon simple summons sent to him, by registered mail, by the King's prosecutor.

This summons contains the indication of the Chamber of the court before which the application will be brought and the day and hour of the appearance. There shall be at least a period of eight days between the notification and the day of appearance. The handing over of the registered mail to the post office constitutes notification.

If, upon notification, the interested party does not appear either in person, or through a solicitor, or through a lawyer bearing the documents, the court may, before ruling on the application, adjourn the case in order to allow the public prosecutor's office to send him a new summons.

The file of the public prosecutor's office is deposited at the registry of the court at least eight days before the scheduled hearing. The proceedings continue at the hearing as in correctional matters.

The judgment rendered on the application is without appeal.

If the application is rejected in whole or in part, it cannot be renewed before the expiry of two years from the date of the judicial decision.

In case of death of the interested party, the remedies and applications provided for in this law may be pursued by his spouse, his descendants, his ascendants or his brothers and sisters.

They may also be pursued by one or more successors in title on a universal or particular basis who will prove a pecuniary interest.


This excerpt sits under: CODE PENAL > Livre II > Chapitre III, du Titre IX seront remplacées :

###### § 3. The restitution of rights of which the convicted persons had been deprived by

application of the preceding article, has effect only for the future.

Art. 123octies. <L 30-06-1961, art. 1> When the judgment or ruling resulting in deprivation for life or for a time of rights by application of article 123sexies, has become final, the Public Prosecutor's Office has it published by extract in the "Moniteur belge" with mention of the deprivation pronounced or resulting from it. In addition, it notifies it by extract to the civil status officer of the last domicile for the purpose of recording this same mention in the population register. This mention is reproduced in the population register of any new domicile.

Art. 123nonies. <L 30-06-1961, art. 1> Whoever, despite the deprivation resulting from the application of §§ 1 or 2 of article 123sexies, makes use, either directly or through the interposition of a person, of one of the rights enumerated in that article, shall be punished by imprisonment of one year to three years and a fine of ten thousand to one hundred thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

Art. 123decies. <AL 20-09-1945, art. 2> <L 01-06-1949, art. 1, 4°> Companies are civilly liable for convictions to damages, fines, costs, confiscations, restitutions and any pecuniary sanctions pronounced for offences against the provisions of this chapter against their organs or employees.

The same applies to members of all commercial associations without legal personality, when the offence was committed by a partner, manager or employee, in connection with a transaction falling within the scope of the association's activity. The civilly liable partner is however personally liable only up to the amounts or values he has obtained from the transaction.

These companies and partners may be summoned directly before the criminal court by the public prosecutor or the civil party.

##### CHAPITRE III. - CRIMES AGAINST THE INTERNAL SECURITY OF THE STATE.

###### Art. 124. (Voir NOTE sous TITRE) The attempt the purpose of which is to incite civil

war, by arming or inciting citizens or inhabitants to arm themselves against each other, shall be punished by (detention of fifteen years to twenty years). <L 2003-01- 23/42, art. 35, 040; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

The conspiracy formed for the same purpose shall be punished by ten years to fifteen years of detention, if any act has been committed to prepare its execution; and by five years to ten years of the same penalty, in the contrary case.

###### Art. 125. (Voir NOTE sous TITRE)

<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 36, 040; En vigueur : 13- 03-2003> The attempt the purpose of which is to bring devastation, massacre or pillage in one or more communes, shall be punished by fifteen years to twenty years of imprisonment.

The conspiracy formed for the same purpose shall be punished by ten years to fifteen years of the same penalty, if any act has been committed to prepare its execution; and by five years to ten years of the same penalty, in the contrary case.

###### Art. 126. (Voir NOTE sous TITRE) Shall be punished by detention of five years to ten

years, those who have raised or caused to be raised armed troops, engaged or enlisted, caused to be engaged or enlisted soldiers, or have supplied or procured for them either arms or ammunition, without order or authorization of the government.

###### Art. 127. (Voir NOTE sous TITRE) Shall be punished by detention of five years to ten

years:

Those who, without right or legitimate reason, have taken command of a corps of army, a troop, a warship, a fortified place, a post, a port, a city;

Those who have retained, against the order of the government, any military command;

The commanders who have kept their army or troop assembled, after the disbanding or separation has been ordered.

###### Art. 128. (Voir NOTE sous TITRE) Whoever, either to seize public funds, or to invade

domains, properties, places, cities, fortresses, posts, warehouses, arsenals, ports, vessels or buildings belonging to the State, or to make attack or resistance against the public force acting against the perpetrators of these crimes, has placed himself at the head of armed bands, or has exercised any function or command therein, shall be punished by (detention of fifteen years to twenty years). <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 37, 040; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 129. (Voir NOTE sous TITRE) If these bands had for their purpose, either to pillage

or to divide public or national properties, or those of a generality of citizens, or to make attack or resistance against the public force acting against the perpetrators of these crimes, those who have placed themselves at the head of these bands, or who have exercised any function or command therein, shall be punished (by imprisonment) of fifteen years to twenty years. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 38, 040; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 130. The penalties respectively established in the two preceding articles

shall be applicable to those who have directed the association, raised or caused to be raised, organized or caused to be organized the bands.

###### Art. 131. In the case where one of the crimes mentioned in articles 101, 102, 103 and

104 has been committed by a band, the penalties provided for by these articles shall be applied, without distinction of rank, to all individuals forming part of the band and who have been seized at the place of the seditious assembly. Shall be punished with the same penalties, although not seized at the place, whoever has directed the sedition or exercised in the band any employment or command whatsoever.

###### Art. 132. Except in the case where the seditious assembly has had for its object or for result

one of the crimes set out in articles 101, 102, 103 and 104, the individuals forming part of the bands referred to above, without exercising any command or employment therein, and who have been seized at the places, shall be punished by the penalty immediately inferior to that which shall be pronounced against the directors or commanders of these bands.

###### Art. 133. (Voir NOTE sous TITRE) Those who, knowing the purpose or character of

said bands, have provided to these bands or to their divisions, lodgings, retreats or meeting places, shall be punished, in the cases of articles 101, 102, 103 and 128, by (imprisonment of five years to ten years), and, in the cases provided for by articles 104 and 127, by detention of five years to ten years. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 39, 040; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 134. No penalty shall be pronounced, for the fact of sedition, against those

who, having been part of these bands without exercising any command and without fulfilling any employment or function therein, withdrew at the first warning of the civil or military authorities, or even since, when they were seized outside the places of the seditious assembly, without opposing resistance and without weapons. Nevertheless, they shall be punished by reason of other crimes or offences which they have personally committed.

###### Art. 135. Included in the word "weapons" are all machines, all instruments,

utensils or other sharp, piercing or blunt objects, which one has seized to kill, injure or strike, even if one has not used them.

Art. 135bis.<L 20-07-1939, art. unique> Any person who, directly or indirectly, receives from a foreign person or organization and in any form whatsoever, donations, gifts, loans or other benefits, intended or used in whole or in part to conduct or remunerate in Belgium an activity or propaganda of a nature to undermine the integrity, sovereignty or independence of the kingdom, or to shake the loyalty that citizens owe to the State and to the institutions of the Belgian people, is punished by imprisonment from six months to five years, and by a fine of one thousand [euros] to twenty thousand [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>.

In all cases of infringement, the things received are confiscated [2 ...]2. Deprivation of the exercise of the rights enumerated in [1 l'article 31, alinéa 1er]1 or of certain of those rights may be pronounced for a term of five to ten years. (1)<L 2009-04-14/01, art. 9, 073; En vigueur : 15-04-2009>

(2)<L 2014-05-05/09, art. 10, 106; En vigueur : 18-07-2014>

Art. 135ter. (Abroge) <L 01-08-1979, art. 7>

Art. 135quater. <L 23-06-1961, art. unique> Any person who obtains an undertaking to serve in a foreign army or troop, from a minor not authorized to do so by his parents, his guardian or his curator, is punished by imprisonment (from three months to two years). <L 01-08-1979, art. 6>

Art. 135quinquies.<L 23-06-1961, art. unique> The attempt to commit the offences provided for [1 à l'article 135quater]1 shall be punished by the same penalties.

(1)<L 2014-05-05/09, art. 11, 106; En vigueur : 18-07-2014>

COMMON PROVISION TO THIS TITLE.

###### Art. 136. Shall be exempted from the penalties imposed for conspiracies punished by

this title, and for the offences provided for in article 111, those of the offenders who, before any attack and before any proceedings commenced, have given the authority knowledge of those conspiracies or those offences, and of their perpetrators or accomplices.

TITLE Ibis. - Serious violations of international humanitarian law. <inséré par L 2003-08-05/32, art. 6; En vigueur : 07-08-2003>

Art. 136bis. <inséré par L 2003-08-05/32, art. 6; En vigueur : 07-08-2003> Constitutes a crime under international law and is punished in accordance with the provisions of this title, the crime of genocide, as defined below, whether committed in time of peace or in time of war. In accordance with the Convention on the Prevention and Punishment of the Crime of Genocide of 9 December 1948, and without prejudice to the criminal provisions applicable to offences committed by negligence, the crime of genocide means any of the following acts committed with intent to destroy, in whole or in part, a national, ethnical, racial or religious group as such:

1° killing members of the group;

2° causing serious bodily or mental harm to members of the group;

3° deliberately inflicting on the group conditions of life calculated to bring about its physical destruction in whole or in part;

4° imposing measures intended to prevent births within the group;

5° forcibly transferring children of the group to another group.

Art. 136ter. <inséré par L 2003-08-05/32, art. 7; En vigueur : 07-08-2003> Constitutes a crime under international law and is punished in accordance with the provisions of this title, the crime against humanity, as defined below, whether committed in time of peace or in time of war. In accordance with the Statute of the International Criminal Court, the crime against humanity means any of the following acts when committed as part of a widespread or systematic attack directed against any civilian population, with knowledge of the attack:

1° murder;

2° extermination;

3° enslavement;

4° deportation or forcible transfer of population;

5° imprisonment or other severe deprivation of physical liberty in violation of fundamental rules of international law;

6° torture;

7° rape, sexual slavery, enforced prostitution, forced pregnancy, enforced sterilization, and any other form of sexual violence of comparable gravity;

8° persecution against any identifiable group or collectivity on political, racial, national, ethnic, cultural, religious, gender or other grounds that are universally recognized as impermissible under international law, in connection with any act referred to in articles 136bis, 136ter and 136quater;

9° enforced disappearance of persons;

10° the crime of apartheid;

11° other inhumane acts of a similar character intentionally causing great suffering, or serious injury to body or to mental or physical health.

Art. 136quater. <inséré par L 2003-08-05/32, art. 8; En vigueur : 07-08-2003> (NOTE : le troisième paragraphe de l'article 136quater entre en vigueur le jour de l'entrée en vigueur pour la Belgique du Deuxième Protocole relatif à la Convention de La Haye de 1954 pour la protection des biens culturels en cas de conflit armé, adopté à La Haye le 26 mars 1999; voir L 2003-08-05/32, art. 29, §2)

###### § 1

er. Constitute crimes under international law and are punishable in accordance with the provisions of this title, the war crimes referred to in the Conventions adopted at Geneva on 12 August 1949 and in Protocols I and II additional to those Conventions, adopted at Geneva on 8 June 1977, under the laws and customs applicable to armed conflicts, as defined in Article 2 of the Conventions adopted at Geneva on 12 August 1949, in Article 1 of Protocols I and II adopted at Geneva on 8 June 1977 additional to those Conventions, as well as in Article 8, § 2, f) of the Statute of the International Criminal Court, and enumerated below, when such crimes violate, by action or omission, the protection of persons and property guaranteed respectively by those Conventions, Protocols, laws and customs, without prejudice to the criminal provisions applicable to offences committed through negligence:

1° wilful killing;

2° torture or other inhuman treatment, including biological experiments;

3° wilfully causing great suffering or serious injury to body or health;

4° rape, sexual slavery, enforced prostitution, forced pregnancy, enforced sterilization or any other form of sexual violence constituting a grave breach of the Geneva Conventions or a serious violation of common Article 3 of those Conventions;

5° other outrages upon human dignity, in particular humiliating and degrading treatment;

6° compelling a prisoner of war, a civilian person protected by the Convention on the protection of civilian persons in time of war or a person protected in that same respect by Protocols I and II additional to the Geneva Conventions of 12 August 1949, to serve in the armed forces or armed groups of the hostile power or of the adverse party;

7° conscripting or enlisting children under the age of 15 years into armed forces or armed groups or using them to participate actively in hostilities;

8° depriving a prisoner of war, a civilian person protected by the Convention on the protection of civilian persons in time of war or a person protected in that same respect, by Protocols I and II additional to the Geneva Conventions of 12 August 1949, of the right to a fair and regular trial according to the requirements of those instruments;

9° the deportation, unlawful transfer or displacement, unlawful confinement of a civilian person protected by the Convention on the protection of civilian persons in time of war or a person protected in those same respects by Protocols I and II additional to the Geneva Conventions of 12 August 1949; 10° intentionally using starvation of civilians as a method of warfare, by depriving them of objects indispensable to their survival, including by wilfully impeding relief supplies as provided for under the Geneva Conventions; 11° taking of hostages;

12° destroying or seizing the property of an enemy, in case of international armed conflict, or of an adversary, in case of armed conflict not of an international character, unless such destruction or seizure be imperatively demanded by the necessities of war;

13° extensive destruction and appropriation of property, not justified by military necessity of the kind admitted under the law of nations and carried out on a large scale unlawfully and wantonly;

14° intentionally launching attacks against civilian objects, that is, objects which are not military objectives;

15° intentionally launching attacks against buildings, material, units and means of transport, and personnel using, in conformity with international law, the distinctive emblems provided for by international humanitarian law;

16° utilizing the presence of a civilian or other person protected by international humanitarian law to render certain points, areas or military forces immune from military operations;

17° intentionally launching attacks against personnel, installations, material, units or vehicles involved in a humanitarian assistance or peacekeeping mission in accordance with the Charter of the United Nations, as long as they are entitled to the protection given to civilians and civilian objects under the international law of armed conflict;

18° acts and omissions, not legally justified, which are liable to endanger the health and physical or mental integrity of persons protected by international humanitarian law, in particular any medical act which would not be justified by the state of health of such persons or would not be consistent with generally recognized rules of medical practice;

19° except where justified under the conditions provided for in 18°, acts consisting of carrying out on the persons referred to in 18°, even with their consent, physical mutilations, medical or scientific experiments or removal of tissue or organs for transplantation, unless these are blood donations for transfusions or skin donations for grafts, provided that such donations are voluntary, consensual and intended for therapeutic purposes;

20° intentionally directing an attack against the civilian population or against civilians not taking direct part in hostilities;

21° intentionally launching an attack against places where the sick and wounded are collected, provided that such places are not military objectives;

22° intentionally launching an attack in the knowledge that such attack will cause loss of life, injury to civilians or damage to civilian objects or widespread, long-term and severe damage to the natural environment which would be excessive in relation to the concrete and direct military advantage anticipated, without prejudice to the criminality of the attack whose damaging effects, even if proportionate to the expected military advantage, would be incompatible with the principles of the law of nations, as they result from established usage, the principles of humanity and the dictates of public conscience;

23° launching an attack against works or installations containing dangerous forces, in the knowledge that such attack will cause loss of life, injury to civilians or damage to civilian objects, which would be excessive in relation to the concrete and direct military advantage anticipated, without prejudice to the criminality of the attack whose damaging effects even if proportionate to the expected military advantage would be incompatible with the principles of the law of nations, as they result from established usage, the principles of humanity and the dictates of public conscience; 24° attacking or bombarding, by whatever means, demilitarized zones or towns, villages, dwellings or buildings which are undefended and which are not military objectives;

25° pillaging a town or place, even when taken by assault;

26° directing an attack against a person in the knowledge that the person is hors de combat, provided that such attack results in death or injury; 27° killing or wounding treacherously individuals belonging to the hostile nation or army or an adverse combatant;

28° declaring that no quarter will be given;

29° making improper use of the distinctive emblem of the red cross or the red crescent or other protective signs recognized by international humanitarian law, provided that this act results in death or serious injury; 30° making improper use of a flag of truce, the flag or the military insignia and uniform of the enemy or of the United Nations, provided that this act results in loss of life or serious injury;

31° the transfer, directly or indirectly, into occupied territory of parts of the civilian population of the occupying power, in the case of an international armed conflict, or of the occupying authority in the case of a non-international armed conflict;

32° unjustifiably delaying the repatriation of prisoners of war or civilians;

33° practising apartheid or other inhuman or degrading practices based on racial discrimination and giving rise to outrages upon personal dignity;

34° directing attacks against historic monuments, works of art or places of worship which are clearly recognized and which constitute the cultural or spiritual heritage of peoples and to which special protection has been given by virtue of a special arrangement, where there is no evidence of violation by the adverse party of the prohibition on using such property in support of the military effort, and such property is not located in the immediate proximity of military objectives;

35° intentionally launching attacks against buildings dedicated to religion, education, art, science or charitable purposes, historic monuments, hospitals, provided that such buildings are not military objectives;

36° employing poison or poisoned weapons;

37° employing asphyxiating, poisonous or other gases, and all analogous liquids, materials or devices;

38° employing bullets which expand or flatten easily in the human body, such as bullets with a hard envelope which does not entirely cover the core or is pierced with incisions;

39° declaring abolished, suspended or inadmissible in a court of law the rights and actions of persons belonging to the adverse party;

40° employing weapons, projectiles, material and methods of warfare of a nature to cause superfluous injury or unnecessary suffering or to strike indiscriminately in violation of the international law of armed conflict, on condition that such weapons, projectiles, material and methods of warfare are the subject of a comprehensive prohibition and are included in an annex to the Statute of the International Criminal Court.

###### § 2. Constitute crimes under international law and are punished

in accordance with the provisions of this title, serious violations of common Article 3 of the Conventions signed in Geneva on August 12, 1949, in the event of an armed conflict as defined by that common Article 3, and listed hereinafter, when such violations undermine, by action or omission, the protection of persons guaranteed by these Conventions, without prejudice to the criminal provisions applicable to offenses committed through negligence:

1° attacks on life and physical integrity, in particular murder in all its forms, mutilation, cruel treatment and torture; 2° attacks on the dignity of the person, in particular humiliating and degrading treatment;

3° taking of hostages;

4° sentences pronounced and executions carried out without a prior judgment, rendered by a regularly constituted court, accompanied by judicial guarantees generally recognized as indispensable.

###### § 3. Constitute crimes under international law and are punished

in accordance with the provisions of this title, serious violations defined in Article 15 of the Second Protocol to the Hague Convention of 1954 for the Protection of Cultural Property in the Event of Armed Conflict, adopted in The Hague on March 26, 1999, committed in the event of armed conflict, as defined in Article 18, §§ 1 and 2, of the Hague Convention of 1954 and in Article 22 of the aforementioned Second Protocol, and listed hereinafter, when such offenses undermine, by action or omission, the protection of property guaranteed by these Convention and Protocol, without prejudice to the criminal provisions applicable to offenses committed through negligence:

1° making cultural property under enhanced protection the object of an attack; 2° using cultural property under enhanced protection or its immediate surroundings in support of military action;

3° destroying or appropriating on a large scale cultural property protected by the Convention and the Second Protocol.

Art. 136Quinquies.<inséré par L 2003-08-05/32, art. 9, En vigueur : 07-08-2003> (NOTE : le dernier alinéa de l'article 136quinquies entre en vigueur le jour de l'entrée en vigueur pour la Belgique du Deuxième Protocole relatif à la Convention de La Haye de 1954 pour la protection des biens culturels en cas de conflit armé, adopté à La Haye le 26 mars 1999; voir L 2003-08-05/32, art. 29, §2) The offenses listed in Articles 136bis and 136ter are punished by life imprisonment.

The offenses listed in 1°, 2°, 15°, 17°, 20° to 24° and 26° to 28° of

###### paragraph 1 of Article 136quater are punished by life imprisonment.

The offenses listed in 3°, 4°, 10°, 16°, 19°, 36° to 38° and 40° of the same paragraph of the same article are punished by imprisonment of twenty to thirty years. They are punished by life imprisonment if they have resulted in the death of one or more persons.

The offenses listed in 12° to 14° and 25° of the same paragraph of the same article are punished by imprisonment of fifteen to twenty years. The same offense as well as those referred to in 29° and 30° of the same paragraph of the same article are punished by imprisonment of twenty to thirty years if they have resulted in either an apparently incurable disease, or [1 incapacity for personal work of more than four months]1, or the loss of the absolute use of an organ or serious mutilation. They are punished by life imprisonment if they have resulted in the death of one or more persons.

The offenses listed in 6° to 9°, 11° and 31° of the same paragraph of the same article are punished by imprisonment of ten to fifteen years. In cases of aggravating circumstances provided for in the preceding paragraph, they are punished, as the case may be, by the penalties provided for in that paragraph.

The offenses listed in 5° and 32° to 35° of the same paragraph of the same article are punished by imprisonment of ten to fifteen years, subject to the application of more severe criminal provisions punishing serious attacks on the dignity of the person.

The offense provided for in 18° of the same paragraph of the same article is punished by imprisonment of ten to fifteen years. It is punished by imprisonment of fifteen to twenty years when it has resulted in serious consequences for public health. The offense listed in 39° of the same paragraph of the same article is punished by imprisonment of ten to fifteen years.

The offense listed in 1° of paragraph 2 of Article 136quater is punished by life imprisonment.

The offenses listed in 2° and 4° of the same paragraph of the same article are punished by imprisonment of ten to fifteen years, subject to the application of more severe criminal provisions punishing serious attacks on the dignity of the person.

The offense listed in 3° of the same paragraph of the same article is punished by imprisonment of ten to fifteen years. The same offense is punished by imprisonment of twenty to thirty years if it has resulted in either an apparently incurable disease, or [1incapacity for personal work of more than four months]1, or the loss of the absolute use of an organ, or serious mutilation. It is punished by life imprisonment if it has resulted in the death of one or more persons.

The offenses listed in 1° to 3° of paragraph 3 of Article 136quater are punished by imprisonment of fifteen to twenty years.

(1)<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 20, 114; En vigueur : 29-02-2016>

Art. 136sexies. <inséré par L 2003-08-05/32, art. 10; En vigueur : 07-08-2003> Those who manufacture, possess or transport an instrument, device or any object, erect a construction or transform an existing construction, knowing that the instrument, device, object, construction or transformation is intended to commit one of the offenses provided for in Articles 136bis, 136ter and 136quater or to facilitate its perpetration, are punished by the penalty provided for the offense whose perpetration they have enabled or facilitated. Art. 136septies. <inséré par L 2003-08-05/32, art. 11; En vigueur : 07-08-2003> Are punished by the penalty provided for the completed offense:

1° the order, even if not followed by effect, to commit one of the offenses provided for by Articles 136bis, 136ter and 136quater;

2° the proposal or offer to commit such an offense and the acceptance of such a proposal or offer;

3° the incitement to commit such an offense, even if not followed by effect; 4° participation, within the meaning of Articles 66 and 67, in such an offense, even if not followed by effect;

5° the failure to act within the limits of their ability to act on the part of those who had knowledge of orders given for the execution of such an offense or of facts that begin its execution, and could have prevented its completion or put an end to it;

6° the attempt, within the meaning of Articles 51 to 53, to commit such an offense. Art. 136octies. <inséré par L 2003-08-05/32, art. 12; En vigueur : 07-08-2003> § 1er. Without prejudice to the exceptions set out in points 18°, 22° and 23° of Article 136quater, § 1er, no interest, no necessity of a political, military or national nature can justify the offenses defined in Articles 136bis, 136ter, 136quater, 136sexies and 136septies, even if they are committed as reprisals.

###### § 2. The fact that the accused acted on the order of his government or of a superior

does not exempt him from his responsibility if, in the circumstances given, the order could clearly result in the commission of one of the offenses referred to in Articles 136bis, 136ter and 136quater.

TITRE Iter. - DES INFRACTIONS TERRORISTES. <Inséré par L 2003-12-19/34, art. 2; En vigueur : 08-01-2004>


This excerpt sits under: CODE PENAL > Livre II > CHAPITRE III. - DES CRIMES CONTRE LA SURETE INTERIEURE DE L'ETAT.

###### Art. 137.

<Inserted by L 2003-12-19/34, art. 3, 046; In force: 08-01-2004> § 1. Constitutes a terrorist offence, the offence provided for in §§ 2 and 3 which, by its nature or context, may seriously harm a country or an international organisation and is intentionally committed with the aim of seriously intimidating a population or unduly compelling public authorities or an international organisation to perform or abstain from performing an act, or seriously destabilising or destroying the fundamental political, constitutional, economic or social structures of a country or an international organisation.

###### § 2. Constitutes, under the conditions provided for in § 1, a terrorist offence:

1° intentional homicide or intentional assault and battery referred to in articles 393 to 404, 405bis, 405ter insofar as it refers to the aforementioned articles, 409, § 1, paragraph 1, and §§ 2 to 5, 410 insofar as it refers to the aforementioned articles, 417ter and 417quater;

2° hostage-taking referred to in article 347bis;

3° abduction referred to in articles 428 to 430, and 434 to 437;

4° massive destruction or damage referred to in articles 521, paragraphs 1 and 3, 522, 523, 525, 526, 550bis, § 3, 3°, in article 15 of the law of 5 June 1928 revising the Disciplinary and Penal Code for the merchant navy and maritime fishing, as well as in article 114, § 4, of the law of 21 March 1991 reforming certain public economic enterprises, having the effect of endangering human lives or producing considerable economic losses;

5° the seizure of aircraft referred to in article 30, § 1, 2°, of the law of 27 June 1937 revising the law of 16 November 1919 on the regulation of air navigation;

6° the act of seizing by fraud, violence or threats against the captain of a ship, referred to in article 33 of the law of 5 June 1928 revising the Disciplinary and Penal Code for the merchant navy and maritime fishing [1 as well as acts of piracy referred to in article 3 of the law of 30 December 2009 on combating maritime piracy]1;

7° offences covered by the royal decree of 23 September 1958 establishing general regulations on the manufacture, storage, possession, sale, transport and use of explosive products, amended by the royal decree of 1 February 2000, and punished by articles 5 to 7 of the law of 28 May 1956 on explosive or deflagrable substances and mixtures and on devices loaded with them;

8° offences referred to in articles 510 to 513, 516 to 518, 520, 547 to 549, as well as in article 14 of the law of 5 June 1928 revising the Disciplinary and Penal Code for the merchant navy and maritime fishing, having the effect of endangering human lives;

9° offences covered by the law of [2 8 June 2006 regulating economic and individual activities with weapons]2;

10° offences referred to in article 2, paragraph one, 2°, of the law of 10 July 1978 approving the Convention on the Prohibition of the Development, Production and Stockpiling of Bacteriological (Biological) and Toxin Weapons and on their Destruction, done in London, Moscow and Washington on 10 April [2 11° attempt, within the meaning of articles 51 to 53, to commit the offences referred to in this paragraph.]2

###### § 3. Also constitutes, under the conditions provided for in § 1, a terrorist

offence:

1° massive destruction or damage, or the causing of a flood of an infrastructure, a transport system, public or private property, having the effect of endangering human lives or producing considerable economic losses, other than those referred to in § 2;

2° the seizure of means of transport other than those referred to in 5° and 6° of § 2;
3° the manufacture, possession, acquisition, transport or supply of nuclear or chemical weapons, the use of nuclear, biological or chemical weapons, as well as research and development of chemical weapons;

4° the release of dangerous substances having the effect of endangering human lives;

5° disruption or interruption of the supply of water, electricity or any other fundamental natural resource having the effect of endangering human lives;

6° the threat to carry out one of the offences listed in § 2 or in this paragraph.

(1)<L 2009-12-30/12, art. 7, 076; In force: 14-01-2010>

(2)<L 2013-02-18/01, art. 2, 091; In force: 14-03-2013>

###### Art. 138.

<Inserted by L 2003-12-19/34, art. 4, 046; In force: 08-01-2004> § 1. The penalties provided for the offences listed in article 137, § 2, are replaced as follows, if these offences constitute terrorist offences:

1° fine, by imprisonment of one year to three years;

2° imprisonment of six months at most, by imprisonment of three years at most;

3° imprisonment of one year at most, by imprisonment of three years at most;

4° imprisonment of three years at most, by imprisonment of five years at most;

5° imprisonment of five years at most, by criminal imprisonment of five years to ten years;

6° criminal imprisonment of five years to ten years, by criminal imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years;
7° criminal imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years, by criminal imprisonment of fifteen years to twenty years;
8° criminal imprisonment of ten years to twenty years by criminal imprisonment of fifteen years to twenty years;
9° criminal imprisonment of fifteen years to twenty years, by criminal imprisonment of twenty years to thirty years;

10° criminal imprisonment of twenty years to thirty years, by life imprisonment.
[1 In the cases referred to in article 137, § 2, 11°, the maximum penalty provided for the completed offence shall be reduced by one year.]1

###### § 2. The terrorist offences referred to in article 137, § 3, shall be punished by:

1° in the case referred to in 6°, imprisonment of three months to five years where the threat relates to an offence punishable by a correctional penalty, and criminal imprisonment of five years to ten years where the threat relates to an offence punishable by a criminal penalty;

2° criminal imprisonment of fifteen years to twenty years in the cases referred to in 1°, 2° and 5°;
3° life imprisonment in the cases referred to in 3° and 4°.

(1)<L 2013-02-18/01, art. 3, 091; In force: 14-03-2013>

###### Art. 139.

<Inserted by L 2003-12-19/34, art. 5, 046; In force: 08-01-2004> Constitutes a terrorist group the structured association of more than two persons, established over time, and which acts in a concerted manner with a view to committing terrorist offences referred to in article 137.

An organisation whose real purpose is exclusively political, trade union, philanthropic, philosophical or religious in nature or which exclusively pursues any other legitimate aim cannot, as such, be considered a terrorist group within the meaning of paragraph 1.

###### Art. 140.

<Inserted by L 2003-12-19/34, art. 6, 046; In force: 08-01-2004> § 1. Any person who participates in an activity of a terrorist group, including by providing information or material means to the terrorist group, or by any form of financing of an activity of the terrorist group, [1 having had or having had to have knowledge]1 that this participation [1 could contribute]1 to committing a crime or an offence of the terrorist group, shall be punished by criminal imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of one hundred euros to five thousand euros.

###### § 2. Any leader of the terrorist group is liable to imprisonment from fifteen years to twenty years and a fine from one thousand euros to two hundred thousand euros.

(1)<L 2016-12-14/09, art. 2, 120; En vigueur : 01-01-2017>

Art. 140bis.[1 Without prejudice to the application of Article 140, any person who disseminates or otherwise makes available to the public a message, with the intention of inciting [2 directly or indirectly]2 the commission of one of the offences referred to [2 in Articles 137 or 140sexies]2, with the exception of that referred to in Article 137, § 3, 6°, shall be punished by imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine from one hundred euros to five thousand euros [ 2 ...] 2 .]1
