(2)<L 2016-08-03/15, art. 2, 119; In force: 21-08-2016> (NOTE: by its judgment annulled article 2, 3°)

Art. 140ter.[1 Without prejudice to the application of Article 140, any person who recruits another person to commit one of the offences referred to [2 in Articles 137, 140 or 140sexies]2, with the exception of that referred to in Article 137, § 3, 6°, shall be punished by imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine from one hundred euros to five thousand euros.]1

(1)<Inserted by L 2013-02-18/01, art. 5, 091; In force: 14-03-2013>

(2)<L 2016-08-03/15, art. 3, 119; In force: 21-08-2016>

Art. 140quater. [1 Without prejudice to the application of Article 140, any person who gives instructions or training for the manufacture or use of explosives, firearms or other weapons or harmful or dangerous substances, or for other specific methods and techniques with a view to committing one of the offences referred to in Article 137, with the exception of that referred to in Article 137, § 3, 6°, shall be punished by imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine from one hundred euros to five thousand euros.]1

(1)<Inserted by L 2013-02-18/01, art. 6, 091; In force: 14-03-2013>

Art. 140quinquies. [1 Without prejudice to the application of Article 140, any person who, in Belgium or abroad, receives instructions or follows training referred to in Article 140quater, with a view to committing one of the offences referred to in Article 137, with the exception of that referred to in Article 137, § 3, 6°, shall be punished by imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine from one hundred euros to five thousand euros.]1

(1)<Inserted by L 2013-02-18/01, art. 7, 091; In force: 14-03-2013>

Art. 140sexies. [1 Without prejudice to the application of Article 140, shall be punished by imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine from one hundred euros to five thousand euros: 1° any person who leaves the national territory with a view to committing, in Belgium or abroad, an offence referred to in Articles 137, 140 to 140quinquies and 141, with the exception of the offence referred to in Article 137, § 3, 6°; 2° any person who enters the national territory with a view to committing, in Belgium or abroad, an offence referred to in Articles 137, 140 to 140quinquies and 141, with the exception of the offence referred to in Article 137, § 3, 6°.]1 (1)<Inserted by L 2015-07-20/08, art. 2, 112; In force: 15-08-2015>

Art. 140septies. [1 § 1er. Any person who prepares the commission of a terrorist offence referred to in Article 137, with the exception of the offence referred to in Article 137, § 3, 6°, shall be punished:

- by a term of imprisonment from eight days to one year, if the offence prepared is punished by a term of imprisonment of five years or less;
- by a term of imprisonment of three years or less, if the offence prepared is punished by imprisonment from five years to ten years;
- by a term of imprisonment of five years or less, if the offence prepared is punished by imprisonment from ten years to fifteen years or by imprisonment from fifteen years to twenty years;
- by imprisonment from five years to ten years, if the offence prepared is punished by imprisonment from twenty years to thirty years or by life imprisonment.

The accessory penalties provided for preparation are identical to those provided for the offence prepared.

###### § 2. For the application of this article, "to prepare" means in particular:

1° collecting information concerning places, events or persons so as to be able to commit an act in those places or during those events or to harm those persons, and observing those places, those events or those persons;

2° possessing, seeking, acquiring, transporting or manufacturing objects or substances likely to present a danger to others or to cause considerable economic losses;

3° possessing, seeking, acquiring, transporting or manufacturing financial or material means, false documents or documents obtained illegally, computer media, means of communication, means of transport;

4° possessing, seeking, acquiring premises that can serve as a hideout, meeting place, place of encounter or accommodation;

5° claiming in advance, in whatever form and by whatever means, the commission of a terrorist offence, with the exception of the offence referred to in Article 137, § 3, 6°.]1

(1)<Inserted by L 2016-12-14/09, art. 3, 120; In force: 01-01-2017>

###### Art. 141.[1 Shall be punished by imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine from one hundred euros to five thousand euros, any person who provides or collects, by any means whatsoever, directly or indirectly, material means, including financial assistance, with the intention that they be used or knowing that they will be used, in whole or in part, 1° with a view to committing or contributing to an offence referred to in Articles 137 and 140 to 140septies; or

2° by another person when the person who provides or collects the material means knows that that other person is committing or will commit an offence referred to in Article 137.]1

(1)<L 2016-12-14/09, art. 4, 120; In force: 01-01-2017>

Art. 141bis. <Inserted by L 2003-12-19/34, art. 8; In force: 08-01-2004> This title does not apply to the activities of armed forces during armed conflict, as defined and governed by international humanitarian law, nor to activities carried out by the armed forces of a State in the exercise of their official functions, insofar as they are governed by other rules of international law.

Art. 141ter.[1 No provision of this title may be interpreted as aimed at reducing or hindering [2 ...]2 fundamental rights or freedoms such as the right to strike, freedom of assembly and association, including the right to form and join trade unions with others for the protection of one's interests, and the related right to demonstrate, freedom of expression, in particular freedom of the press and freedom of expression in other media, and as enshrined in particular in Articles 8 to 11 of the European Convention for the Protection of Human Rights and Fundamental Freedoms.]1

(1)<L 2013-02-18/01, art. 8, 091; In force: 14-03-2013>

(2)<L 2014-04-25/23, art. 2, 104; In force: 24-05-2014>

#### TITRE II. - CRIMES AND OFFENCES THAT INFRINGE RIGHTS GUARANTEED BY THE CONSTITUTION.

##### CHAPITRE I. - OFFENCES RELATING TO THE EXERCISE OF POLITICAL RIGHTS.

<Repealed by L 2003-12-19/34, art. 10, 046; In force: 08-01-2004>

##### CHAPITRE I. OFFENCES RELATING TO THE FREE EXERCISE OF WORSHIP. <L 2003-12-19/34, art. 11, 046; In force: 08-01-2004>

###### Art. 142. Any person who, through violence or threats, has compelled

or prevented one or more persons from exercising a religion, from attending the exercise of that religion, from celebrating certain religious festivals, from observing certain days of rest, and, consequently, from opening or closing their workshops, shops or stores, and from doing or leaving certain work, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to two months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.

[1 If the offense was committed to the detriment of a person whose situation of vulnerability by reason of age, state of pregnancy, illness, infirmity or physical or mental deficiency was apparent or known to the perpetrator, the latter shall be punished by imprisonment of fifteen days to six months and a fine of twenty-six euros to five hundred euros.]1

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 2, 084; In force: 02-02-2012>

###### Art. 143. Those who, through disturbances or disorders, have prevented, delayed

or interrupted the exercises of a religion that are practiced in a place intended for or customarily serving the religion or in public ceremonies of that religion, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-

###### Art. 144. Any person who, by acts, words, gestures or threats, has outraged

the objects of a religion, either in places intended for or customarily serving its exercise, or in public ceremonies of that religion, shall be punished by imprisonment of fifteen days to six months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.

###### Art. 145. Shall be punished by the same penalties anyone who, by acts, words, gestures or

threats, has outraged a minister of a religion, in the exercise of his ministry. If he has struck him, he shall be punished by imprisonment of two months to two years and a fine of fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.

###### Art. 146. If the blows have caused effusion of blood, wound or

illness, the guilty person shall be punished by imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of one hundred [euros] to one thousand [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force

##### CHAPITRE II. - VIOLATIONS COMMITTED BY PUBLIC OFFICIALS OF

RIGHTS GUARANTEED BY THE CONSTITUTION. <L 2003-12-19/34, art. 12, 046; In force: 08-01-2004>

###### Art. 147. Any public official or officer, any depositary or agent of

the authority or of the public force, who has illegally and arbitrarily arrested or caused to be arrested, detained or caused to be detained one or more persons, shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to two years.

The imprisonment shall be six months to three years, if the illegal and arbitrary detention has lasted more than ten days.

If it has lasted more than one month, the guilty person shall be sentenced to imprisonment of one year to five years.

He shall, moreover, be punished by a fine of fifty [euros] to one thousand [euros] and may be sentenced to disqualification from the rights indicated in nos. 1, 2 and 3 of [1 article 31, paragraph 1]1. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002> (1)<L 2009-04-14/01, art. 10, 073; In force: 15-04-2009>

###### Art. 148. Any official of the administrative or judicial order, any officer of

justice or police, any commander or agent of the public force, who, acting in that capacity, has entered the dwelling of an inhabitant against his will, outside the cases provided for and without the formalities prescribed by law, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to six months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.

###### Art. 149. (Repealed)

<L 26-12-1956, art. 36>

###### Art. 150. (Repealed)

<L 13-10-1930, art. 31>

###### Art. 151. Any other arbitrary act violating the liberties and rights

guaranteed by the Constitution, ordered or executed by a public official or officer, by a depositary or agent of the authority or of the public force, shall be punished by imprisonment of fifteen days to one year.

###### Art. 152. If the accused justifies that he acted by order of his superiors, for

matters within their jurisdiction and on which hierarchical obedience was due to them, the penalties provided by the preceding articles shall be applied only to the superiors who gave the order.

###### Art. 153. If the public officials or officers, accused of having ordered,

authorized or facilitated one of the acts mentioned in articles 148 to 151, claim that their signature was obtained by surprise, they shall be required, by stopping, where applicable, the act, to denounce the guilty person; otherwise, they shall be prosecuted personally.

###### Art. 154. (See NOTE under TITLE) If one of the arbitrary acts mentioned in

articles 148 to 151 was committed by means of the forged signature of a public official, the perpetrators of the forgery and those who, maliciously or fraudulently, have made use of it, shall be punished (by imprisonment for a term) of ten years to fifteen years. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 40, 040; In force: 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 155. Public officials or officers responsible for the

administrative or judicial police, who, having the power, have neglected or refused to stop an illegal detention brought to their knowledge, shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to one year.

###### Art. 156. Public officials or officers responsible for the

administrative or judicial police, who, not having the power to stop an illegal detention, have neglected or refused to record that which has been brought to their knowledge, and to report it to the competent authority, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to six months.

###### Art. 157. The directors, commanders, guards and concierges of houses of

detention, arrest, (...) or penal institutions, who have received a prisoner without legal order or warrant or without judgment; In force: 01-07-1999>

Those who have detained him or have refused to present him to the police officer or to the bearer of his orders, without justifying the prohibition of the public prosecutor or the judge;

Those who have refused to exhibit their registers to the police officer; Shall be punished by imprisonment of fifteen days to two years and a fine of twenty-six francs to two hundred francs.

###### Art. 158. Shall be punished by a fine of two hundred [euros] to two thousand [euros],

and may be sentenced to disqualification from the right to hold public functions, employment or offices, all judges, all officers of the public prosecutor's office or of the judicial police, all other public officials who, without the prescribed authorizations, have provoked, given, signed either a judgment against a minister, a senator or a representative, or an order or warrant tending to prosecute them or to indict them, or who, without the same authorizations, have given or signed the order or warrant to seize or arrest either a minister, or a senator or a representative, except, as to the latter two, the case of flagrant offense. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 159. Shall be punished by the same penalty the officers of the public prosecutor's office, the

judges or the public officials who have detained or caused to be detained a person outside the places determined by the government or by the public administration.

#### TITLE III. - CRIMES AND OFFENSES AGAINST PUBLIC FAITH.

##### CHAPITRE I. - COUNTERFEITING.

###### Art. 160. (See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 12-07-1932, art. 1, 1°> Whoever has counterfeited gold or silver coins having legal tender in Belgium or abroad shall be punished (by imprisonment for a term) of ten years to fifteen years. <L 2003-01-23/42,

###### art. 41

, 040; In force: 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 161. (See NOTE under TITLE) Shall be punished with (imprisonment of five to ten years)

whoever shall have altered the same coins. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 42, 040; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 162.

<L 2001-04-04/39, art. 2, 031; En vigueur : 03-07-2001> Whoever shall have counterfeited coins of other metal having legal tender in Belgium or abroad or who shall have counterfeited coins denominated in euro shall be punished with imprisonment of five to ten years.

The guilty party may, in addition, be sentenced to prohibition, in accordance with article 33.

###### Art. 163.

<L 2001-04-04/39, art. 3, 031; En vigueur : 03-07-2001> The alteration of the same coins shall be punished with imprisonment of five to ten years.

<L 12-07-1932, art. 1, 3°>

<L 12-07-1932, art. 1, 3°>

###### Art. 166. (Repealed)

<L 12-07-1932, art. 1, 3°>

###### Art. 167. (Repealed)

###### Art. 168. Shall be punished as the forgers or as their accomplices, according to

the distinctions established in the preceding articles, those who, in concert with them, shall have participated either in the issuance or attempted issuance of the said counterfeited or altered coins, or in their introduction into Belgian territory or the attempt of such introduction.

###### Art. 169.Whoever, without being guilty of the participation stated in the

preceding article, shall have procured, with knowledge, counterfeited or altered coins and shall have put them into circulation, or attempted to put them into circulation, shall be punished with imprisonment of one month [to five years].

[Whoever, with the intention of putting them into circulation, shall have imported, exported, transported, received or procured counterfeited or altered coins, shall be punished with imprisonment of eight days to five years.]

(The attempt of the offense provided for in the preceding paragraph shall be punished with imprisonment of eight days to six months.) <L 12-07-1932, art. 1, 4°>

(1)<L 2017-10-18/09, art. 3, 125; En vigueur : 13-11-2017>

###### Art. 170. Whoever, having received as genuine counterfeited

or altered coins, shall have put them back into circulation, after having verified or had verified the defects, shall be punished with a fine of twenty-six [euros] to one thousand [euros] <L 2000-06- 26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>.

(The attempt of the offense provided for in the preceding paragraph shall be punished with a fine of twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros].) <L 2001-04-04/39, art. 4, 031; En vigueur : 03-07-2001> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

SPECIAL PROVISIONS.

###### Art. 171. (See NOTE under TITLE) Those who render themselves guilty of fraud in

the choice of samples intended, in execution of the monetary law, for the verification of the fineness and weight of gold and silver coins, shall be sentenced (to imprisonment) of fifteen to twenty years. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 43, 040; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 172. (See NOTE under TITLE) Those who shall have committed this fraud in the

choice of samples of coins of other metal shall be punished with (imprisonment of

##### CHAPTER II. - COUNTERFEITING OR FALSIFICATION OF PUBLIC INSTRUMENTS, OF

SHARES, OF BONDS, INTEREST COUPONS AND BANK NOTES

AUTHORIZED BY LAW.

###### Art. 173.

<L 2001-04-04/39, art. 5, 031; En vigueur : 03-07-2001> Shall be punished with imprisonment of fifteen to twenty years, those who shall have counterfeited or falsified bonds issued by the Public Treasury, interest coupons relating to these bonds, vouchers, checks or transfers issued by the treasury, bearer notes issued by the Public Treasury or bearer bank notes having legal tender or whose issuance is authorized by a law or pursuant to a law or denominated in euro.

Shall be punished with the same penalties, those who shall have counterfeited or falsified bearer notes having legal tender or whose issuance is authorized by a law of a foreign country or pursuant to a provision having force of law therein.

###### Art. 174. (See NOTE under TITLE) Shall be punished (with imprisonment) of ten to

fifteen years, those who shall have counterfeited or falsified either bearer bonds of the public debt of a foreign country, or interest coupons relating to these securities (...). <L 12-07-1932, art. 1, 6°> <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 45, 040; En vigueur

###### Art. 175. (See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 46, 040; En vigueur : 13- 03-2003> Those who shall have counterfeited or falsified either shares, bonds or other securities, legally issued by provinces, municipalities, administrations or public establishments, under whatever denomination, by companies or individuals, or interest or dividend coupons relating to these various securities, shall be punished with ten to fifteen years of imprisonment, if the issuance took place in Belgium and five to ten years of the same penalty, if the issuance took place abroad.

###### Art. 176. Shall be punished as the forgers or as their accomplices, according to

the distinctions established in the preceding articles, those who, in concert with them, shall have participated either in the issuance or attempted issuance of these counterfeited or falsified shares, bonds, coupons or notes, or in their introduction into Belgium, or the attempt of such introduction.

###### Art. 177.Whoever, without having rendered themselves guilty of the participation stated in the

preceding article, shall have procured, with knowledge, these counterfeited or falsified shares, bonds, coupons, notes and shall have issued or attempted to issue them, shall be punished with imprisonment of one year to five years.

[Whoever, with the intention of putting them into circulation, shall have imported, exported, transported, received or procured counterfeited or falsified notes shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to five years.]

(The attempt of the offense provided for in the preceding paragraph shall be punished with imprisonment of three months to one year.) <L 12-07-1932, art. 1, 7°>

(1)<L 2017-10-18/09, art. 2, 125; En vigueur : 13-11-2017>

Art. 177bis. [Articles 173, 176 and 177 apply indiscriminately to notes that have already been issued and put into circulation as notes having legal tender and to notes that, although intended to be put into circulation as notes having legal tender, have not yet been issued.]

(1)<Inséré par L 2017-10-18/09, art. 6, 125; En vigueur : 13-11-2017>

###### Art. 178. Anyone who, having received as genuine counterfeit or falsified shares, bonds, coupons or

bills, shall have put them back into circulation after having verified or had verified their defects, shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to one year and by a fine of fifty [euros] to one thousand [euros], or by only one of these penalties. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

(The attempt of the offence provided for in the preceding paragraph shall be punished by imprisonment of fifteen days to six months and by a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros] or by only one of these penalties.) <L 2001-04-04/39, art. 6, 031; In force: 03-07-2001> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

CHAPITRE IIbis. <Inserted by L 2001-12-10/31, art. 19; In force: 01-01-2002> -

PROTECTION OF MONETARY SIGNS HAVING LEGAL TENDER.

Art. 178bis. <Inserted by L 2001-12-10/31, art. 20; In force: 01-01-2002> Anyone who shall have issued a monetary sign intended to circulate among the public as a means of payment without having been authorized to do so by the competent authority, shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to one year and by a fine of 50 to 10,000 EUR, or by only one of these penalties. ".

Art. 178ter. <Inserted by L 2001-12-10/31, art. 21; In force: 01-01-2002> Anyone who shall have, knowingly, used a monetary sign having legal tender in Belgium or abroad as a medium for a message, advertising or otherwise, or who, knowingly, shall have made its use as a means of payment more difficult by deteriorating, staining, overloading it or by making it unfit in any manner whatsoever, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three months and by a fine of 26 to 1,000 EUR, or by only one of these penalties.

##### CHAPITRE III. - COUNTERFEITING OR FALSIFICATION OF SEALS, STAMPS,

HALLMARKS, MARKS, ETC.

###### Art. 179. (See NOTE under TITLE) Shall be punished (by imprisonment) of ten years to

fifteen years, those who shall have counterfeited the seal of the State, or made use of the counterfeit seal. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 47, 040; In force: 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 180.

<L 2001-04-04/39, art. 7, 031; In force: 03-07-2001> Shall be punished by imprisonment of five years to ten years:

Those who shall have counterfeited or falsified either national stamps, or the hallmarks used to mark gold or silver materials.

Those who shall have made use of these counterfeit or falsified stamps or hallmarks.
Those who shall have counterfeited or falsified the hallmarks, dies, squares or other objects or means intended for the manufacture of coins.

Those who shall have counterfeited or falsified the hallmarks, matrices, blocks, plates or other objects or means used for the manufacture, either of stamps, or of shares, bonds, interest or dividend coupons, or of bearer notes issued by the Public Treasury or of banknotes having legal tender or the issue of which has been authorized by a law or pursuant to a law or denominated in euro.

###### Art. 181. Shall be punished by the same penalty those who shall have knowingly exposed for

sale papers or gold or silver materials marked with a counterfeit or falsified stamp or hallmark.

###### Art. 182. If the marks affixed by the assay office have been

fraudulently applied on other objects, or if these marks or the impression of a stamp have been counterfeited without the use of a counterfeit hallmark or stamp, the offenders shall be punished by imprisonment of six months to five years.

###### Art. 183. Anyone who, having obtained with knowledge paper marked with a

counterfeit or falsified stamp, shall have made use of it, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to six months.

###### Art. 184. Shall be punished by three months to three years' imprisonment and may be

sentenced to prohibition in accordance with article 33:

Anyone who shall have counterfeited coupons for the transport of persons or things, or who shall have made use of the counterfeit coupon;

Anyone who shall have counterfeited the seal, stamp or mark either of any authority whatsoever, or of a private establishment, bank, industry or commerce, or of a private individual, or who shall have made use of counterfeit seals, stamps or marks.

The attempt of these offences shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to one year.

###### Art. 185. Shall be punished by imprisonment of two months to three years, anyone who,

having improperly obtained the true seals, stamps, hallmarks and marks having one of the purposes expressed in articles 179 and 180, shall have made an application or use thereof prejudicial to the rights and interests of the State, of any authority whatsoever or even of a private individual.

The attempt of this offence shall be punished by imprisonment of fifteen days to one year.
Art. 185bis.<L 2001-04-04/39, art. 8, 031; In force: 03-07-2001> Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to one year:

Those who, with fraudulent intent, [shall have received, obtained or possessed] either the hallmarks, dies, squares or other counterfeit or falsified objects or means, referred to in the penultimate paragraph of article 180, or the true hallmarks, dies, squares or other objects or means intended for the manufacture of coins.

Those who, with fraudulent intent, shall have received or obtained either the hallmarks, matrices, blocks, plates or other counterfeit or falsified objects or means, intended by their nature for the counterfeiting or falsification of bearer notes issued by the Public Treasury or of banknotes having legal tender or the issue of which has been authorized by a law or pursuant to a law or denominated in euro, or the true hallmarks, matrices, blocks, plates or other objects or means intended for the manufacture of these notes.

[Those who, with fraudulent intent, shall have manufactured, received, possessed or obtained security devices used to protect coins or notes against counterfeiting.]

(1)<L 2017-10-18/09, art. 7, 125; In force: 13-11-2017>

###### Art. 186.

<L 2001-04-04/39, art. 9, 031; In force: 03-07-2001> Those who shall have counterfeited or falsified the seals, stamps, hallmarks or marks having one of the purposes indicated in articles 179 and 180 and belonging to foreign countries, or who shall have made use of these counterfeit or falsified seals, stamps, hallmarks or marks, shall be punished by imprisonment of five years to ten years.
Shall be punished by the same penalty:

Those who shall have counterfeited or falsified the hallmarks, dies, squares or other objects or means intended for the manufacture of foreign coins.

Those who shall have counterfeited or falsified the hallmarks, matrices, blocks, plates or other objects or means used for the manufacture of bearer notes issued by a foreign State or of banknotes having legal tender there or the issue of which is authorized by a law of a foreign country or by a provision having force of law there.

Those who shall have counterfeited the seal, stamp or mark of any foreign authority whatsoever or who shall have made use of counterfeit seals, stamps or marks, shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to three years and may be sentenced to prohibition in accordance with article 33.

The attempt of this offence shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to one year.

###### Art. 187. (Sera puni d'un emprisonnement d'un mois à deux ans, quiconque,

s'étant indûment procuré les vrais sceaux, timbres, poinçons ou marques ayant l'une des destinations indiquées aux articles 179 et 180 et appartenant à des pays étrangers, en aura fait une application ou un usage préjudiciable aux droits et aux intérêts de ces pays, d'une autorité quelconque ou même d'un particulier.) <L 22- 06-1896, art. 2>

La tentative de ce délit sera punie d'un emprisonnement de huit jours à six mois. Art. 187bis. <L 2001-04-04/39, art. 10, 031; En vigueur : 03-07-2001> Sera puni d'un emprisonnement de huit jours à un an :

Quiconque, dans une intention frauduleuse, aura reçu ou se sera procuré soit les poinçons, coins, carrés ou autres objets ou moyens contrefaits ou falsifiés visés à l'article 186, alinéa 3, soit les vrais poinçons, coins, carrés ou autres objets ou moyens destinés à la fabrication des monnaies étrangères.

Quiconque, dans une intention frauduleuse, aura reçu ou se sera procuré soit les poinçons, matrices, clichés, planches ou autres objets ou moyens contrefaits ou falsifiés visés à l'article 186, alinéa 4, soit les vrais poinçons, matrices, clichés, planches ou autres objets ou moyens destinés à la fabrication de billets au porteur émis par un Etat étranger ou de billets de banque y ayant cours légal ou dont l'émission est autorisée par une loi d'un pays étranger ou par une disposition y ayant force de loi.

###### Art. 188. Shall be punished by imprisonment from two months to three years and

may be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with Article 33, those who shall have counterfeited postage stamps or other national or foreign adhesive stamps, or who shall have exposed for sale or put into circulation counterfeit stamps.

The attempt to counterfeit shall be punished by imprisonment from one month to one year.

###### Art. 189. Those who, having obtained counterfeit postage stamps or other adhesive stamps,

shall have made use thereof, shall be punished by imprisonment from eight days to one month.

###### Art. 190. Shall be punished by a fine from twenty-six [euros] to three hundred [euros]: <L

2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

Those who shall have removed, either from a postage stamp or other adhesive stamp, or from a coupon for the transport of persons or things, the mark indicating that they have already been used;

Those who shall have made use of a stamp or coupon from which this mark has been removed.

Art. 190bis.<Inséré par L 1991-03-21/30, art. 150, 006; En vigueur : 01-10-1992> The provisions of Articles 188 to 190 apply not only to adhesive postage stamps, but also to those that are printed on documents issued by [ bpost] , as well as to postage values represented by machine impressions or by symbols approved by [ bpost] .

(1)<L 2010-12-13/07, art. 4, 081; En vigueur : 17-01-2011>

###### Art. 191. Anyone who shall have, either affixed, or caused to be affixed by addition,

removal or by any alteration whatsoever, on manufactured objects, the name of a manufacturer other than the one who is the author thereof, or the trade name of a factory other than that of manufacture, shall be punished by imprisonment from one month to six months.

The same penalty shall be pronounced against any merchant, commission agent or retailer whatsoever who shall have knowingly exposed for sale or put into circulation objects marked with false or altered names.

[Special provision] <L 2014-05-05/09, art. 12, 106; En vigueur : 18-07-

###### Art. 192.

<L 12-07-1932, art. 1, 11°> Persons guilty of the offences mentioned in Articles 160 to 168, 169, paragraph 2, 171 to 176, 177, paragraph 2, in the last two paragraphs of Article 180, in Articles 185bis, 186, paragraphs 2 to 4, 187bis, 497, paragraph 2, and in Article 497bis, paragraph 1, shall be exempt from penalties if, before any issue of counterfeit or altered coins or counterfeit or falsified papers, and before any prosecution, they have given notice thereof and revealed the perpetrators to the authority.

Art. 192bis. <Inséré par L 2001-04-04/39, art. 11; En vigueur : 03-07-2001> Acts qualified as offences concerning the euro, described in Chapters I, II and III of this title, are punished by the penalties provided for in the same provisions when they are committed with regard to Belgian coins or banknotes or those of a Member State of the European Union no longer having legal tender or whose issuance is no longer authorized following the introduction or adoption of the euro in cash.

Art. 192ter. <Inséré par L 2005-01-10/38, art. 2, En vigueur : 20-02-2005> § 1er. Anyone who, having been sentenced to imprisonment of more than five years, by a court of a Member State of the European Union for acts referred to in Articles 160 to 170, 173, 176 to 178, 180, and 185 to 187bis, shall have committed one of these acts again, may be sentenced to imprisonment from ten years to fifteen years, if this act is a crime carrying imprisonment from five years to ten years. If this act is a crime carrying imprisonment from ten years to fifteen years, he may be sentenced to imprisonment from fifteen years to twenty years. He shall be sentenced to imprisonment of at least seventeen years, if this act is a crime carrying imprisonment from fifteen years to twenty years.

###### § 2. Anyone who, having been sentenced to imprisonment of more than

five years, by a court of a Member State of the European Union for acts referred to in Articles 160 to 170, 173, 176 to 178, 180, and 185 to 187bis, shall have committed one of these acts again, may be sentenced to a penalty of double the maximum provided by law for this act, if this act is an offence

###### § 3. Anyone who, having been sentenced to imprisonment of at least one year,

by a court of a Member State of the European Union for acts referred to in Articles 160 to 170, 173, 176 to 178, 180, and 185 to 187bis, shall have committed one of these acts again, may be sentenced to a penalty of double the maximum provided by law for this act, if this act is an offence.

##### CHAPTER IV. - FORGERIES COMMITTED IN WRITINGS, IN COMPUTER DATA AND IN

TELEGRAPHIC DISPATCHES. <L 2000-11-28/34, art. 2 , 028; En vigueur : 13-

###### Art. 193. Forgery committed in writings (,in computer data) or in telegraphic

dispatches, with fraudulent intent or with the intention to cause harm, shall be punished in accordance with the following articles. <L 2000-11-28/34, art. 3, 028; En vigueur

###### SECTION I. - FORGERIES IN AUTHENTIC AND PUBLIC WRITINGS, IN

COMMERCIAL OR BANKING WRITINGS AND IN PRIVATE WRITINGS.

###### Art. 194. (Voir NOTE sous TITRE) Any public servant or public officer who, in

the exercise of his functions, shall have committed a forgery, Either by false signatures, Or by alteration of deeds, writings or signatures, Or by substitution of persons, Or by writings made or inserted in registers or other public deeds, after their completion or closing, Shall be punished (by imprisonment) from ten years to fifteen years. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 48, 040; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 195. (Voir NOTE sous TITRE) Shall be punished (by imprisonment) from ten years to fifteen

years, any public servant or public officer who, in drawing up deeds of his office, shall have distorted the substance or circumstances thereof, <L 2003-01-23/42,

###### art. 48

, 040; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

Either by writing conventions other than those that were drawn up or dictated by the parties, Or by recording as true facts that were not.

###### Art. 196. (See NOTE under TITLE) Shall be punished with (imprisonment of five to ten

years) other persons who have committed forgery in authentic and public writings, and all persons who have committed forgery in commercial writings, banking writings or private writings, <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 49, 040; Effective: 13-03-2003>

Either by false signatures, Or by counterfeiting or alteration of writings or signatures, Or by fabrication of agreements, provisions, obligations or releases or by their subsequent insertion in the deeds, Or by addition or alteration of clauses, declarations or facts that these deeds were intended to receive or record.

###### Art. 197. In all cases expressed in this section, whoever has made

use of the false deed or the false document shall be punished as if he were the author of the forgery.

###### SECTION II. - FORGERY COMMITTED IN PASSPORTS, WEAPONS PERMITS,

BOOKLETS, TRAVEL WARRANTS AND CERTIFICATES.

###### Art. 198. Whoever has counterfeited or falsified a passport, (a document covered

by the law on weapons) or a booklet, or has made use of a counterfeited or falsified passport, (document covered by the law on weapons) or booklet, shall be punished with imprisonment of one month to one year. <L 2006-06-08/30, art. 39, 057; Effective

###### Art. 199. Whoever has taken in a passport, (a document covered by the law on

weapons) or a booklet, an assumed name, or has participated as a witness in having these documents issued, under the assumed name, shall be punished with imprisonment of eight days to six months. <L 2006-06-08/30, art. 39, 057; Effective: 09-06-2006> Art. 199bis. <L 14-08-1974, art. 15> Shall be punished with imprisonment of 8 days to 6 months and a fine of 26 [euros] to 500 [euros], or one of these penalties only: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, Effective: 01-01-2002>

1° whoever, for fraudulent purposes, uses, transfers to a third party or accepts from a third party, a passport, a travel document, an identity card or a document in lieu thereof, as well as the forms used for their issuance, or who does not respect the prohibitions and restrictions imposed thereon;

2° whoever does not comply, within the time limit set, with a decision to withdraw a passport or document in lieu thereof, issued by the competent authority.

###### Art. 200. Shall be punished with one month to two years of imprisonment, whoever has

manufactured, counterfeited or falsified a travel warrant or has made use of a manufactured, counterfeited or falsified travel warrant.

###### Art. 201. Any person who has had a travel warrant issued by the public officer under

an assumed name or by taking a false status, shall be punished with imprisonment of eight days to two years.

###### Art. 202. The public officer who has issued a passport, (a document covered by the

law on weapons), a booklet, a travel warrant to a person he did not know, without having his name and status attested by two citizens known to him, shall be punished with a fine of twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, Effective: 01-01-2002> <L 2006-06-08/30, art. 39, 057; Effective: 09-06-2006>

If the public officer was aware of the assumed name or status, when he issued these documents, he shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to three years. He shall be punished with imprisonment of one year to five years, if he was motivated by gifts or promises.

In these last two cases, he may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33.

###### Art. 203. Shall be punished with imprisonment of eight days to one year, any person

who, to redeem himself or release another from a service legally due, or from any other obligation imposed by law, has manufactured a certificate of illness or infirmity, either under the name of a doctor, surgeon or other health officer, or under any name by falsely adding one of these qualifications.

###### Art. 204. Any doctor, surgeon or other health officer who, to favor

someone, has falsely certified illnesses or infirmities capable of dispensing with a service legally due or any other obligation imposed by law, shall be punished with imprisonment of eight days to two years.

If he was motivated by gifts or promises, he shall be punished with imprisonment of one year to five years; he may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33.

###### Art. 205. Whoever has manufactured, under the name of a public official or officer

public, a certificate attesting to good conduct, indigence or any other circumstance capable of calling the benevolence of the public authority or private individuals upon the person designated therein, or of procuring for him positions, credit or assistance, shall be punished with imprisonment of one month to one year.

If the certificate was manufactured under the name of a private individual, the guilty party shall be punished with imprisonment of eight days to six months.

###### Art. 206. Those who have manufactured, under the name of a public official or officer

public, certificates of any nature capable of compromising public or private interests, shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to five years, and may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33.

If the certificate was manufactured under the name of a private individual, the guilty party shall be punished with imprisonment of two months to one year.

###### Art. 207. Whoever has falsified a certificate, and whoever has made use of a

falsified, false or manufactured certificate in the circumstances enumerated in articles 203, 204, 205 and 206, shall be punished with the penalties provided by these articles and according to the distinctions they establish.

###### Art. 208. (See NOTE under TITLE) Any public official or officer who, in

the exercise of his functions, has issued a false certificate, falsified a certificate, or made use of a false or falsified certificate, shall be punished with (imprisonment of five to ten years). <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 49, 040; Effective: 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 209. Those who have participated as witnesses in having a false

certificate issued by a public authority shall be punished with imprisonment of eight days to two years.

If they have allowed themselves to be corrupted by gifts or promises, they shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to three years, and they may be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33.

###### Art. 210.

<L 17-12-1963, art. 3> Whoever, entrusted by law or by virtue thereof to keep a register or files concerning the accommodation of travelers, has knowingly registered these persons under false names or has falsified this register or these files in any other manner, shall be punished with imprisonment of eight days to three months.

SECTION IIbis. - Computer forgery. <inserted by L 2000-11-28/34, art. 4; Effective: 13-02-2001>

Art. 210bis. <inserted by L 2000-11-28/34, art. 4; Effective: 13-02-2001> § 1er. Whoever commits a forgery, by introducing into a computer system, by modifying or deleting data, which are stored, processed or transmitted by a computer system, or by modifying by any technological means the possible use of data in a computer system, and thereby modifies the legal scope of such data, is punished with imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to one hundred thousand [euros] or one of these penalties only <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, Effective: 01-01-2002>.

###### § 2. Whoever makes use of the data thus obtained, while knowing that they

are false, is punished as if he were the author of the forgery.

###### § 3. The attempt to commit the offence referred to in § 1er and is punished with

imprisonment of six months to three years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to fifty thousand [euros] or one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, Effective: 01-01-2002>

###### § 4. The penalties provided for in §§ 1 to 3 are doubled if an offence against one

of these provisions is committed within five years following the pronouncement of a conviction for one of these offences or for one of the offences provided for in articles 259bis, 314bis, 504quater or in title IXbis.

###### SECTION III. - FORGERY COMMITTED IN TELEGRAPHIC DISPATCHES.

###### Art. 211. Public officers, employees and agents of a telegraphic service,

who have committed a forgery in the exercise of their functions, by fabricating or falsifying telegraphic dispatches, shall be punished by imprisonment from one year to five years.

###### Art. 212. Whoever has made use of the false dispatch shall be punished as if he were

the perpetrator of the forgery.

[Provisions common to the five preceding chapters]<L 2014-05-05/09, art.

13, 106; En vigueur : 18-07-2014>

###### Art. 213. The application of penalties imposed against those who have made use of

counterfeit, fabricated or falsified coins, effects, coupons, notes, seals, stamps, punches, marks, telegraphic dispatches and writings, shall only take place insofar as these persons have made use of the false thing, with fraudulent intent or with intent to cause harm.

###### Art. 214.In the cases provided for [ in chapters I to IV of this title] and for

which no fine is specifically imposed, a fine of twenty-six [euros] to two thousand [euros] shall be pronounced. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01- (1)<L 2014-05-05/09, art. 14, 106; En vigueur : 18-07-2014>

##### CHAPITRE V. - FALSE TESTIMONY AND FALSE OATH.

###### Art. 215. (See NOTE under TITLE) False testimony in criminal matters, either

against the accused or in his favour, shall be punished by (imprisonment from five years to ten years).

<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 49, 040; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 216.

<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 50, 040; En vigueur : 13-03-2003> If the accused has been sentenced, either to detention of more than ten years or to fixed-term imprisonment of more than ten years, the false witness who has testified against him shall undergo the penalty of imprisonment from ten years to fifteen years.

He shall undergo that of imprisonment from twenty years to thirty years, if the accused has been sentenced to life imprisonment.

###### Art. 217. The penalties imposed by the two preceding articles shall be reduced by one

degree, in accordance with article 80, when persons called to court to provide simple information have been found guilty of false declarations, either against the accused or in his favour.

###### Art. 218. The person guilty of false testimony in correctional matters, either against

the defendant or in his favour, shall be punished by imprisonment from six months to five years.

###### Art. 219. The person guilty of false testimony in police matters, either against the

defendant or in his favour, shall be punished by imprisonment from three months to one year.

###### Art. 220. False testimony in civil matters shall be punished by imprisonment

from two months to three years.

###### Art. 221. The interpreter and the expert guilty of false declarations, either in

criminal matters against the accused or in his favour, or in correctional or police matters, against the defendant or in his favour, or in civil matters, shall be punished as false witnesses, in accordance with articles 215, 216, 218, 219 and 220. The expert in criminal matters who has been heard without taking an oath shall be punished in accordance with article 217.

Art. 221bis. <L 10-10-1967, art. 132> Whoever, being responsible for making the literal recording of an inquiry in civil matters, has knowingly omitted a question, declaration, interpellation or answer, knowingly modifies its content by addition, deletion or alteration of words or sentences, distorted, removed or caused to disappear, in whole or in part, the notes or devices used to collect the recorded words, makes use of these notes or devices, reproduces or discloses their content for purposes unrelated to the inquiry, or knowingly transcribes inaccurately the recorded words, shall be punished by imprisonment from eight days to three years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros], or by only one of these penalties. <L 2000-06-26/42,

###### art. 2

, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

He shall be punished by a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros] if he has neglected to take the necessary precautions to prevent either the disappearance or distortion of the notes or devices used to collect the recorded words, or the use of these notes or devices, the reproduction or disclosure of their content for purposes unrelated to the inquiry. <L 2000-06-26/42,

###### art. 2

, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 222.

<L 10-10-1967, art. 133> In the cases provided for by articles 217, 218, 219, 220, 221 and 221bis, paragraph 1, the guilty person may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33.

###### Art. 223.

<L 10-10-1967, art. 134> The person guilty of subornation of witnesses, experts, interpreters or persons referred to in article 221bis shall be liable to the same penalties as the false witness, according to the distinctions established by articles 215 to 222.

Art. 223bis. <L 10-10-1967, art. 135> Whoever, except in the case referred to in article 221bis, has distorted, removed or caused to disappear, in whole or in part, the notes or devices used to collect the words recorded during an inquiry in civil matters, or made use of these notes or devices, reproduced or disclosed their content for purposes unrelated to the inquiry, shall be punished by the penalties provided for in articles 220 and 222.

###### Art. 224. (The person guilty of false testimony, false declarations or one of the

acts referred to in articles 221bis and 223bis, who has received money, any reward or promises, shall be sentenced, in addition, to a fine of fifty [euros] to three thousand [euros].) <L 10-10-1967, art. 136> <L 2000-06- 26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

The same penalty shall be applied to the suborner, without prejudice to other penalties.

###### Art. 225. The preceding provisions relating to false declarations are not

applicable to children under sixteen years of age, nor to persons who are heard without taking an oath, by reason of the kinship or affinity that unites them with the accused or the defendants, when these declarations have been made in favour of the accused or defendants.

###### Art. 226. Whoever has been tendered or referred an oath in civil matters, and who

has made a false oath, shall be punished by imprisonment from six months to three years, and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to ten thousand [euros]; he may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

(Whoever has made a false oath during an opposition to seals or an inventory is punished by the same penalties.) <L 10-10-1967, art. 167>

##### CHAPITRE VI. - USURPATION OF FUNCTIONS, TITLES OR NAMES.

###### Art. 227. Whoever has interfered in public, civil or

military functions, shall be punished by imprisonment from one month to two years.

Art. 227bis. <L 07-05-1947, art. unique> § 1. Shall be punished by a fine of two hundred [euros] to one thousand [euros], whoever, without right, has publicly attributed to himself the title or rank belonging, as a holder or substitute, to persons participating in the exercise of a public power or exercising a public, civil or military function.) <L 01-02-1977, art. unique> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### § 2. Seront punis d'une amende de cent à cinq cents [euros], les officiers de

reserve, retired officers, officers and reserve officers holding an honorary rank, who have publicly worn the title of officer or that of their rank without following it, as appropriate, with the mention "reserve", "retired", "honorary", "reserve, honorary". <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01- Art. 227ter. <L 10-10-1967, art. 138> Anyone who has publicly assumed either the title of lawyer, without being registered on the roll of the Order or on a list of trainees, or the title of honorary lawyer, without having obtained the authorization referred to in Article 436 of the Judicial Code, shall be punished by a fine of two hundred [euros] to one thousand [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>.

Art. 227quater. [ Shall be punished by a fine of two hundred euros to twenty thousand euros 1° anyone who acts professionally as a mediator within the meaning of the Judicial Code, without appearing on the list of accredited mediators referred to in Article 1727 and without being exempt from accreditation, with the exception of anyone who acts professionally as a mediator within the meaning of the Judicial Code in disputes between businesses.

2° anyone who, without being authorized to do so, publicly assumes the professional title of accredited mediator and anyone who bears a title or adds to the title they bear a mention that may cause confusion with the professional title of accredited mediator.

Anyone who provides their collaboration to a third party or lends them their name in order to exempt them from the penalty for unlawful use of the title of accredited mediator or unlawful practice of the profession of accredited mediator shall be punished by the same penalty.]

(1)<Inséré par L 2018-06-18/03, art. 238, 130; En vigueur : 12-07-2018>

###### Art. 228. Any person who has publicly worn a costume, a uniform,

a decoration, a ribbon or other insignia of an order to which they do not belong, shall be punished by a fine of two hundred [euros] to one thousand [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42,

###### art. 2

, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>.

###### Art. 229. Any Belgian who has publicly worn the decoration, ribbon or other

insignia of a foreign order before having obtained the authorization of the King, shall be punished by a fine of fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42,

###### art. 2

, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>.

###### Art. 230. Anyone who has publicly assumed titles of nobility to which they do not

belong shall be punished by a fine of two hundred [euros] to one thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 231. Anyone who has publicly assumed a name to which they do not belong

shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three months, and by a fine of twenty-five [euros] to three hundred [euros], or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 232. Any official, any public officer who, in their acts, attributes

to the persons named therein names or titles of nobility to which they do not belong, shall be punished, in case of connivance, by a fine of two hundred [euros] to one thousand [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>.

#### TITLE IV. - (CRIMES AND OFFENCES AGAINST PUBLIC ORDER, COMMITTED BY

PERSONS EXERCISING A PUBLIC FUNCTION OR BY MINISTERS

OF RELIGION IN THE EXERCISE OF THEIR MINISTRY.) <L 1999-02-10/39, art. 2, 023; En vigueur : 02-04-1999>

##### CHAPTER I. - COALITION OF PUBLIC OFFICIALS.

###### Art. 233. When measures contrary to the laws or to royal decrees have

been concerted, either in a meeting of individuals or bodies holding any part of public authority, or by deputation or correspondence between them, the offenders shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to six months.

###### Art. 234.(See NOTE under TITLE) If, by one of the means expressed in the preceding

article, measures have been concerted against the execution of a law or a royal decree, the penalty shall be imprisonment of six months to five years.

The offenders may, in addition, be sentenced to deprivation of the rights mentioned in the first three numbers of [ Article 31, paragraph 1] .

If the concerted action has taken place between the civil authorities and the military corps or their leaders, those who provoked it shall be punished by detention of ten years to fifteen years; the others, by detention of five years to ten years.

(1)<L 2009-04-14/01, art. 11, 073; En vigueur : 15-04-2009>

###### Art. 235. (See NOTE under TITLE) In the case where the civil authorities have

formed with the military corps or their leaders a conspiracy threatening the security of the State, the instigators shall be punished by (detention of fifteen years to twenty years); the others, by detention of ten years to fifteen years. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 51, 040; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 236. Public officials who, as a result of concerted action, have tendered their resignations

with the aim of preventing or suspending either the administration of justice or the performance of a lawful service shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to two years and by a fine of one hundred [euros] to five hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

They may be sentenced, in addition, to deprivation of the right to hold public functions, positions or offices.

##### CHAPTER II. - ENCROACHMENT BY ADMINISTRATIVE AND

JUDICIAL AUTHORITIES.

###### Art. 237.Shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to two years, by a fine

of fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros], and may be sentenced to deprivation, for five years to ten years, of the rights mentioned in the first three numbers of [ Article 31, paragraph 1] : <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

(Members and lay members of courts and tribunals, judicial police officers) who have interfered in the exercise of legislative power, either by regulations containing legislative provisions, or by stopping or suspending the execution of one or more laws, or by deliberating on the question of whether such laws shall be executed;

(Members and lay members of courts and tribunals, judicial police officers), who have exceeded their power by interfering in matters attributed to administrative authorities, either by making regulations on these matters, or by prohibiting the execution of orders issued by the administration. <L 10-10-1967, art. 139>

(1)<L 2009-04-14/01, art. 12, 073; En vigueur : 15-04-2009>

###### Art. 238. (Judges and social or commercial lay judges) who, when

the administrative authority is a party before them, have nevertheless proceeded to judgment of the case, despite the conflict legally raised by that authority and before the decision of the Court of Cassation, shall each be punished by a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros]. <L 10-10-1967, art. 139, § 2> <L 2000-06- 26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

Officers of the public prosecutor's office who have made requisitions or given submissions for the said judgment shall be punished by the same penalty.

###### Art. 239.Governors, district commissioners, mayors and

members of administrative bodies who have interfered in the exercise of legislative power, as stated in paragraph 2 of Article 237, or who have undertaken to make orders tending to issue orders or prohibitions of any kind to courts or tribunals, shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to two years and by a fine of fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>.

They may, in addition, be sentenced to deprivation, for five years to ten years, of the rights mentioned in the first three numbers of [ Article 31, paragraph 1] . (1)<L 2009-04-14/01, art. 13, 073; En vigueur : 15-04-2009>

##### CHAPTER III. - (MISAPPROPRIATION, EXTORTION AND CONFLICT OF INTEREST

COMMITTED BY PERSONS EXERCISING A PUBLIC FUNCTION). <L 1999-02-10/39, art. 3, 023; En vigueur : 02-04-1999>

###### Art. 240.

<L 1999-02-10/39, art. 3, 023; En vigueur : 02-04-1999> Any person exercising a public function who has misappropriated public or private funds, instruments in lieu thereof, documents, titles, deeds, movable property that were in their hands either by virtue of or by reason of their function shall be punished by imprisonment of five to ten years and a fine of 500 [euros] to 100,000 [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 241.

<L 1999-02-10/39, art. 3, 023; En vigueur : 02-04-1999> Any person exercising a public function who has maliciously or fraudulently destroyed or suppressed deeds or titles of which they were the depositary in that capacity, which had been communicated to them or to which they had access by reason of their function shall be punished by imprisonment of five to ten years and a fine of 500 [euros] to 100,000 [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-

###### Art. 242.

<L 1999-02-10/39, art. 3, 023; En vigueur : 02-04-1999> Where documents or acts of judicial proceedings, or other papers, registers, computer or magnetic media, deeds or instruments contained in archives, registry offices or public depositories, or entrusted to a public depositary in that capacity, have been removed or destroyed, the depositary guilty of negligence shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to six months and a fine of 100 [euros] to 10,000 [euros] or one of these penalties. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-

###### Art. 243.

<L 1999-02-10/39, art. 3, 023; En vigueur : 02-04-1999> Any person exercising a public function who has been guilty of extortion by ordering the collection of, demanding or receiving what they knew was not due or exceeded what was due for duties, taxes, contributions, funds, revenues or interest, for salaries or remuneration, shall be punished by imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of 100 [euros] to 50,000 [euros] or one of these penalties, and may in addition be sentenced to disqualification from the right to hold public functions, employments or offices, in accordance with article 33. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

The penalty shall be imprisonment of five to ten years and a fine of 500 [euros] to 100,000 [euros], if the extortion was committed by means of violence or threats. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 244. (Repealed)

<L 1999-02-10/39, art. 3, 023; En vigueur : 02-04-1999> (...). <L 1999-02-10/39, art. 3, 023; En vigueur : 02-04-1999>

###### Art. 245.

<L 1999-02-10/39, art. 3, 023; En vigueur : 02-04-1999> Any person exercising a public function who, either directly or through the interposition of persons or by simulated acts, has taken or received any interest whatsoever in acts, adjudications, enterprises or administrations of which they had, at the time of the act, in whole or in part, the administration or supervision, or who, having the mission to authorize payment or to settle an affair, has taken any interest therein, shall be punished by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of 100 [euros] to 50,000 [euros] or one of these penalties, and may in addition be sentenced to disqualification from the right to hold public functions, employments or offices, in accordance with article 33. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

The preceding provision shall not apply to one who could not, by reason of the circumstances, favor their private interests by their position, and who has acted openly.

##### CHAPTER IV. - (CORRUPTION OF PERSONS EXERCISING A PUBLIC

FUNCTION). <L 1999-02-10/39, art. 4, 023; En vigueur : 02-04-1999>

###### Art. 246.

<L 1999-02-10/39, art. 4, 023; En vigueur : 02-04-1999> § 1er. Passive corruption consists in the act of a person exercising a public function [soliciting, accepting or receiving], directly or through the interposition of persons, an offer, a promise or an advantage of any nature, for themselves or for a third party, to adopt one of the behaviors referred to in article 247.

###### § 2. Active corruption consists in the act of proposing, directly or through the

interposition of persons, to a person exercising a public function an offer, a promise or an advantage of any nature, for themselves or for a third party, so that they adopt one of the behaviors referred to in article 247.

###### § 3. Assimilated to a person exercising a public function within the meaning of

this article is any person who has presented themselves as a candidate for such a function, who makes it believed that they will exercise such a function, or who, by using false credentials, makes it believed that they exercise such a function.

(1)<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 21, 114; En vigueur : 29-02-2016>

###### Art. 247.

<L 1999-02-10/39, art. 4, 023; En vigueur : 02-04-1999> § 1er. Where the corruption is for the purpose of the accomplishment by the person exercising a public function of an act of their function, lawful but not subject to remuneration, the penalty shall be imprisonment of six months to one year and a fine of 100 [euros] to 10,000 [euros] or one of these penalties <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-
Where, in the case provided for in the preceding paragraph, the solicitation referred to in article 246, § 1er, is followed by a proposal referred to in article 246, § 2, as well as in the case where the proposal referred to in article 246, § 2, is accepted, the penalty shall be imprisonment of six months to two years and a fine of 100 [euros] to 25,000 [euros] or one of these penalties <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-

###### § 2. Where the corruption is for the purpose of the accomplishment by the person

exercising a public function of an unlawful act in the course of the exercise of their function or the abstention from performing an act that was within the scope of their duties, the penalty shall be imprisonment of six months to two years and a fine of 100 [euros] to 25,000 [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>
Where, in the case provided for in the preceding paragraph, the solicitation referred to in article 246, § 1er, is followed by a proposal referred to in article 246, § 2, as well as in the case where the proposal referred to in article 246, § 2, is accepted, the penalty shall be imprisonment of six months to three years and a fine of 100 [euros] to 50,000 [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>.

In the case where the corrupted person has accomplished the unlawful act or has abstained from performing an act that was within the scope of their duties, they shall be punished by imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of 100 [euros] to 75,000 [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>.

###### § 3. Where the corruption is for the purpose of the accomplishment by the person

exercising a public function of a crime or an offence in the course of the exercise of their function, the penalty shall be imprisonment of six months to three years and a fine of 100 [euros] to 50,000 [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-
Where, in the case provided for in the preceding paragraph, the solicitation referred to in article 246, § 1er, is followed by a proposal referred to in article 246, § 2, as well as in the case where the proposal referred to in article 246, § 2, is accepted, the penalty shall be imprisonment of two years to five years and a fine of 500 [euros] to 100,000 [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>.

###### § 4. Where the corruption has as its object the use by the person exercising a

public function of the real or supposed influence which they have by virtue of their function, in order to obtain an act of a public authority or public administration or the abstention from such an act, the penalty shall be imprisonment from six months to one year and a fine from 100 [euros] to 10,000 [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.

Where, in the case provided for in the preceding paragraph, the solicitation referred to in Article 246, § 1er, is followed by a proposal referred to in Article 246, § 2, as well as in the case where the proposal referred to in Article 246, § 2, is accepted, the penalty shall be imprisonment from six months to two years and a fine from 100 [euros] to 25,000 [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.

If the corrupted person has actually used the influence which they had by virtue of their function, they shall be punished by imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine from 100 [euros] to 50,000 [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 248.

<L 1999-02-10/39, art. 4, 023; In force: 02-04-1999> Where the acts provided for in Articles 246 and 247, §§ 1er to 3, concern a police officer, a person vested with the capacity of judicial police officer or a member of the public prosecution service, the maximum penalty is raised to double the maximum penalty provided for by Article 247 for the acts.

###### Art. 249.

<L 1999-02-10/39, art. 4, 023; In force: 02-04-1999> § 1er. Where the corruption provided for in Article 246 concerns an arbitrator and has as its object an act within their jurisdictional function, the penalty shall be imprisonment from one year to three years and a fine from 100 [euros] to 50,000 [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

Where, in the case provided for in the preceding paragraph, the solicitation referred to in Article 246, § 1er, is followed by a proposal referred to in Article 246, § 2, as well as in the case where the proposal referred to in Article 246, § 2, is accepted, the penalty shall be imprisonment from two years to five years and a fine from 500 [euros] to 10,000 [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.

###### § 2. Where the corruption provided for in Article 246 concerns a lay judge or

a juror and has as its object an act within their jurisdictional function, the penalty shall be imprisonment from two years to five years and a fine from 500 [euros] to 100,000 [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.

Where, in the case provided for in the preceding paragraph, the solicitation referred to in Article 246, § 1er, is followed by a proposal referred to in Article 246, § 2, as well as in the case where the proposal referred to in Article 246, § 2, is accepted, the penalty shall be imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine from 500 [euros] to 100,000 [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.

###### § 3. Where the corruption provided for in Article 246 concerns a judge and has as its object

an act within their jurisdictional function, the penalty shall be imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine from 500 [euros] to 100,000 [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42,

###### art. 2

, In force: 01-01-2002>.

Where, in the case provided for in the preceding paragraph, the solicitation referred to in Article 246, § 1er, is followed by a proposal referred to in Article 246, § 2, as well as in the case where the proposal referred to in Article 246, § 2, is accepted, the penalty shall be imprisonment from ten years to fifteen years and a fine from 500 [euros] to 100,000 [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>.

###### Art. 250.[ Where the corruption provided for by Articles 246 to 249 concerns a

person exercising a public function in a foreign State or in an organization governed by public international law, the minimum of the fine penalties is tripled and the maximum of the fine penalties is quintupled.]

(1)<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 22, 114; In force: 29-02-2016>

###### Art. 251. (Repealed)

<L 2007-05-11/42, art. 6, 066; In force: 08-06/2007>

###### Art. 252.

<L 1999-02-10/39, art. 4, 023; In force: 02-04-1999> Without prejudice to the application of Articles 31 and 32, persons punished pursuant to the provisions of this chapter may also be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with Article 33.

###### Art. 253. (Repealed)

<L 1999-02-10/39, art. 4, 023; In force: 02-04-1999>

##### CHAPITRE V. - ABUSE OF AUTHORITY.

###### Art. 254.Shall be punished by imprisonment from one year to five years, any

public official, government agent or employee, of whatever status or rank they may be, who shall have requisitioned or ordered, caused to be requisitioned or ordered the action or deployment of public force against the execution of a law or royal decree, or against the collection of a legally established tax, or against the execution of either an ordinance or warrant of justice, or any other order emanating from authority. The guilty party may be sentenced, in addition, to disqualification from the rights mentioned in the first three numbers of [Article 31, paragraph 1er]. (1)<L 2009-04-14/01, art. 14, 073; In force: 15-04-2009>

###### Art. 255. (See NOTE under TITLE) If such requisition or order has been followed

through, the guilty party shall be sentenced to detention from five years to ten years.

###### Art. 256. If the orders or requisitions have been the direct cause of other crimes

punishable by stronger penalties than those expressed in Articles 254 and 255, these stronger penalties shall be applied to the officials, agents or employees guilty of having given the said orders or made the said requisitions. (Nevertheless, the penalty of life imprisonment shall be replaced, in this case, by that of imprisonment from twenty years to thirty years.) <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 52, 040; In force: 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 257. Where a public official or officer, an administrator, government or

police agent or employee, an executor of warrants of justice or judgments, a commander-in-chief or subordinate commander of public force, shall have, without legitimate reason, used or caused to be used violence against persons, in the exercise of or on the occasion of the exercise of their functions, the minimum penalty imposed for such acts shall be increased in accordance with Article 266.

###### Art. 258. Any judge, any administrator or member of an administrative body, who,

under whatever pretext, even that of the silence or obscurity of the law, shall have denied to render the justice which they owe to the parties, shall be punished by a fine from two hundred [euros] to five hundred [euros], and may be sentenced to disqualification from the right to perform public functions, employments or offices. <L 2000-06-26/42,

###### art. 2

, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 259. Any commander, any officer or non-commissioned officer of the public force,

who, after having been legally requisitioned by the civil authority, shall have refused to deploy the force placed under his orders, shall be punished by imprisonment of fifteen days to three months.

CHAPTER Vbis. - [ On the interception, disclosure and recording of communications not accessible to the public and of data from a computer system]

(1)<L 2016-12-25/37, art. 29, 122; En vigueur : 27-01-2017>

Art. 259bis.<Inséré par L 1994-06-30/49, art. 1; En vigueur : 03-02-1995> § 1. Shall be punished [ by imprisonment of six months to three years] and a fine of five hundred [euros] to twenty thousand [euros] or by only one of these penalties, any officer or public official, depositary or agent of the public force who, in the exercise of his functions, outside the cases provided for by law or without complying with the formalities it prescribes: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01- 1° [ either, intentionally, by means of any device, intercepts or causes to be intercepted, takes cognizance or causes cognizance to be taken, records or causes to be recorded communications not accessible to the public, in which he does not take part, without the consent of all the participants in these communications;]

2° either, with the intention of committing one of the offences mentioned above, installs or causes to be installed any device;

3° [ either, knowingly, possesses, reveals or discloses to another person the content of communications not accessible to the public or of data from a computer system illegally intercepted or recorded, or of which he has taken cognizance illegally, or knowingly uses in any manner information obtained in this way.]

###### § 2. Shall be punished [ by imprisonment of six months to five years] and a fine

of five hundred [euros] to thirty thousand [euros] or by only one of these penalties, any officer or public official, depositary or agent of the public force who, in the exercise of his functions, outside the cases provided for by law or without complying with the formalities it prescribes, with fraudulent intent or with intent to harm, uses a recording, lawfully made, [ of communications not accessible to the public or of data from a computer system] . <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

[§ 2bis. Shall be punished [ by imprisonment of six months to three years] and a fine of five hundred euros to twenty thousand euros or by only one of these penalties, any officer or public official, depositary or agent of the public force who, in the exercise of his functions, outside the cases provided for by law or without complying with the formalities it prescribes, unduly, possesses, produces, sells, obtains for use, imports, distributes or makes available in another form a device, including computer data, primarily designed or adapted to enable the commission of the offence provided for in § 1.] <L 2006-05-15/46, art. 2, 1°, 059; En vigueur : 22-09-2006>

###### § 3. The attempt to commit one of the offences referred to in [§§ 1, 2 or 2bis]

vigueur : 22-09-2006>

###### § 4. The penalties [provided for in §§ 1 to 3] are doubled if an offence against one of

these provisions is committed within five years from the pronouncement of a judgment or decision, having become final and binding, imposing a conviction for one of these offences or one of the offences referred to [in article 314bis, §§ 1 to 3]. <L 2006-05-15/46, art. 2, 3°, 059; En vigueur : 22-09-2006>

###### § 5. [ ...]

(1)<L 2010-02-04/26, art. 38, 079; En vigueur : 01-09-2010> (2)<L 2016-12-25/37, art. 30, 122; En vigueur : 27-01-2017> (3)<L 2017-03-30/11, art. 83, 123; En vigueur : 08-05-2017> (4)<L 2017-07-06/24, art. 212, 124; En vigueur : 03-08-2017>

PROVISION COMMON TO THE PRECEDING CHAPTERS.

###### Art. 260. When a public official or officer, a depositary or agent of the

public force, shall have ordered or done any act contrary to a law or to a royal decree, if he proves that he acted by order of his superiors, for matters within their jurisdiction and on which hierarchical obedience was owed to them, he shall be exempt from the penalty, which shall, in this case, be applied only to the superiors who gave the order.

##### CHAPTER VI. - ON THE ILLEGALLY

ANTICIPATED OR PROLONGED EXERCISE OF PUBLIC AUTHORITY.

###### Art. 261. Any public official who shall have entered into the exercise of his functions,

without having taken the oath prescribed by law, shall be sentenced to a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-

###### Art. 262. Any public official revoked, dismissed, suspended or prohibited

lawfully, who, after having had official knowledge thereof, shall have continued the exercise of his functions, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to one year and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros].

Shall be punished by the same penalties any elective or temporary public official who shall have continued to exercise his functions, after their lawful cessation. <L 2000-06- 26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

##### CHAPTER VII. - ON CERTAIN OFFENCES RELATING TO THE KEEPING OF CIVIL STATUS

RECORDS.

###### Art. 263.

<L 31-03-1987, art. 88> Shall be punished by a fine of twenty-six [euros] to three hundred [euros], the civil status officer who shall have contravened one of the provisions [ of title 2 of book I] of the Civil Code. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>
