(1)<L 2018-06-18/03, art. 99, 130; En vigueur : 31-03-2019>

###### Art. 264.

<L 31-03-1987, art. 89> Shall be punished by a fine of one hundred [euros] to five hundred [euros], the civil status officer or the agent specially delegated by him who shall have contravened one of the provisions of [ article 29, § 1,] of the Civil Code. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

(1)<L 2018-06-18/03, art. 100, 130; En vigueur : 31-03-2019>

###### Art. 265.

<L 31-03-1987, art. 90> Shall be punished by a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros], the civil status officer who shall have proceeded with the celebration of a marriage without having ensured the required consents. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

SPECIAL PROVISION.

###### Art. 266. (Voir NOTE sous TITRE) Outside the case where the law specially regulates the

penalties incurred for crimes or offences committed by public officials or officers, those among them who shall have rendered themselves guilty of other crimes or other offences which they were charged with preventing, establishing, prosecuting or suppressing, shall be sentenced to the penalties attached to these crimes or these offences, the minimum of which shall be doubled, if it concerns imprisonment, and increased by two years, if it concerns (imprisonment for fifteen years to twenty years or a lesser term). <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 53, 040; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

##### CHAPTER VIII. - ON OFFENCES COMMITTED BY MINISTERS OF RELIGION

IN THE EXERCISE OF THEIR MINISTRY.

###### Art. 267. (Shall be punished by a fine of fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros], any

minister of a religion who shall proceed with the nuptial blessing before the celebration of the civil marriage.) <L 03-08-1909, art. unique> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur (This provision shall not be applicable when one of the persons who received the nuptial blessing was in danger of death, and any delay brought to this ceremony could have had the effect of making it impossible.) <L 03-08-1909, art. unique>

In case of a new offence of the same kind, he may, in addition, be sentenced to imprisonment of eight days to three months.

###### Art. 268. Shall be punished with imprisonment from eight days to three months and a

fine from twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros], ministers of a religious denomination who, in the exercise of their ministry, through speeches delivered in a public assembly, shall have directly attacked the government, a law, a royal decree or any other act of public authority. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-

#### TITLE V. - OF CRIMES AND OFFENCES AGAINST PUBLIC ORDER COMMITTED BY

PRIVATE INDIVIDUALS.

##### CHAPTER I. - OF REBELLION.

###### Art. 269. Is qualified as rebellion, any attack, any resistance with violence

or threats against ministerial officers, field guards or forest guards, depositaries or agents of the public force, persons responsible for the collection of taxes and contributions, bearers of orders for payment, customs officers, sequestrators, officers or agents of the administrative or judicial police, acting for the execution of laws, orders or ordinances of the public authority, warrants or judgments.

###### Art. 270. (Repealed)

<L 13-10-1930, art. 31>

###### Art. 271. Rebellion committed by a single person, armed, shall be

punished with imprisonment from three months to two years; if it took place without weapons, with imprisonment from eight days to six months.

###### Art. 272. (See NOTE under TITLE) If the rebellion was committed by several

persons, and as a result of prior agreement, the rebels, bearing arms, shall be sentenced to (imprisonment of five years to ten years), and the others to imprisonment from one year to five years. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 54, 040; En vigueur If the rebellion was not the result of prior agreement, the guilty parties who were armed shall be punished with imprisonment from one year to five years, and the others, with imprisonment from three months to two years.

###### Art. 273. In case of rebellion with a band or gathering, article 134 of the

present Code shall be applicable to rebels without functions or employment in the band, who withdrew at the first warning from the public authority, or even since, if they were seized outside the place of the rebellion, without fresh resistance and without arms.

###### Art. 274. In all cases where imprisonment is imposed for an act of rebellion, the

guilty parties may be sentenced, in addition, to a fine from twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

The leaders of the rebellion and those who incited it may, moreover, be sentenced (...) to disqualification, in accordance with article 33. <L 09-04-1930, art.

##### CHAPTER II. - OF INSULTS AND VIOLENCE AGAINST MINISTERS, MEMBERS OF THE

LEGISLATIVE CHAMBERS, DEPOSITARIES OF AUTHORITY OR OF THE PUBLIC FORCE.

###### Art. 275.(Shall be punished with imprisonment from fifteen days to six months and a

fine from fifty to three hundred [euros], anyone who shall have insulted by acts, words, gestures or threats, a member of the Legislative Chambers in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of his mandate, a Minister (, a member of the

[ Constitutional Court] ) or a (magistrate of the administrative order or a member of the judiciary) or an officer of the public force on active service, in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of their functions.) <L 27-07-1934, art. 1> <L 10-10-1967, art. 139, § 5> <L 02-02-1984, art. 6> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

If the insult took place at the sitting of one of the Chambers or at the hearing of a court or tribunal, the imprisonment shall be from two months to two years, and the fine from two hundred [euros] to one thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01- Insults addressed to a member of the Chambers may not, except in the case of flagrante delicto, be prosecuted except on the complaint of the insulted person or on the denunciation of the Chamber of which he is a member.

(1)<L 2010-02-21/03, art. 6, 078; En vigueur : 08-03-2010>

###### Art. 276. Insult by words, acts, gestures or threats, directed, in the exercise

or on the occasion of the exercise of their functions, against a ministerial officer, an agent depositary of authority or of the public force, or against any other person having a public character, shall be punished with imprisonment from eight days to one month and a fine from twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06- 26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 277. Insults committed against constituted bodies shall be punished in the

same manner as insults committed against members of those bodies, according to the distinctions established in the two preceding articles.

###### Art. 278.(Shall be punished with imprisonment from two months to two years and a

fine from fifty to five hundred [euros], anyone who shall have struck a member of the Legislative Chambers in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of his mandate, a Minister, (a member of the [ Constitutional Court] ,) a magistrate or an officer of the public force on active service, in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of their functions.) <L 27-07-1934, art. 2> <L 02-02-1984, art. 7> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

If the blows were struck at the sitting of one of the Chambers or at the hearing of a court or tribunal, the guilty party shall be punished with imprisonment from three months to three years and a fine from two hundred [euros] to one thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06- 26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

(1)<L 2010-02-21/03, art. 7, 078; En vigueur : 08-03-2010>

###### Art. 279. If the blows struck caused bloodshed, wounds or

illness, the guilty party shall be sentenced to imprisonment from six months to five years and a fine from two hundred [euros] to fifteen hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06- 26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

Art. 279bis. <inséré par L 2006-12-20/41, art. 2; En vigueur : 22-02-2007> When the blows struck without intention to cause death have nevertheless caused it, the guilty party shall be punished with imprisonment of seven years to ten years.

He shall be punished with imprisonment of twelve years to fifteen years if he committed these acts of violence with premeditation.

###### Art. 280.[ If the crime or offence was committed against a ministerial officer, an

agent depositary of authority or of the public force, or against any other person having a public character, in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of his functions, the penalties shall be as follows:

1° in the cases referred to in article 398, paragraph 1, the penalties shall be imprisonment from one month to one year and a fine from fifty euros to three hundred euros;

2° in the cases referred to in article 398, paragraph 2, the penalties shall be imprisonment from two months to two years and a fine from fifty euros to three hundred euros;

3° in the cases referred to in article 399, paragraph 1, the penalties shall be imprisonment from four months to four years and a fine from one hundred euros to five hundred euros;

4° in the cases referred to in article 399, paragraph 2, the penalties shall be imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine from one hundred euros to five hundred euros;

5° in the cases referred to in article 400, paragraph 1, the penalty shall be imprisonment of five years to ten years;

6° in the cases referred to in article 400, paragraph 2, the penalty shall be imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years;

7° in the cases referred to in article 401, paragraph 1, the penalty shall be imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years;

8° in the cases referred to in article 401, paragraph 2, the penalty shall be imprisonment of fifteen years to twenty years.]

(1)<L 2010-03-08/08, art. 2, 080; En vigueur : 09-04-2010>

###### Art. 281.

<Abrogé par L 2010-03-08/08, art. 3, 080; En vigueur : 09-04-2010> Art. 281bis.

<Abrogé par L 2010-03-08/08, art. 4, 080; En vigueur : 09-04-2010> Art. 281ter.

<Abrogé par L 2010-03-08/08, art. 5, 080; En vigueur : 09-04-2010>

###### Art. 282. The penalties provided by articles 275, 278 and 279 shall be applicable

in the case where insults have been made to or jurors have been struck by reason of their functions, or witnesses by reason of their testimony.

##### CHAPITRE III. - BREAKING OF SEALS.

###### Art. 283. When seals, affixed by order of the public authority, have

been broken, the guardians shall be punished, for simple negligence, with imprisonment from eight days to six months.

###### Art. 284. Those who have intentionally broken seals shall be punished with

imprisonment from six months to two years, and if it is the guardian himself or the public official who ordered or carried out the affixing, he shall be punished with imprisonment from one year to three years.

The attempt of this offence shall be punished, in the first case of this article, with imprisonment from three months to one year, and, in the second case, with imprisonment from six months to two years.

###### Art. 285.

<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 55, 040; In force: 13-03-2003> If the broken seals were affixed on papers or effects of an individual charged, accused or indicted for a crime carrying life imprisonment or life detention, imprisonment from twenty years to thirty years or detention from twenty years to thirty years, or of an individual sentenced to one of these penalties, the negligent guardian shall be punished with imprisonment from three months to one year.

###### Art. 286. Whoever has intentionally broken seals affixed on papers or

effects of the nature set forth in the preceding article, shall be punished with imprisonment from one year to three years, and if it is the guardian himself or the public official who ordered the affixing, the guilty party shall be punished with imprisonment from two years to five years.

The attempt of this offence shall be punished, in the first case provided for by this article, with imprisonment from six months to two years, and, in the second case, with the same penalty from one year to three years.

###### Art. 287. If the breaking of seals is committed with violence against persons, the

guilty party shall be punished with imprisonment from two years to five years.

The attempt of this breaking of seals shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to three years.

###### Art. 288. In the cases of articles 284, 286 and 287, the guilty party may, in addition,

be sentenced to a fine from fifty [euros] to two thousand [euros]. <L 2000- 06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

##### CHAPITRE IV. - OBSTACLES TO THE EXECUTION OF PUBLIC

WORKS.

###### Art. 289. Whoever, by acts of violence, has opposed the execution of works

ordered or authorized by the competent authority, shall be punished with imprisonment from eight days to three months.

###### Art. 290. Those who, by unlawful assembly and violence, acts of violence or threats, have

opposed the execution of these works, shall be sentenced to imprisonment from three months to two years.

The leaders or instigators shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to three years.

###### Art. 291. In the cases provided for in the two preceding articles, the guilty parties

may, in addition, be sentenced to a fine from twenty-six [euros] to five hundred

[euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

##### CHAPITRE V. - CRIMES AND OFFENCES OF SUPPLIERS.

###### Art. 292. (See NOTE under TITLE) Persons responsible for supplies,

enterprises or administrations on behalf of the army or navy, who have voluntarily caused the service with which they are charged to fail, shall be punished with (imprisonment from five years to ten years) and a fine from two hundred [euros] to three thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002> <L 2003-01- 23/42, art. 56, 040; In force: 13-03-2003>

The same penalties shall be applied to the agents of suppliers, if these agents have voluntarily caused the service to fail.

###### Art. 293. (See NOTE under TITLE) Public officials or agents

appointed or employed by the government, who have incited or assisted the guilty parties in causing the service to fail, shall be sentenced to imprisonment (from seven years to ten years), and to a fine from three hundred [euros] to three thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002> <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 57, 040; In force: 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 294. When the cessation of service is the result of negligence on the

part of suppliers, their agents, public officials or agents, appointees or employees of the government, the guilty parties shall be punished with imprisonment from three months to two years and a fine from one hundred [euros] to one thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 295. Although the service has not failed, if deliveries or works have

been voluntarily delayed, the guilty parties shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to two years and a fine from two hundred [euros] to one thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

They shall be punished with imprisonment from one month to one year and a fine from fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros], if the delay is the result of negligence. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 296. In the various cases provided for by articles 294 and 295, § 2, prosecution

may only be brought upon denunciation by the minister concerned by the matter.

###### Art. 297. If there has been fraud concerning the nature, quality or quantity of works or

labor or things supplied, the guilty parties shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine from one hundred [euros] to ten thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

They may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article

###### 33. Art. 298. Public officials or agents appointed or employed by the

government, who have participated in this fraud, shall be punished with imprisonment from two years to five years and a fine from two hundred [euros] to ten thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002> They shall, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33.

##### CHAPITRE VI. - PUBLICATION OR DISTRIBUTION OF WRITINGS WITHOUT

INDICATION OF THE NAME AND ADDRESS OF THE AUTHOR OR PRINTER.

###### Art. 299. Any person who has knowingly contributed to the publication or

distribution of any printed matter in which the true indication of the name and address of the author or printer is not found, shall be punished with imprisonment from eight days to two months and a fine from twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros], or with only one of these penalties. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

However, imprisonment may not be pronounced when the printed matter, published without the required indications, forms part of a publication whose origin is known by its prior appearance.

###### Art. 300. Shall be exempted from the penalty provided by the preceding article:

Those who have disclosed the printer;

Criers, bill-posters, vendors or distributors who have disclosed the person from whom they received the printed writing.

##### CHAPITRE VII. - VIOLATIONS OF LAWS AND REGULATIONS ON LOTTERIES,

GAMING HOUSES AND PAWNSHOPS.

###### Art. 301. Are deemed lotteries, all operations offered to the public and intended

to procure a gain by way of chance.

###### Art. 302. The originators, entrepreneurs, administrators, appointees or agents of

legally unauthorized lotteries, shall be punished with imprisonment from eight days to three months and a fine from fifty [euros] to three thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

Shall be confiscated the movable objects put up in the lottery, and those which are used or intended for its operation.

When immovable property has been put up in a lottery, confiscation shall not be pronounced; it shall be replaced by a fine from one hundred [euros] to ten thousand [euros]. <L 2000- 06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 303. Shall be punished with imprisonment from eight days to one month and a

fine from twenty-six [euros] to one thousand [euros], or with only one of these penalties: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

Those who have placed, peddled or distributed tickets of lotteries not legally authorized;

Those who, by notices, announcements, posters or by any other means of publication, have made known the existence of these lotteries or facilitated the issue of their tickets.

In all cases, the tickets, as well as the notices, announcements or posters, shall be seized and destroyed.

###### Art. 304. Shall be exempt from the penalties provided for by the preceding article, the criers and

bill-posters who have made known the person from whom they hold the tickets or the writings mentioned above.

###### Art. 305. (Repealed)

<L 1999-05-07/77, art. 73, 027; En vigueur : 30-12-2000>

###### Art. 306. Those who, without legal authorization, have operated pawnshops or

pledge houses, shall be punished with imprisonment from eight days to three months and a fine from twenty-six [euros] to one thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 307. Those who, having an authorization, have not kept a register

in conformity with the regulations, containing consecutively, without any blank spaces or interlineation, the sums or objects lent, the names, domiciles and professions of the borrowers, the nature, quality, value of the objects placed as security, shall be punished with imprisonment from eight days to one month and a fine from twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros], or with only one of these penalties. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 308. Shall be punished with imprisonment from eight days to three months and a

fine from twenty-six [euros] to one thousand [euros]: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur Individuals who have habitually taken effects to the offices of the pawnshop for others and for remuneration;

Those who have habitually purchased acknowledgments from the pawnshop; Those who have transferred or purchased acknowledgments from these establishments, evidencing loans on new goods.

##### CHAPTER VIII. - OFFENCES RELATING TO INDUSTRY, COMMERCE AND

PUBLIC AUCTIONS.

###### Art. 309. Anyone who has maliciously or fraudulently communicated

secrets of the factory in which he has been or is still employed, shall be punished with imprisonment from three months to three years and a fine from fifty [euros] to two thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 310. (Repealed)

<L 24-05-1921, art. unique>

###### Art. 311. Persons who, by any fraudulent means, have brought about

the rise or fall of the price of foodstuffs or goods or of public papers and securities, shall be punished with imprisonment from one month to two years and a fine from three hundred [euros] to ten thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 312.Any commander of military divisions, provinces or fortified places

and towns, any governor or district commissioner who has, in the area where he has the right to exercise his authority, engaged in similar maneuvers or participated therein, either openly or by simulated acts or through the interposition of persons, shall incur, independently of the penalties pronounced by the preceding article, the prohibition of the rights set out in the first three numbers of [article 31, paragraph 1].

(1)<L 2009-04-14/01, art. 15, 073; En vigueur : 15-04-2009>

###### Art. 313. Those who, by assembly and by violence or threats, have

disturbed public order in the markets or grain halls, with the intention of provoking pillage or only to force the sellers to part with their goods at a price lower than that which would result from free competition, shall be punished with imprisonment from three months to two years.

The leaders or instigators shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to three years (...). <L 09-04-1930, art. 32>

###### Art. 314.

<L 1993-12-24/37, art. 66, 011; En vigueur : 01-05-1997> Persons who, in the adjudications of ownership, usufruct or lease of movable or immovable property, of an enterprise, a supply, an operation or any service, have obstructed or disturbed the freedom of bids or tenders, by violence or by threats, by gifts or promises or by any other fraudulent means, shall be punished with imprisonment from fifteen days to six months and a fine from one hundred [euros] to three thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002> CHAPTER VIIIbis. - [Offences relating to the secrecy of communications not accessible to the public and data from a computer system]

(1)<L 2016-12-25/37, art. 31, 122; En vigueur : 27-01-2017>

Art. 314bis.<Inséré par L 1994-06-30/49, art. 2; En vigueur : 03-02-1995> § 1. Shall be punished [with imprisonment from six months to two years] and a fine from two hundred [euros] to ten thousand [euros] or with only one of these penalties, anyone who: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

1° [either, intentionally, by means of any device, intercepts or causes to be intercepted, takes cognizance or causes cognizance to be taken, records or causes to be recorded communications not accessible to the public, in which he does not take part, without the consent of all participants in such communications;]

2° either, with the intention of committing one of the offences mentioned above, installs or causes to be installed any device whatsoever.

###### § 2. [Shall be punished [with imprisonment from six months to three years] and a

fine from five hundred euros to twenty thousand euros or with only one of these penalties, anyone who knowingly possesses, reveals or discloses to another person the content of communications not accessible to the public or data from a computer system illegally intercepted or recorded, or of which he has become aware illegally, or knowingly uses in any manner information obtained in this way.

Shall be punished with the same penalties anyone who, with fraudulent intent or with the purpose of causing harm, uses a recording, legally made, of communications not accessible to the public or data from a computer system.]

###### (§ 2

bis. Shall be punished [with imprisonment from six months to two years] and a fine from two hundred euros to ten thousand euros or with only one of these penalties, anyone who, unduly, possesses, produces, sells, obtains for use, imports, distributes or makes available in another form a device, including computer data, primarily designed or adapted to enable the commission of the offence provided for in § 1.) <L 2006-05-15/46, art. 3, 1°, 059; En vigueur : 22-09-2006>

###### § 3. The attempt to commit one of the offences referred to in (§§ 1, 2 or 2bis)

vigueur : 22-09-2006>

###### § 4. The penalties (provided for in §§ 1 to 3) are doubled if an offence against one of

these provisions is committed within five years from the pronouncement of a judgment or decision, having acquired the force of res judicata, imposing a conviction by reason of one of these offences or one of the offences referred to (in article 259bis, §§ 1 to 3). <L 2006-05-15/46, art. 3, 3°, 059; En vigueur : 22-09-2006> (1)<L 2016-12-25/37, art. 32, 122; En vigueur : 27-01-2017>

(2)<L 2017-07-06/24, art. 213, 124; En vigueur : 03-08-2017>

##### CHAPITRE IX. - ON CERTAIN OTHER OFFENCES AGAINST PUBLIC ORDER.

###### SECTION I. - ON OFFENCES AGAINST THE LAWS ON BURIALS.

###### Art. 315. Shall be punished by eight days to two months' imprisonment, or a

fine of twenty-six [euros] to three hundred [euros]: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

Those who, without the prior authorization of the public officer, shall have proceeded or caused to proceed with a burial.

Those who shall have contravened, in any manner whatsoever, the regulations relating to places of burial and hasty burials.

###### SECTION II. - (ON OBSTACLES TO THE EXERCISE OF THE JUDICIAL FUNCTION).

<L 10-10-1967, art. 140>

###### Art. 316.

<L 10-10-1967, art. 140> Shall be punished by a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros] anyone who abstains from responding to inquiries ordered by the authority for the purpose of establishing jury lists or who, in order to be exempted from performing the function of juror, makes an inaccurate declaration. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

Art. 316bis. <L 10-10-1967, art. 140> Shall be punished by a fine of fifty [euros] to one thousand [euros]: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002> 1° the juror not exempted who does not appear at the assize court on the day and at the time indicated for the opening of proceedings, upon the summons that has been served on him or upon the notice he has received;

2° the juror who, after having complied with the summons or notice, withdraws without the authorization of the president before the expiration of his functions.

<L 03-03-1933, art. 28>

#### TITLE VI. - ON CRIMES AND OFFENCES AGAINST PUBLIC SECURITY.

##### CHAPTER I. - (ON THE ASSOCIATION FORMED FOR THE PURPOSE OF COMMITTING ATTEMPTS AGAINST

PERSONS OR PROPERTY and on CRIMINAL ORGANIZATION). <L 1999- 01-10/49, art. 2, 022; En vigueur : 08-03-1999>

###### Art. 322. Any association formed for the purpose of committing attempts against persons or

property is a crime or an offence, which exists by the mere fact of the organization of the band.

###### Art. 323. (If the association has had for its purpose the perpetration of crimes carrying the

punishment of imprisonment for life or imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years or a greater term, the instigators of this association, the leaders of this band and those who shall have exercised any command therein, shall be punished by imprisonment of five years to ten years.) <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 58, 040; En vigueur : 13-
They shall be punished by imprisonment of two years to five years, if the association was formed to commit other crimes, and by imprisonment of six months to three years, if the association was formed to commit offences.

###### Art. 324. All other individuals forming part of the association and those who shall have

knowingly and voluntarily supplied to the band or its divisions weapons, ammunition, instruments of crime, lodgings, shelter or meeting place, shall be punished:

In the first case provided for by the preceding article, by imprisonment of six months to five years;

In the second case, by imprisonment of two months to three years;

And in the third, by imprisonment of one month to two years.

Art. 324bis. (inserted by <L 1999-01-10/49, art. 3, En vigueur : 08-03-1999>) Constitutes a criminal organization the structured association of more than two persons, established over time, with a view to committing in a concerted manner, crimes and offences punishable by imprisonment of three years or a more serious penalty, to obtain, directly or indirectly, patrimonial advantages, (...). <L 2005-08-10/61, art. 4, 053; En vigueur : 12-09-2005>
An organization whose real object is exclusively of a political, trade union, philanthropic, philosophical or religious nature or which exclusively pursues any other legitimate purpose cannot, as such, be considered a criminal organization within the meaning of paragraph 1.

Art. 324ter. (inserted by <L 1999-01-10/49, art. 3, En vigueur : 08-03-1999>)

###### § 1er.

(When the criminal organization uses intimidation, threat, violence, fraudulent manoeuvres or corruption or resorts to commercial or other structures to conceal or facilitate the commission of offences, any person who, knowingly and voluntarily, forms part thereof, is punished by imprisonment of one year to three years and a fine of one hundred euros to five thousand euros or one of these penalties only, even if he does not intend to commit an offence within the framework of this organization nor to associate himself therewith in one of the manners provided for by articles 66 to 69.) <L 2005-08-10/61, art. 5, 053; En vigueur : 12-09-2005>

###### § 2. Any person who participates in the preparation or realization of any

lawful activity of this criminal organization, while knowing that his participation contributes to its objectives, as provided for in article 324bis, is punished by imprisonment of one year to three years and a fine of one hundred [euros] to five thousand [euros] or one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06- 26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### § 3. Any person who participates in any decision-making within the framework of

activities of the criminal organization, while knowing that his participation contributes to its objectives, as provided for in article 324bis, is punished by imprisonment of five years to ten years and a fine of five hundred [euros] to one hundred thousand [euros] or one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### § 4. Any leader of the criminal organization is punished by imprisonment of ten years

to fifteen years and a fine of one thousand [euros] to two hundred thousand [euros] or one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 325. The guilty parties convicted, pursuant to articles 323(, 324 and 324ter), to

the penalty of imprisonment, may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33 (...). <L 09-04-1930, art. 32> <L 1999-01-10/49, art. 4, 022; En vigueur : 08-03-1999>

###### Art. 326. Shall be exempted from the penalties pronounced by this chapter, those

of the guilty parties who, before any attempt at crimes or offences forming the object of the association and before any proceedings commenced shall have revealed to the authority the existence of these bands and the names of their commanders in chief or subordinate.

(Paragraph 2 repealed) <L 09-04-1930, art. 32>

##### CHAPTER II. - (ON THREATS OF ATTEMPT AGAINST PERSONS OR AGAINST

PROPERTY, AND FALSE INFORMATION RELATING TO SERIOUS

ATTEMPTS). <L 04-07-1972, art. 5>

###### Art. 327.

<L 04-07-1972, art. 1> Whoever shall have, either verbally or by anonymous or signed writing, with order or under condition, threatened an attempt against persons or property, punishable by a criminal penalty, shall be punished by imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of one hundred [euros] to five hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

The threat by anonymous or signed writing of an attempt against persons or property punishable by a criminal penalty, not accompanied by order or condition, shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to two years and a fine of fifty [euros] to three hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 328.

<L 04-07-1972, art. 2> Whoever, either verbally or in anonymous or signed writing, (or by any action,) has knowingly given false information concerning the existence of a danger of an attack against persons or property punishable by a criminal penalty, shall be punished by imprisonment from three months to two years and by a fine of fifty [euros] to three hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002> <L 2003- 04-04/59, art. 2, 042; In force: 15-05-2003>

Art. 328bis. <Inserted by L 2003-04-04/59, art. 3; In force: 15-05-2003> Whoever has disseminated, in any manner whatsoever, substances which, presenting no danger in themselves, give the impression of being dangerous, and which he knows or ought to know can inspire serious fears of an attack against persons or property, punishable by imprisonment of at least two years, shall be punished by imprisonment from three months to two years and by a fine of fifty euros to three hundred euros.

###### Art. 329.

<L 04-07-1972, art. 3> The threat by gestures or emblems of an attack against persons or property, punishable by a criminal penalty, shall be punished by imprisonment from eight days to three months and by a fine of twenty-six [euros] to one hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 330.

<L 04-07-1972, art. 4> The threat, made either verbally or in anonymous or signed writing, with order or under condition, of an attack against persons or property, punishable by imprisonment of at least three months, shall be punished by imprisonment from eight days to three months and by a fine of twenty-six [euros] to one hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force Art. 330bis. [ In the cases referred to in articles 327 to 330, the minimum penalties provided for by those articles shall be doubled where the person to whom the threats of attack are addressed or to whom false information relating to an attack is given is a person whose situation of vulnerability by reason of age, state of pregnancy, illness, infirmity or physical or mental deficiency was apparent or known to the perpetrator.] (1)<Inserted by L 2011-11-26/19, art. 3, 084; In force: 02-02-2012>

###### Art. 331. In the cases provided for by article 327, the guilty party may, in addition, be

sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33 (...). <L 09-04-1930, art. Art. 331bis.(See NOTE under TITLE) <L 17-04-1986, art. 1> Shall be punished by (imprisonment of five years to ten years): <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 59, 040; In force: 13- 1° [ any person who, with the intent to cause death or serious injury to others or substantial damage to property or the environment, threatens to use radioactive materials or devices or threatens to commit an act directed against a nuclear installation or to disrupt the operation of such an installation;] 2° any person who threatens to commit a theft of nuclear materials in order to compel a natural or legal person, an international organization or a State to do or refrain from doing an act.

(3° any person who threatens to use biological or chemical weapons or products to commit an attack against persons, property, legal persons, international organizations or a State.) <L 2003-04-04/59, art. 4, 042; In force: 15-05-2003>

(1)<L 2013-05-23/09, art. 2, 093; In force: 16-06-2013>

##### CHAPITRE III. - ESCAPE OF DETAINEES.

###### Art. 332. In case of escape of detainees, the persons responsible for their escort

or custody shall be punished as follows.

###### Art. 333.

<L 29-08-1945, art. 1> If the escaped person was being prosecuted or sentenced for an offence, if he was a prisoner of war, or if he was detained at the disposal of the Minister of Justice, these officers shall be punished, in case of negligence, by imprisonment from eight days to three months, and, in case of connivance, by imprisonment from six months to two years.

The same penalties shall apply in the case of escape of any individual interned by application of the Social Defence Act of 9 April 1930.

###### Art. 334.If the escaped person was being prosecuted or sentenced for a crime, or if he was

arrested under the law on extraditions [ under the law on extraditions] , these officers shall undergo imprisonment from fifteen days to one year, in case of negligence, and imprisonment from one year to five years, in case of connivance.

(1)<L 2015-07-20/19, art. 2, 113; In force: 05-09-2015>

###### Art. 335. Those who, not being responsible for the custody or escort of the detainee,

have procured or facilitated his escape, shall be punished, in the case of article 333, by imprisonment from fifteen days to one year, and, in the case of article 334, by imprisonment from three months to two years.

(Paragraph 2 repealed) <L 1993-06-29/31, art. 1, 010; In force: 05-09-1993>

###### Art. 336. If the escape has taken place or has been attempted with violence, threats or prison-breaking,

the penalties against those who have favoured it by providing instruments suitable for effecting it shall be:

In the circumstances stated in article 333, imprisonment from two years to five years against the officers, and from three months to two years against other persons;

In the circumstances stated in article 334, (imprisonment of five years to ten years) against the officers, and imprisonment from six months to three years against other persons. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 60, 040; In force: 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 337. (See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 61, 040; In force: 13- 03-2003> If the escape has taken place or has been attempted with violence, threats or prison-breaking, the penalties against those who have favoured it by transmission of weapons shall be:

In the circumstances stated in article 333, imprisonment of five years to ten years against the officers, and imprisonment from two years to five years against other persons.

In the circumstances stated in article 334, imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years against the officers, imprisonment of five years to ten years against other persons.

##### CHAPITRE IV. - BREAKING OF BANISHMENT AND CERTAIN CONCEALMENTS.

###### Art. 338. (Repealed)

<L 09-04-1930, art. 32>

###### Art. 339. Those who have concealed or caused to be concealed persons whom they knew to be

prosecuted or sentenced for a crime, shall be punished by imprisonment from eight days to two years and by a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2,In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 340. Whoever has concealed or caused to be concealed, hidden or caused to be hidden the corpse

of a person who has been murdered or died as a result of blows or wounds, shall be punished by imprisonment from three months to two years, and by a fine of fifty [euros] to six hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 341. Excepted from the two preceding provisions are the ascendants or

descendants, spouses even if divorced, brothers or sisters, and relatives in the same degrees of the concealed criminals, of the perpetrators or accomplices of the homicide, blows or wounds.

TITLE VIbis. - (CRIMES RELATING TO HOSTAGE-TAKING). <L 02-07-1975, art. Art. 347bis.<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 4, 029; In force: 27-03-2001> § 1er. Hostage-taking consists of the arrest, detention or abduction of persons in order to secure compliance with an order or condition, such as to prepare or facilitate the commission of a crime or offence, to facilitate flight, escape, to obtain the release or ensure the impunity of the perpetrators or accomplices of a crime or offence.

###### § 2. Hostage-taking shall be punished by imprisonment for twenty to thirty years.

[ The penalty shall be life imprisonment if the person taken hostage is a minor or a person whose situation of vulnerability due to age, pregnancy, illness, infirmity or physical or mental disability was apparent or known to the perpetrator.]

###### § 3. Except in the cases referred to in § 4, the penalty shall be imprisonment for fifteen to twenty

years if within five days of the arrest, detention or abduction, the person taken hostage was voluntarily released without the order or condition having been carried out.

###### § 4. The penalty shall be life imprisonment in the following cases:

1° if the arrest, detention or abduction of the person taken hostage has caused either an apparently incurable illness, or [ an incapacity for personal work of more than four months] , or the complete loss of use of an organ, or serious mutilation, or death;

2° (if the person taken hostage has been subjected to the acts referred to in Article 417ter, first paragraph.) <L 2002-06-14/42, art. 2, 036; In force: 24-08-2002> (1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 4, 084; In force: 02-02-2012>

(2)<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 23, 114; In force: 29-02-2016>

##### CHAPTER III. - CRIMES AND OFFENCES TENDING TO PREVENT OR DESTROY THE

PROOF OF THE CIVIL STATUS OF THE CHILD.

###### Art. 361.

<L 30-03-1984, art. 4> Shall be punished with imprisonment of eight days to three months and a fine of 26 [euros] to 200 [euros], or one of these penalties only: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

1° any person who, required to declare the birth of a child by virtue of [ Article 43, § 1er,] of the Civil Code, has not made the declaration [ as provided in that Article] ;

2° any person who, required to inform the civil registrar of a birth by virtue of [ Article 42,] of the Civil Code, has not given notice in accordance with those provisions.

(1)<L 2018-06-18/03, art. 101, 130; In force: 31-03-2019>

###### Art. 362.

[ Shall be punished with the penalties referred to in the preceding Article, any person who, having found a newborn child, has not immediately informed the public emergency services, as prescribed by Article 45 of the Civil Code.] This provision shall not apply to anyone who has consented to take charge of the child and who has made a declaration to that effect before the municipal authority of the place where the child was found.

(1)<L 2018-06-18/03, art. 102, 130; In force: 31-03-2019>

###### Art. 363.

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 5, 029; In force: 27-03-2001> Shall be punished with imprisonment for five to ten years anyone who substitutes one child for another or who attributes to a woman a child she has not delivered.

Shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to five years anyone who destroys the proof of the civil status of a child or prevents its establishment.

The same penalty shall apply to those who have given the task of committing the acts mentioned in the preceding paragraphs, if that task has been carried out.
