##### CHAPTER V. - [ Voyeurism, indecent assault and rape]

(1)<L 2016-02-01/09, art. 7, 115; In force: 29-02-2016>

###### Art. 371

/1. [ Shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to five years whoever shall have:

1° observed or had observed a person or shall have made or had made a visual or audio recording of them,

- directly or by technical or other means,

- without the authorization of that person or without their knowledge,

- while that person was nude or engaged in explicit sexual activity, and

- while they were in circumstances where they could reasonably consider that their privacy would not be violated; 2° shown, made accessible or disseminated the visual or audio recording of a person who is nude or engaged in explicit sexual activity, without their consent or without their knowledge, even if that person consented to its creation.

If these acts have been committed on the person or with the assistance of the person of a minor over sixteen years of age, the offender shall be subject to imprisonment from five years to ten years.

The sentence shall be imprisonment from ten years to fifteen years, if the minor was under sixteen years of age.

Voyeurism exists as soon as there is a commencement of execution.]

(1)<Inséré par L 2016-02-01/09, art. 8, 115; En vigueur : 29-02-2016>

###### Art. 372.(Voir NOTE sous TITRE)

<L 15-05-1912, art. 48> Any indecent assault committed without violence or threats on the person or with the assistance of the person of a child of either sex, under sixteen years of age, shall be punished with imprisonment (from five years to ten years). <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 6, 029; En vigueur (Shall be punished with imprisonment from ten to fifteen years the indecent assault committed, without violence or threats, by any ascendant or adopter on the person or with the assistance of the person of a minor, even over sixteen years of age [ ...] . (The same penalty shall be applied if the offender is either the brother or sister of the minor victim or any person who occupies a similar position within the family, or any person habitually or occasionally cohabiting with them and who has authority over them.) <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 6, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> (1)<L 2016-05-31/02, art. 3, 118; En vigueur : 18-06-2016>

Art. 372bis. (Repealed) <L 18-06-1985, art. 1>

###### Art. 373.(Voir NOTE sous TITRE)

<L 15-05-1912, art. 49> [ Shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to five years, indecent assault committed on persons or with the assistance of persons of either sex, with violence, coercion, threat, surprise or deception, or which has been made possible due to an infirmity or physical or mental deficiency of the victim.]

If the assault has been committed on the person [ or with the assistance of the person] of a minor over sixteen years of age, the offender shall be subject to imprisonment (from five years to ten years). <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 7, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001>

The sentence shall be (imprisonment) from ten to fifteen years, if the minor was under sixteen years of age. <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 7, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> (1)<L 2016-02-01/09, art. 9, 115; En vigueur : 29-02-2016>

###### Art. 374. The assault exists as soon as there is a commencement of execution.

###### Art. 375.(Voir NOTE sous TITRE) (Any act of sexual penetration, of any

nature whatsoever and by any means whatsoever, committed on a person who does not consent to it, constitutes the crime of rape.

There is no consent in particular when the act has been imposed by violence, coercion [ , threat, surprise] or deception, or has been made possible due to an infirmity or physical or mental deficiency of the victim.) <L 04-07-1989,

###### art. 1, 1°>

(Whoever shall have committed the crime of rape shall be punished with imprisonment from five years to ten years.) <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 8, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001>

(If the crime has been committed on the person of a minor over sixteen years of age, the offender shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from ten to fifteen years.) <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 8, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001>

(If the crime has been committed on the person of a child over fourteen years of age and under sixteen years of age, the offender shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from fifteen to twenty years.) <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 8, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001>

(Any act of sexual penetration, of any nature whatsoever and by any means whatsoever, committed on the person of a child who has not reached the age of fourteen years, is deemed to be rape with violence. In this case, the penalty shall be imprisonment from fifteen to twenty years.) <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 8, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001>

(It shall be imprisonment from twenty years to thirty years if the child was under ten years of age.) <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 8, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> (1)<L 2016-02-01/09, art. 10, 115; En vigueur : 29-02-2016>

###### Art. 376.(Voir NOTE sous TITRE)

<L 04-07-1989, art. 2> If the rape or indecent assault has caused the death of the person on whom it was committed, the offender shall be punished (with imprisonment from twenty years to thirty years). <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 9, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001>

(If the rape or indecent assault has been preceded or accompanied by the acts referred to in Article 417ter, first paragraph, or by unlawful confinement, the offender shall be punished with imprisonment from fifteen years to twenty years.) <L 2002-06-14/42, art. 3, 036; En vigueur If the rape or indecent assault has been committed either on a person [ whose situation of vulnerability due to age, a state of pregnancy, an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental deficiency was apparent or known to the perpetrator] , or under the threat of a weapon or an object resembling one, the offender shall be punished (with imprisonment) from ten to fifteen years. <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 9, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001>

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 5, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

###### Art. 377.(See NOTE under TITLE) [ Penalties shall be fixed as provided in

paragraphs 2 to 6:

- if the offender is the ancestor or adoptive parent of the victim, a direct descendant of the victim or a direct descendant of a brother or sister of the victim;
- if the offender is either the brother or sister of the minor victim or any person who occupies a similar position within the family, or any person habitually or occasionally cohabiting with the victim and who has authority over the victim;
- if the offender is one of those who have authority over the victim; if he has abused the authority or facilities conferred upon him by his functions; if he is a physician, surgeon, midwife or health officer and the child or any other vulnerable person referred to in article 376, paragraph 3, was entrusted to his care;
- if in the case [ of articles 371/1, 373, 375 and 376] , the offender, whoever he may be, was assisted in the execution of the crime or offence by one or more persons.]

(In the cases provided for by [ paragraph 2 of article 371/1, by paragraph 1 of article 372, and by paragraph 2 of article 373] , the penalty shall be imprisonment for ten to fifteen years.) <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 10, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001>

(...) <L 18-06-1985, art. 2>

([ In the cases provided for by paragraph 1 of article 371/1 and by paragraph 1 of article 373] , the minimum imprisonment shall be doubled.

(In the cases provided for by [ paragraph 3 of article 371/1, by paragraph 3 of article 373] , by paragraph 4 of article 375 and by paragraph 3 of article 376, the penalty of imprisonment shall be at least twelve years;) <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 10, 029; En vigueur In the case provided for by [ paragraph 3 of article 375] , the penalty of imprisonment shall be at least seven years.

(In the cases provided for by paragraphs 5 and 6 of article 375 and by paragraph 2 of article 376, the penalty of imprisonment shall be at least seventeen years.) <L 2000-11- 28/35, art. 10, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001>

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 6, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

(2)<L 2016-02-01/09, art. 11, 115; En vigueur : 29-02-2016>

Art. 377bis.<L 2007-05-10/35, art. 33, 064; En vigueur : 09-06-2007> In the cases provided for by this chapter, the minimum penalties laid down by these articles may be doubled if it concerns imprisonment, and increased by two years if it concerns imprisonment, where one of the motives for the crime or offence is hatred, contempt or hostility towards a person on the grounds of their alleged race, skin colour, ancestry, national or ethnic origin, nationality, sex, sexual orientation, marital status, birth, age, fortune, religious or philosophical conviction, current or future state of health, disability, language, political conviction, [ trade union conviction,] physical or genetic characteristic or social origin.

(1)<L 2009-12-30/01, art. 109, 074; En vigueur : 31-12-2009>

Art. 377ter. [ In the cases provided for by this chapter or by chapters VI and VII of this title, the minimum penalties laid down by the articles concerned is doubled if it concerns imprisonment, and increased by two years if it concerns imprisonment, where the crime or offence was committed against a minor under the age of sixteen and where, prior to this crime or offence, the perpetrator had solicited this minor with the intention of subsequently committing the acts referred to in this chapter or in chapters VI and VII of this title. In the cases referred to in article 377, paragraphs 4 to 6, the increase in the minimum penalty provided for in paragraph 1 is limited in such a way that, combined with the increase in penalties provided for in article 377bis, it does not exceed the maximum penalty provided for.]

(1)<Inséré par L 2014-04-10/24, art. 2, 102; En vigueur : 10-05-2014>

Art. 377quater. [ An adult who, through information and communication technologies, proposes a meeting to a minor under the age of sixteen with the intention of committing an offence referred to in this chapter or in chapters VI and VII of this title, shall be punished by imprisonment of one to five years, if this proposal was followed by material acts leading to said meeting.]

(1)<Inséré par L 2014-04-10/24, art. 3, 102; En vigueur : 10-05-2014>

###### Art. 378.

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 11, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> In the cases provided for by this chapter, offenders shall be sentenced to the prohibition of the rights set out in [ article 31, paragraph 1] .

[ The courts may also prohibit the convicted person, for a fixed term or permanently, from operating directly or indirectly a rest home, a home, a manor or any collective accommodation facility for persons referred to in article 376, paragraph 3, or from being a member, as a voluntary member, statutory or contractual staff member or as a member of the administrative and management bodies, of any institution or association whose activity mainly concerns vulnerable persons as referred to in article 376, paragraph 3. The application of this prohibition shall be in accordance with article (1)<L 2009-04-14/01, art. 16, 073; En vigueur : 15-04-2009>

(2)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 7, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

Art. 378bis. <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 12, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> The publication and dissemination by book, press, cinematography, radio, television or by any other means, of texts, drawings, photographs, images of any kind or sound messages likely to reveal the identity of the victim of an offence referred to in this chapter are prohibited, unless the latter has given written consent or the King's Prosecutor or the investigating magistrate has given consent for the purposes of information or investigation.

Offences against this article are punishable by imprisonment of two months to two years and a fine of three hundred [euros] to three thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-

##### CHAPTER VI. - (CORRUPTION OF YOUTH AND PROSTITUTION). <L

26-05-1914, art. 4>

###### Art. 379. (See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 1995-04-13/32, art. 2, 014; En vigueur : 05- 05-1995> Whoever has committed an offence against morals by inciting, favouring or facilitating, in order to satisfy the passions of others, the debauchery, corruption or prostitution of a minor of either sex, shall be punished by imprisonment (of five to ten years) and a fine of five hundred [euros] to twenty-five thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06- 26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002> <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 13, 029; En vigueur He shall be punished (by imprisonment) of ten to fifteen years and a fine of five hundred [euros] to fifty thousand [euros] if the minor has not reached the age of sixteen. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002> <L 2000-11- 28/35, art. 13, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001>

(The penalty shall be imprisonment of fifteen to twenty years and a fine of one thousand [euros] to one hundred thousand [euros], if the minor has not reached the age of fourteen.) <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002> <L 2000-11-28/35,

###### art. 13

, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001>

###### Art. 380.(See NOTE under TITLE)

(Formerly art. 380bis.)

###### § 1. Shall be punished by

imprisonment from one to five years and a fine from five hundred [euros] to twenty-five thousand [euros]:<L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002> 1° anyone who, in order to satisfy the passions of others, shall have recruited, enticed, diverted or retained, with a view to debauchery or prostitution, even with their consent, an adult (...);

2° anyone who shall have operated a house of debauchery or prostitution;

3° anyone who shall have sold, rented or made available for the purposes of prostitution rooms or any other premises with the aim of realizing an abnormal profit; 4° anyone who shall have, in any manner whatsoever, exploited the debauchery or prostitution of another.

###### § 2. The attempt to commit the offenses referred to in § 1 shall be punished by

imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine from one hundred [euros] to five thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### § 3. Shall be punished (by criminal detention) from ten to fifteen years and a fine from

five hundred [euros] to fifty thousand [euros], the offenses referred to in § 1, to the extent that their perpetrator: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002> <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 14, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>

1° makes use, directly or indirectly, of fraudulent maneuvers, violence, threats or any form of coercion;

2° or abuses the [situation of vulnerability in which a person finds themselves due to their illegal or precarious administrative situation, their age, a state of pregnancy, an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental deficiency].

###### § 4. Shall be punished (by criminal detention) from ten to fifteen years and a fine from one thousand

[euros] to one hundred thousand [euros]: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002> <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 14, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>

1° anyone who, in order to satisfy the passions of others, shall have recruited, enticed, diverted or retained, either directly or through an intermediary, a minor (...), even with their consent, with a view to debauchery or prostitution; <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002> <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 14, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>

2° anyone who shall have operated, either directly or through an intermediary, a house of debauchery or prostitution where minors engage in prostitution or debauchery;

3° anyone who shall have sold, rented or made available to a minor, for the purposes of debauchery or prostitution, rooms or any other premises with the aim of realizing an abnormal profit;

4° anyone who shall have exploited, in any manner whatsoever, the debauchery or prostitution of a minor (...). <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 14, 029; In force: 27-03- (5° anyone who shall have obtained by the delivery, offer or promise of a material or financial advantage, the debauchery or prostitution of a minor.) <L 2000-11- 28/35, art. 14, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>

###### § 5. (The offenses referred to in § 4 shall be punished by criminal detention from fifteen to

twenty years and a fine from one thousand [euros] to one hundred thousand [euros] if they are committed against a minor under sixteen years of age.) <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002> <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 14, 029; In force: 27-03-

###### (§ 6. Anyone who shall have attended [, live, including by means of information

and communication technologies,] the debauchery or prostitution of a minor shall be punished by imprisonment from one month to two years and a fine from one hundred [euros] to two thousand [euros].) <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 14, 029; In force: 27-03-2001> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

[ § 7. The fine shall be applied as many times as there are victims.]

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 8, 084; In force: 02-02-2012>

(2)<L 2013-06-24/24, art. 2, 097; In force: 02-08-2013>

(3)<L 2016-05-31/02, art. 4, 118; In force: 18-06-2016>

Art. 380bis. <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 15, 029; In force: 27-03-2001> (Previously art. 380quater) Shall be punished by imprisonment from eight days to three months and a fine from twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros], anyone who, in a public place shall have by words, gestures or signs incited a person to debauchery. The penalty shall be doubled if the offense was committed against a minor. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

Art. 380ter. <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 16, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>

(Previously art. 380quinquies)

###### § 1. Shall be punished by imprisonment from two

months to two years and a fine from two hundred [euros] to two thousand [euros], anyone who, by whatever means, makes or has made, publishes, distributes or disseminates advertising, directly or indirectly, even by concealing its nature under linguistic artifices, for an offer of services of a sexual nature, when such advertising is specifically addressed to minors or when it refers to services offered either by minors or by persons purported to be such. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

The penalty shall be imprisonment from three months to three years and a fine from three hundred [euros] to three thousand [euros] when the advertising referred to in article 1 has as its purpose or effect, direct or indirect, to facilitate the prostitution or debauchery of a minor or their exploitation for sexual purposes. <L 2000-06-26/42,

###### art. 2

, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### § 2. Shall be punished by imprisonment from one month to one year and a fine from one hundred

[euros] to one thousand [euros], anyone who, by whatever means, makes or has made, publishes, distributes or disseminates advertising, directly or indirectly, even by concealing its nature under linguistic artifices, for an offer of services of a sexual nature, when such services are provided by means of telecommunication. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### § 3. In cases not covered by §§ 1 and 2, shall be punished by

imprisonment from one month to one year and a fine from one hundred [euros] to one thousand

[euros], anyone who shall have, by any means of advertising, even by concealing the nature of their offer or request under linguistic artifices, made known that they engage in prostitution, that they facilitate the prostitution of another or that they wish to enter into relations with a person engaging in debauchery. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

Shall be punished by the same penalties, anyone who, by any means of advertising, shall incite, by the allusion made therein, the exploitation of minors or adults for sexual purposes, or shall use such advertising in connection with an offer of services.

Art. 380quater. (repealed) <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 15, 029; In force: 27-03- Art. 380quinquies. (repealed) <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 16, 029; In force: 27-03-

###### Art. 381.

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 17, 029; In force: 27-03-2001> The offenses referred to in articles 379 and 380, §§ 3 and 4, shall be punished by criminal detention from fifteen to twenty years and a fine from one thousand [euros] to one hundred thousand [euros] and the offenses referred to in article 380, § 5, shall be punished by criminal detention from seventeen to twenty years and a fine from one thousand [euros] to one hundred thousand [euros], if they constitute acts of participation in the principal or accessory activity of an association, whether or not the offender has the status of leader. <L 2000-06- 26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

Art. 381bis. (repealed) <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 17, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>

###### Art. 382.

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 18, 029; In force: 27-03-2001> § 1. In the cases referred to in articles 379 and 380, the offenders shall, in addition, be sentenced to the prohibition of the rights set out in [article 31, paragraph 1].

###### § 2. The courts may prohibit persons convicted of an

offense provided for in article 380, §§ 1 to 3, for a term of one year to three years, from operating, either by themselves or through an intermediary, a drinking establishment, an employment agency, an entertainment business, a visual media rental or sales agency, a hotel, a furnished rental agency, a travel agency, a matrimonial brokerage business, an adoption institution, an establishment entrusted with the care of minors, a business that provides transport for students and youth groups, a leisure or vacation establishment, or any establishment offering bodily or psychological care, or from being employed therein in any capacity whatsoever.

In case of a second conviction for an offense provided for in article 380, §§ 1 to 3, the prohibition may be pronounced for a term of one year to twenty years. In case of a conviction for an offense provided for in articles 379 and 380, §§ 4 and 5, the prohibition may be pronounced for a term of one to twenty years.

###### § 3. Without regard to the capacity of the natural or legal person of

the operator, owner, tenant or manager, the court may order the closure of the establishment in which the offenses were committed, for a period of one month to three years.

Where the convicted person is neither the owner, nor the operator, nor the tenant, nor the manager of the establishment, closure may only be ordered if the gravity of the concrete circumstances so requires, and this, for a period of no more than two years, after summons at the request of the public prosecutor, the owner, the operator, the tenant or the manager of the establishment.

The summons before the court is transcribed [at the competent office of the General Administration of Patrimonial Documentation] of the location of the property at the instance of the bailiff who executed the writ.

The summons must contain the cadastral designation of the property concerned and identify the owner in the form and under the sanction provided for in article 12 of the law of 10 October 1913 introducing amendments to the mortgage law and the law on forced expropriation and regulating anew the organization of the conservation of mortgages.

Any decision rendered in the case is mentioned in the margin of the transcription of the summons according to the procedure provided for by article 84 of the mortgage law. The clerk sends [to the competent office of the General Administration of Patrimonial Documentation] the extracts and the declaration that no appeal has been filed.

###### § 4. Article 389 is applicable to this provision.

(1)<L 2009-04-14/01, art. 17, 073; En vigueur : 15-04-2009>

(2)<L 2018-07-11/07, art. 68, 133; En vigueur : 30-07-2018>

Art. 382bis.<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 20, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> Without prejudice to the application of article 382, any conviction for acts referred to [in articles 371/1] to 377, [377quater,] 379 to 380ter, 381 and 383 to 387, committed against a minor or involving his or her participation, may include, for a period of one year to twenty years, the prohibition of the right:

1° to participate, in any capacity whatsoever, in teaching provided in a public or private establishment that receives minors;

2° to be part of, as a volunteer member, statutory or contractual staff member, or as a member of the administrative and management bodies, any legal person or de facto association whose activity principally concerns minors;

3° to be assigned to an activity that places the convicted person in a relationship of trust or authority vis-à-vis minors, as a volunteer member, statutory or contractual staff member or as a member of the administrative and management bodies, of any legal person or de facto association.

[4° to inhabit, reside or be present in the designated determined zone designated by the competent judge. The imposition of this measure must be specially motivated and take into account the gravity of the facts and the reintegration capacity of the convicted person.]

Article 389 is applicable to this provision.

(1)<L 2012-12-14/52, art. 5, 092; En vigueur : 02-05-2013>

(2)<L 2014-04-10/24, art. 4, 102; En vigueur : 10-05-2014>

(3)<L 2016-02-01/09, art. 12, 115; En vigueur : 29-02-2016>

Art. 382ter.[Special confiscation referred to in article 42, 1°, is applied even if the ownership of the things on which it bears does not belong to the convicted person, without this confiscation being able, however, to prejudice the rights of third parties on the property liable to be confiscated. It must also be applied, in the same circumstances, to movable property, to the part thereof, to immovable property, to the room or to any other space.

It may also be applied to the counter-value of these movables or immovables alienated between the commission of the offense and the final judicial decision.

In case of seizure of immovable property, it is proceeded in accordance with the formalities of article 35bis of the Code of Criminal Instruction.]

(1)<L 2013-11-27/05, art. 3, 099; En vigueur : 01-03-2014>

Art. 382quater.[Where an offender who is convicted for acts referred to [in articles 371/1] to 377, [377quater,] 379 to 380ter and 381 is in contact, by reason of his or her status or profession, with minors and an employer, a legal person or an authority exercising disciplinary power is known, the judge may order the transmission of the criminal part of the operative part of the judicial decision to that employer, legal person or disciplinary power. This measure is taken either ex officio, or at the request of the civil party or the public prosecutor in a specially motivated judicial decision by reason of the gravity of the facts, the reintegration capacity or the risk of recidivism.] (1)<Inséré par L 2012-12-14/52, art. 4, 092; En vigueur : 02-05-2013>

(2)<L 2014-04-10/24, art. 5, 102; En vigueur : 10-05-2014>

(3)<L 2016-02-01/09, art. 13, 115; En vigueur : 29-02-2016>

Art. 382quinquies. [The publication and dissemination of texts, drawings, photographs, images of any kind or sound messages likely to reveal the identity of the victim of an offense referred to in this chapter, are prohibited and punished in accordance with article 378bis, unless the latter has given his or her written consent or the public prosecutor or the magistrate in charge of the investigation has given his or her consent for the purposes of information or investigation.]

(1)<Inséré par L 2016-05-31/02, art. 5, 118; En vigueur : 18-06-2016>

##### CHAPTER VII. - PUBLIC OFFENCES AGAINST MORALITY.

###### Art. 383.Whoever shall have exposed, sold or distributed songs, pamphlets or

other writings, printed or not, figures or images contrary to morality, shall be sentenced to imprisonment from eight days to six months and to a fine from twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

(Whoever shall have sung, read, recited, made heard or uttered obscenities in the public meetings or places referred to in § 2 of article 444 shall be punished with the same penalties.) <L 29-01-1905, art. 1>

(Shall be punished with the same penalties:

Whoever shall have, for the purpose of trade or distribution, manufactured, held, imported or caused to be imported, transported or caused to be transported, handed over to a transport or distribution agent, announced by any means of advertising, songs, pamphlets, writings, figures or images contrary to morality;) <L 14-06-1926, art. 1>

Whoever shall have exposed, sold or distributed emblems or objects contrary to morality, shall have, for the purpose of trade or distribution, manufactured or held them, imported or caused to be imported them, transported or caused to be transported them, handed over to a transport or distribution agent, announced by any means of advertising.) <L 14-06-1926, art. 1>

(Paragraphs 8 to 10 repealed) <L 09-07-1973, art. unique>

(1)<L 2018-10-15/03, art. 7, 136; En vigueur : 08-11-2018>

Art. 383bis.§ 1. [ Without prejudice to the application of articles 379 and 380, whoever shall have without right exposed, offered, sold, rented, transmitted, supplied, distributed, disseminated, or made available, or handed over pedopornographic material or shall have produced, imported or caused to be imported it, shall be punished with imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine from five hundred euros to ten thousand euros.]

###### 2 §

2. [ Whoever shall have knowingly and without right acquired, possessed pedopornographic material or shall have, knowingly, accessed it by means of information and communication technologies, shall be punished with imprisonment from one month to one year and a fine from one hundred euros to one thousand euros.]

###### § 3. The offence referred to under § 1, shall be punished (with imprisonment) from ten years to

fifteen years and a fine from five hundred [euros] to fifty thousand [euros], if it constitutes an act of participation in the main or accessory activity of an association, whether or not the offender has the capacity of a leader. <L 2000-11-

###### 2 §

4. [ For the application of this article, "pedopornographic material" means: either, a minor engaging in sexually explicit conduct, real or simulated, or representing the sexual organs of a minor for principally sexual purposes; or, a person who appears to be a minor engaging in sexually explicit conduct, real or simulated, or representing the sexual organs of that person, for principally sexual purposes;

3° realistic images representing a minor who does not exist, engaging in sexually explicit conduct, or representing the sexual organs of that minor for principally sexual purposes.]

###### 2 §

5. [ Articles 382, 382ter, 382quater, 382quinquies and 389 apply to the offences referred to in paragraphs 1 to 3.]

(1)<L 2011-11-30/28, art. 7, 083; En vigueur : 30-01-2012>

(2)<L 2016-05-31/02, art. 6, 118; En vigueur : 18-06-2016>

Art. 383bis/1. [ An organization approved by the King may as of right receive reports relating to images likely to be covered by article 383bis, analyze their content and origin, and transmit them to the police services and judicial authorities.

For this purpose, this organization performs the mission entrusted to it, according to the modalities set by the King and relating more particularly to:

1° the obligation to be a member of an international association of Internet hotlines fighting against child pornography;

2° the transmission of the aforementioned reports to the police services and judicial authorities;

3° the transmission of the aforementioned reports which relate to images hosted abroad, to the aforementioned international association;

4° the supervision of persons responsible for receiving reports, analyzing their content and origin and their transmission, and of those persons responsible for supervising these tasks within the organization, in application of the law of 11 December 1998 relating to classification and security clearances, certificates and opinions;

5° the annual transmission of an activity report to the Minister of Justice; 6° the prohibition to constitute a database from the images which have been reported to it.

The King determines the procedure for granting and withdrawing approval.] (1)<Inséré par L 2016-05-31/02, art. 7, 118; En vigueur : 18-06-2016>

###### Art. 384.

<L 14-06-1926, art. 2> (In the cases referred to in article 383), the author of the writing, figure, image or object, shall be punished with imprisonment from one month to one year and a fine from fifty [euros] to one thousand [euros]. <L 2000-11-

###### Art. 385. Whoever shall have publicly outraged morals by actions which

offend decency, shall be punished with imprisonment from eight days to one year and a fine from twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

(If the outrage has been committed in the presence of a minor under sixteen years of age, the penalty shall be imprisonment from one month to three years and a fine from one hundred [euros] to one thousand [euros].) <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 23, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 386.

<L 28-07-1962, art. 2> If the offences provided for in article 383 have been committed against minors, the imprisonment shall be from six months to two years and the fine from one thousand [euros] to five thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01- In the same case and without prejudice to the application of paragraph 2 of article 385, the penalties provided for in the first paragraph of that article may be doubled.

Art. 386bis. (repealed by change of numbering) <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 24, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001>

Art. 386ter. (repealed by change of numbering) L 2000-11-28/35, art. 24, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001>

###### Art. 387.

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 24, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001>

(Former article 386bis) Whoever sells or distributes to minors or exposes on the public highway or along it indecent images, figures or objects of a nature to disturb their imagination shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to two years and a fine from one thousand [euros] to five thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 388.

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 25, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> In the cases provided for in this chapter, the guilty parties may furthermore be sentenced to the prohibition of the rights set out in [ article 31, paragraph 1] .

In the event of conviction by application of articles 386, paragraph 1, or 387 and if the offense was committed in the operation of a bookshop, second-hand bookshop or photographic products business or of equipment necessary for the creation of any type of visual medium, or of an entertainment business, the closure of the establishment may be ordered for a period of one month to three months.

In the event of a second conviction for one of the acts referred to in paragraph 2, committed within three years from the first conviction, the closure may be ordered for a period of three months to six months.

In the event of a third conviction for the same acts, committed within five years from the second conviction, permanent closure may be ordered. In this latter case, the courts and tribunals may furthermore prohibit the convicted persons from operating, either themselves or through an intermediary, a bookshop, a second-hand bookshop, a photographic products business or of equipment necessary for the creation of any type of visual medium, an entertainment business or one or more of these businesses or from being employed therein in any capacity whatsoever.

Where the convicted person is neither owner, nor operator, nor tenant, nor manager of the establishment, closure may only be ordered if the seriousness of the concrete circumstances so requires. In this case, article 382, § 3, paragraphs 2 to 5, is applicable.

Article 389 is applicable to this provision.

(1)<L 2009-04-14/01, art. 18, 073; En vigueur : 15-04-2009>

###### Art. 389.

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 26, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> § 1. The duration of the prohibition pronounced in application of articles 378, 382, § 1, 382bis and 388, paragraph 1, shall run from the day of the suspended sentence or from the day on which the convicted person shall have served or prescribed his sentence of imprisonment not accompanied by a suspension and, in the event of early release, from the day of his release provided that it is not revoked.

However, the prohibition pronounced in application of article 382, § 2, shall take effect from the day on which the adversarial or default judgment shall have become irrevocable.

###### § 2. Any violation of the provision of the judgment or order pronouncing a

prohibition in application of the articles referred to in § 1 shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to six months and a fine of one hundred [euros] to one thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur

###### § 3. The closure pronounced in application of articles 382, § 3, and 388 shall take

effect from the day on which the adversarial or default judgment shall have become irrevocable.

###### § 4. Any violation of the provision of the judgment or order that orders the

closure of an establishment in application of the articles referred to in § 3 shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to three years and a fine of one thousand [euros] to five thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>
