marriage, shall have contracted another before the dissolution of the previous one, shall be punished by (imprisonment of five years to ten years). <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 64, 041; En vigueur

##### CHAPITRE IX. - FAMILY ABANDONMENT.

Art. 391bis.<L 05-07-1963, art. 1> Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to six months and a fine of fifty to five hundred [euros] or by one of these penalties only, without prejudice, where applicable, to the application of more severe criminal sanctions, any person who, having been sentenced by a judicial decision that can no longer be subject to opposition or appeal, to provide maintenance to his or her spouse, descendants or ascendants, shall have voluntarily remained more than two months without paying the installments. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

(Shall be punished by the same penalties, the non-performance under the conditions provided for in paragraph 1, of the obligations determined by articles 203bis, 206, 207, 301, 303, (...) 336 and (and 353-14 of the Civil Code) and articles 1288, 3° and 4°, (...) of the Judicial Code.) <L 31-03-1987, art. 93> <L 2003-04-24/32, art. 6; 045; En vigueur : 01- 09-2005> <L 2007-04-27/00, art. 40, 1°, 065; En vigueur : 01-09-2007>

The same penalties shall be applicable to the spouse who shall have voluntarily evaded, in whole or in part, the effects of the authorization given by the judge pursuant to (articles 203ter, 221 and (301, § 11) of the Civil Code, and [ 1253ter/5 and 6] , (...) of the Judicial Code) when it can no longer be subject to opposition or appeal. <L 31-03-1987, art. 93> <L 2007-04-27/00, art. 40, 2° et 3°, 065; En vigueur : 01-09-2007>

It shall be the same for the spouse who, sentenced either to one of the obligations the non-performance of which is sanctioned by the first two paragraphs of this article, or by application of (articles 203ter, 221 and (301, § 11) of the Civil Code, and

[ 1253ter/5 and 6] , (...) of the Judicial Code) voluntarily abstains from completing the formalities provided for by social legislation and thus deprives his or her spouse or children of the benefits to which they could claim. <L 31-03-1987, art. 93> <L 2007-04-27/00, art. 40, 2° et 3°, 065; En vigueur : 01-09-2007>

[ The same penalties shall be applicable to any descendant in direct line who, sentenced to a maintenance obligation, voluntarily abstains from completing the formalities provided for by social legislation and thus deprives an ascendant of the benefits to which he or she could claim.]

(The same penalties shall be applicable to any person who shall have voluntarily obstructed the guardianship over family benefits or other social allowances, by neglecting to provide the necessary documents to the bodies responsible for the payment of these allowances, by making false or incomplete declarations, or by modifying the allocation given to them by the person or authority designated in accordance with article 29 of the law of 8 April 1965 on the protection of youth (, on the care of minors who have committed an act qualified as an offense and on the reparation of the damage caused by this act)). <L 2005-08- 10/62, art. 2, 054 ; En vigueur : 02-09-2005> <L 2006-05-15/35, art. 21; 056; En vigueur : 16-10-2006>

In the event of a second conviction for one of the offenses provided for in this article, committed within five years from the first, the penalties may be doubled.

[ In the event of conviction for one of the offenses provided for in this article, the judge may also pronounce the disqualification from driving a motor vehicle, in accordance with articles 38 to 41 of the law of 16 March 1968 on road traffic policing.]

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 9, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

(2)<L 2014-05-12/07, art. 12, 107; En vigueur : 01-08-2014>

(3)<L 2013-07-30/23, art. 239, 108; En vigueur : 01-09-2014>

Art. 391ter. <L 05-07-1963, art. 2> When a person has remained more than two months without satisfying one of the obligations the non-performance of which is sanctioned by article 391bis, he or she may be summoned before the justice of the peace at the request of any interested person or the public prosecutor. The summons is made by means of a registered letter signed and sent by the clerk with acknowledgment of receipt.

The justice of the peace collects the explanations of the parties and draws up a full report thereof, which he transmits to the public prosecutor.

##### CHAPITRE X.

Offenses and misdemeanors in matters of adoption. <inséré par L 2003-04- 24/32, art. 7; En vigueur : indéterminée>

Art. 391quater. <inséré par L 2003-04-24/32, art. 7; En vigueur : 01-09-2005> Shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to one year and a fine of twenty-six euros to five hundred euros or by one of these penalties only, whoever shall have, with fraudulent intent, obtained or attempted to obtain for himself an adoption in contravention of the provisions of the law.

In the event of recidivism within three years following a judgment of conviction that has become final for an offense under the first paragraph, these penalties may be doubled.

Art. 391quinquies. <inséré par L 2003-04-24/32, art. 7; En vigueur : 01-09-2005> Shall be punished by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of five hundred euros to twenty-five thousand euros or by one of these penalties only, any person who has intervened as an intermediary by obtaining or attempting to obtain an adoption on behalf of another without being a member of an organization previously approved for this purpose by the competent community or who, being a member of an approved organization, has obtained or attempted to obtain for another an adoption in contravention of the provisions of the law.

##### CHAPITRE XI.

Forced marriage [ and forced legal cohabitation] . <L 2007- 04-25/76, art. 2; En vigueur : 25-06-2007>

(1)<L 2013-06-02/08, art. 13, 098; En vigueur : 03-10-2013>

Art. 391sexies.<L 2007-04-25/76, art. 2, 005; En vigueur : 25-06-2007> Any person who, by violence or threats, has forced someone to enter into marriage shall be punished by imprisonment [ of three months to five years and a fine of two hundred and fifty euros to five thousand euros] .

The attempt is punished by imprisonment [ of two months to three years and a fine of one hundred and twenty-five euros to two thousand five hundred euros] .

(1)<L 2013-06-02/08, art. 14, 098; En vigueur : 03-10-2013>

Art. 391septies. [ Any person who, by violence or threats, has forced someone to enter into legal cohabitation shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to five years and a fine of two hundred and fifty euros to five thousand euros.

The attempt is punished by imprisonment of two months to three years and a fine of one hundred and twenty-five euros to two thousand five hundred euros.]

(1)<Inséré par L 2013-06-02/08, art. 15, 098; En vigueur : 03-10-2013> Art. 391octies.[ § 1er. The judge who pronounces a conviction on the basis of articles 391sexies or 391septies or who finds guilt for an offense under these provisions, may also pronounce the nullity of the marriage or legal cohabitation, at the request of the public prosecutor or any party having an interest in the case.

###### § 2. A judgment is enforceable against the spouses or legal cohabitants only if they

have been parties or summoned to the case.

The public prosecutor may summon by forced intervention the spouse or spouses or the legal cohabitant or legal cohabitants who are not parties to the case. The intervention confers upon them the status of party to the case. These parties may exercise remedies.

The intervention is made from the beginning of the proceedings so that the parties can assert their rights on the annulment of the marriage or legal cohabitation.

###### § 3. Any service of a judgment or decision pronouncing the annulment of a

marriage or legal cohabitation is immediately communicated in copy by the executing bailiff to the clerk of the court that pronounced the decision.

###### 2 §

4. [ When the nullity of the marriage has been pronounced by a judgment or decision that has become final, the clerk immediately transmits the data of the judgment or decision to the BAEC, with indication of the date on which the decision became final.

The BAEC makes a notation on this basis and links it to the marriage certificate. The BAEC immediately notifies the Aliens Office of the judicial decision with indication of the date on which it became final. The clerk immediately informs the parties thereof.]

###### § 5. When the nullity of the legal cohabitation has been pronounced by a judgment

or decision that has become final, an extract containing the operative part of the judgment or decision and the indication of the day on which it became final is sent, without delay, by the clerk to the civil registrar of the place where the declaration of legal cohabitation was made and to the Aliens Office.

The clerk notifies the parties thereof.

The civil registrar notes without delay the annulment of the legal cohabitation in the population register.]

(1)<Inséré par L 2013-06-02/08, art. 16, 098; En vigueur : 03-10-2013>

(2)<L 2018-06-18/03, art. 103, 130; En vigueur : 31-03-2019>

#### TITRE VIII. - OFFENSES AND MISDEMEANORS AGAINST PERSONS.

##### CHAPITRE I. - (HOMICIDE AND VOLUNTARY BODILY HARM, TORTURE, INHUMAN

TREATMENT AND DEGRADING TREATMENT.) <L

2002-06-14/42, art. 4, 036; En vigueur : 24-08-2002>

###### Art. 392. Shall be qualified as voluntary, the homicide committed and the injuries caused with

the intent to attack the person of a determined individual, or of the one who shall be found or encountered, even if this intent were dependent on some circumstance or some condition, and even if the perpetrator was mistaken as to the person of the one who was the victim of the attack.

Art. 392bis. <L 31-03-1987, art. 94> For the application of this chapter, the words " father ", " mother " and " ascendant " also designate the adopters and, in the case of adoption and full adoption, the ascendants of the adopters.

###### SECTION I. - MURDER AND ITS VARIOUS FORMS.

###### Art. 393. (Voir NOTE sous TITRE) Homicide committed with intent to cause

death is qualified as murder. It shall be punished (by imprisonment of twenty years to thirty years).

<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 65, 041; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 394.

Murder committed with premeditation is qualified as assassination. It shall be punished (by life imprisonment). <L 1996-07-10/42, art. 15, 018; En vigueur : 11-

###### Art. 395.

<L 31-03-1987, art. 95> Is qualified as parricide and shall be punished (by life imprisonment), the murder of father, mother or other ascendants. <L 1996- 07-10/42, art. 15, 018; En vigueur : 11-08-1996>

###### Art. 396. Is qualified as infanticide, the murder committed on a child at the moment of

its birth or immediately thereafter.

Infanticide shall be punished, according to the circumstances, as murder or as assassination.

(Alinéas 3 et 4 abrogés) <L 12-07-1984, art. unique>

###### Art. 397. Is qualified as poisoning the murder committed by means of

substances that may cause death more or less promptly, in whatever manner such substances have been used or administered. It shall be punished (by life imprisonment). <L 1996-07-10/42, art. 15, 018; En vigueur : 11-08-

###### SECTION II. - VOLUNTARY HOMICIDE NOT QUALIFIED AS MURDER AND

VOLUNTARY BODILY HARM.

###### Art. 398. Whoever has voluntarily inflicted injuries or struck blows

shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to six months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to one hundred [euros], or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06- 26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

In the event of premeditation, the offender shall be sentenced to imprisonment of one month to one year and to a fine of fifty [euros] to two hundred [euros]. <L 2000- 06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 399. If the blows or injuries have caused an illness or an incapacity

for personal work, the offender shall be punished by imprisonment from two months to two years and a fine of fifty [euros] to two hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

The offender shall be punished by imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine of one hundred [euros] to five hundred [euros], if he acted with premeditation. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 400.(See NOTE under TITLE) The penalties shall be imprisonment from two

years to five years and a fine of two hundred [euros] to five hundred [euros], if the blows or injuries have resulted in either an apparently incurable illness, or [ incapacity for personal work of more than four months] , or the loss of absolute use of an organ, or a serious mutilation. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

The penalty shall be that of (imprisonment of five years to ten years), if there was premeditation. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 66, 041; In force: 13-03-2003> (1)<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 20, 114; In force: 29-02-2016>

###### Art. 401. (See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 67, 041; In force: 13-03-2003> When blows inflicted or injuries made voluntarily, but without intention to cause death, have nevertheless caused it, the offender shall be punished by imprisonment of five years to ten years.

II shall be punished by imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years, if he committed these acts of violence with premeditation

Art. 401bis. (repealed) <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 52, 029; In force: 27-03-2001>

###### Art. 402. Shall be punished by imprisonment from three months to five years and a

fine of fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros], whoever shall have caused to another person an illness or incapacity for personal work, by administering to him voluntarily, but without intention to kill, substances that can cause death, or substances that, without being of a nature to cause death, can nevertheless seriously affect health. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-

###### Art. 403.(See NOTE under TITLE) The penalty shall be (imprisonment of five years to ten years),

when these substances shall have caused either an apparently incurable illness, or [ incapacity for personal work of more than four months] , or the loss of absolute use of an organ.

(1)<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 20, 114; In force: 29-02-2016>

###### Art. 404. (See NOTE under TITLE) If substances administered voluntarily,

but without intention to cause death, have nevertheless caused it, the offender shall be punished (by imprisonment) of fifteen years to twenty years. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 69, 041; In force: 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 405. The attempt to administer to another person, without intention to cause death,

substances of the nature of those mentioned in article 402, shall be punished by imprisonment from one month to three years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to three hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

Art. 405bis.<Inserted by L 2000-11-28/35, art. 28, 029; In force: 27-03-2001> In the cases referred to below, if the crime or offence was committed against a minor or against a person [ whose situation of vulnerability by reason of age, pregnancy, illness, infirmity or physical or mental deficiency was apparent or known to the perpetrator] , the penalties shall be as follows:

1° in the cases referred to in article 398, paragraph 1, the penalties shall be imprisonment from one month to one year and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to one hundred [euros]; <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

2° in the cases referred to in article 398, paragraph 2, the penalties shall be imprisonment from two months to two years and a fine of fifty [euros] to two hundred [euros]; <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

3° in the cases referred to in article 399, paragraph 1, the penalties shall be imprisonment from four months to four years and a fine of fifty

[euros] to two hundred [euros]; <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>
4° in the cases referred to in article 399, paragraph 2, the penalties shall be imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine of one hundred [euros] to five hundred [euros]; <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

5° in the cases referred to in article 400, paragraph 1, the penalty shall be imprisonment of five years to ten years;

6° in the cases referred to in article 400, paragraph 2, the penalty shall be imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years;

7° in the cases referred to in article 401, paragraph 1, the penalty shall be imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years;

8° in the cases referred to in article 401, paragraph 2, the penalty shall be imprisonment of fifteen years to twenty years;

9° in the cases referred to in article 402, the penalty shall be imprisonment of five years to ten years;

10° in the cases referred to in article 403, the penalty shall be imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years;

11° in the cases referred to in article 404, the penalty shall be imprisonment of seventeen years to twenty years.

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 10, 084; In force: 02-02-2012>

Art. 405ter.<Inserted by L 2000-11-28/35, art. 28, 029; In force: 27-03-2001> In the cases provided for in articles 398 to 405bis, if the crime or offence was committed against a minor or against a person [ vulnerable by reason of his age, pregnancy, illness, infirmity or physical or mental deficiency and who] , was not able to provide for his maintenance, by his father, mother or other ascendants [ or collaterals up to the fourth degree] , any other person having authority over the minor or [ the vulnerable person] or having his custody, or any person who cohabits occasionally or habitually with the victim, the minimum of the penalties provided by these articles shall be doubled if it is imprisonment, and increased by two years if it is criminal imprisonment.

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 11, 084; In force: 02-02-2012>

Art. 405quater.[ When one of the motives for the crime or offence is hatred, contempt or hostility towards a person by reason of his presumed race, skin color, ancestry, national or ethnic origin, nationality, sex, sex change, sexual orientation, civil status, birth, age, fortune, religious or philosophical conviction, current or future state of health, disability, language, political conviction, trade union conviction, physical or genetic characteristic or social origin, the penalties shall be as follows:

1° in the cases referred to in article 393, the penalty shall be life imprisonment;
2° in the cases referred to in articles 398, 399, 405 and 405bis, 1° to 3°, the maximum imprisonment penalty provided by these articles shall be doubled with a maximum of five years and the maximum fine penalty shall be doubled with a maximum of five hundred euros;

3° in the cases referred to in articles 400, paragraph 1, 402 and 405bis, 4°, the penalty shall be imprisonment of five years to ten years;

4° in the cases referred to in articles 400, paragraph 2, 401, paragraph 1, 403, 405bis, 5° and 9°, the penalty shall be imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years;

5° in the cases referred to in article 401, paragraph 2, 405bis, 6°, 7° and 10°, the penalty shall be imprisonment of fifteen years to twenty years;

6° in the cases referred to in articles 404, 405bis, 8° and 11°, the penalty shall be imprisonment of twenty years to thirty years.]

(1)<L 2013-01-14/06, art. 2, 089; In force: 10-02-2013>

###### Art. 406.(See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 07-06-1963, art. 2> Shall be punished with (imprisonment from five years to ten years) whoever shall have maliciously obstructed railway, road, river or maritime traffic by any action affecting communication routes, engineering structures or equipment, or by any other action likely to make traffic or the use of means of transport dangerous or to cause accidents during their use or traffic. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 70, 041; In force: 13-03-2003> Independently of the cases referred to in the preceding paragraph, shall be punished with imprisonment from eight days to three months and a fine from twenty-six [euros] to one thousand [euros], whoever shall have maliciously obstructed railway, road, river or maritime traffic, by any object constituting an obstacle likely to prevent traffic or the use of means of transport. <L 2000-06-26/42,

###### art. 2

, In force: 01-01-2002>

Shall be punished with a penalty from eight days to two months and a fine from twenty-six to five hundred [euros] whoever, by any other action, shall have maliciously prevented ongoing traffic on the railway or road. [ Furthermore, the judge may pronounce the disqualification from the right to drive a motor vehicle for a period of at least eight days and at most five years or for life in accordance with articles 38 to 49/1 of the coordinated laws of 16 March 1968 relating to road traffic regulations.] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002> (1)<L 2018-03-06/04, art. 3, 127; In force: 15-02-2018>

###### Art. 407. (See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 71, 041; In force: 13- 03-2003> If the act has caused injuries of the nature of those provided for in article 399, the offender shall be sentenced to imprisonment from ten years to fifteen years. He shall be sentenced to imprisonment from fifteen years to twenty years, if the injuries are of the nature of those provided for in article 400.

###### Art. 408. (See NOTE under TITLE) If the act has caused the death of a person, the

offender shall be punished (with imprisonment from twenty years to thirty years). <L 2003-01-23/42,

###### art. 72

, 041; In force: 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 409.

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 29, 029; In force: 27-03-2001> § 1er. Whoever shall have practiced, facilitated or promoted any form of mutilation of the genital organs of a female person, with or without the consent of the latter, shall be punished with imprisonment from three years to five years. The attempt shall be punished with imprisonment from eight days to one year. [ Shall be punished with the same penalty whoever shall have incited the practice of any form of mutilation of the genital organs of a female person or shall have, directly or indirectly, in writing or verbally made, had made, published, distributed or disseminated publicity in favor of such a practice.]

###### § 2. If the mutilation is practiced on a minor or for profit, the penalty shall be

imprisonment from five years to seven years.

###### § 3. When the mutilation has caused an apparently incurable disease or

[ personal incapacity for work of more than four months] , the penalty shall be imprisonment from five years to ten years.

###### § 4. When the mutilation made without intention to cause death shall nevertheless have

caused it, the penalty shall be imprisonment from ten years to fifteen years.

###### § 5. If the mutilation referred to in § 1er has been practiced on a minor or a

person who, by reason of his physical or mental state, was not able to provide for his maintenance, by his father, mother or other ascendants, any other person having authority over the minor or incapable person or having custody thereof, or any person who cohabits occasionally or habitually with the victim, the minimum of the penalties provided in §§ 1er to 4 shall be doubled if it is an imprisonment, and increased by two years if it is imprisonment.

(1)<L 2014-05-05/07, art. 2, 105; In force: 12-07-2014>

(2)<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 24, 114; In force: 29-02-2016>

###### Art. 410.

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 30, 029; In force: 27-03-2001> In the cases mentioned in articles 398 to 405, if the offender has committed the crime or offense against his father and mother or other ascendants [ in direct or collateral line up to the fourth degree] , the minimum of the penalty provided by these articles shall be doubled if it is an imprisonment, and increased by two years if it is imprisonment.

It shall be the same if the offender has committed the crime or offense against his spouse or the person with whom he cohabits or has cohabited and maintains or has maintained a lasting emotional and sexual relationship.

(Furthermore, in the case referred to in article 398, paragraph 1er, the maximum of the penalty is increased to one year of imprisonment.) <L 2003-01-28/33, art. 2, 038; In force: 22- (1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 12, 084; In force: 02-02-2012>

Art. 410bis.<inserted by L 2006-12-20/41, art. 6; In force: 22-02-2007> In the cases mentioned in articles 398 to 405, if the offender has committed the crime or offense against a driver, an attendant, a ticket inspector or a ticket office clerk of an operator of a public transport network, [ a member of staff employed by the FPS Justice in a penitentiary institution or within the security corps,] a postman, a fireman, a member of civil protection, an ambulance driver, a doctor, a pharmacist, a physiotherapist, a nurse, a member of staff assigned to reception in emergency services of care institutions, a social worker, or a psychologist of a public service, in the exercise of their functions, [ the penalties shall be those provided in paragraph 3] . [ If ] the offender, being a pupil or a student who is enrolled in an educational establishment or who has been enrolled there during the six months preceding the facts, or the father or mother or a family member of this pupil or this student, or any other person having authority over this pupil or this student or having custody thereof, has committed the crime or offense against a member of staff or management of this educational establishment, against persons responsible for the care of pupils in a medico-pedagogical Institute organized or subsidized by the community, or against an external intervener charged by the community authorities with preventing and resolving problems of school violence, in the exercise of their functions [ , the penalties shall be those provided in paragraph 3] .

[ The penalties are as follows:

1° in the cases referred to in articles 398, 399 and 405, the maximum of the imprisonment penalty provided by these articles shall be doubled with a maximum of five years;

2° in the cases referred to in articles 400, paragraph 1er and 402, the penalty shall be imprisonment from five years to ten years;

3° in the cases referred to in articles 400, paragraph 2, 401, paragraph 1er and 403, the penalty shall be imprisonment from ten years to fifteen years;

4° in the cases referred to in article 401, paragraph 2, the penalty shall be imprisonment from fifteen years to twenty years;

5° in the cases referred to in article 404, the penalty shall be imprisonment from twenty years to thirty years.]

(1)<L 2012-12-27/29, art. 20, 090; In force: 10-02-2013>

Art. 410ter.[ In the cases referred to in articles 398 to 405, if the offender has committed the crime or offense against a referee at a sports event, the minimum of the penalty provided by these articles shall be increased by half of this penalty if it is an imprisonment and increased by one year if it is imprisonment.]

(1)<Inserted by L 2012-07-05/07, art. 2, 086; In force: 29-07-2012>

###### SECTION III. - EXCUSABLE HOMICIDE, INJURIES AND BLOWS.

###### Art. 411. Homicide, injuries and blows are excusable, if they have been

immediately provoked by serious violence against persons.

###### Art. 412. The crimes and offenses mentioned in the preceding article are also

excusable if they were committed in repelling, during the day, the scaling or breaking through of enclosures, walls or entrances of an inhabited house or apartment or their outbuildings, unless it is established that the agent could not have believed in an attack against persons, either as the direct aim of the person attempting to scale or break through, or as a consequence of the resistance that the designs of the latter would encounter.

###### Art. 413. (Repealed)

<L 1997-11-24/51, art. 3, 020; En vigueur : 16-02-1998>

###### Art. 414.[ When the fact of excuse is proven, the penalty shall be reduced:

- to imprisonment of one year to five years and to a fine of one hundred euros to five hundred euros, if it concerns a crime carrying a maximum penalty exceeding twenty years of imprisonment, whether or not it has been correctionalized,

- to imprisonment of six months to two years and to a fine of fifty euros to two hundred euros, if it concerns any other crime, correctionalized or not;

- to imprisonment of eight days to three months and to a fine of twenty-six euros to one hundred euros, if it concerns another offense.]

(1)<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 25, 114; En vigueur : 29-02-2016>

###### Art. 415. (repealed)

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 52, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001>-03- 1987, art. 97>

###### SECTION IV. - OF JUSTIFIED HOMICIDE, INJURIES AND BLOWS.

###### Art. 416. There is neither crime nor offense when the homicide, injuries and blows

were commanded by the present necessity of self-defense or defense of others.

###### Art. 417. Included in the cases of present necessity of defense are the two

following cases:

If the homicide was committed, if the injuries were inflicted, if the blows were struck in repelling, during the night, the scaling or breaking through of enclosures, walls or entrances of an inhabited house or apartment or their outbuildings, unless it is established that the agent could not have believed in an attack against persons, either as the direct aim of the person attempting to scale or break through, or as a consequence of the resistance that the designs of the latter would encounter. If the act took place in defending oneself against the perpetrators of theft or pillage, carried out with violence against persons.

###### Section V.

<inséré par L 2002-06-14/42, art. 5; En vigueur : 24-08-2002> Of torture, inhuman treatment and degrading treatment

Art. 417bis. <inséré par L 2002-06-14/42, art. 5; En vigueur : 24-08-2002> For the application of this section, the following is understood by:

1° torture: any deliberate inhuman treatment which causes acute pain or very serious and cruel suffering, physical or mental;

2° inhuman treatment: any treatment by which serious mental or physical suffering is intentionally inflicted on a person, in particular for the purpose of obtaining information or confessions from that person, punishing that person, putting pressure on that person or intimidating that person or third parties; 3° degrading treatment: any treatment which causes serious humiliation or debasement to the person subjected to it, in the eyes of others or in that person's own eyes. Art. 417ter.<inséré par L 2002-06-14/42, art. 5; En vigueur : 24-08-2002> Whoever subjects a person to torture shall be punished with imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years.

The offense referred to in the first paragraph shall be punished with imprisonment of fifteen years to twenty years in the following cases:

1° when it has been committed:

- a) either by a public officer or official, a depository or an agent of the public force acting in the performance of their duties;
- b) either against a person [ whose situation of vulnerability by reason of age, a state of pregnancy, an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental deficiency or by reason of their precarious situation was apparent or known to the perpetrator of the acts] ;
- c) either against a minor;

2° or when the act has caused an apparently incurable disease, an [ incapacity for personal work of more than four months] , the complete loss of an organ or the use of an organ, or a serious mutilation.

The offense referred to in the first paragraph shall be punished with twenty years to thirty years of imprisonment:

1° when it has been committed against a minor or against a person who, by reason of their physical or mental condition, was not able to provide for their own maintenance, by their father, mother or other ascendants, any other person having authority over them or having their custody, or any adult person who cohabitates occasionally or habitually with the victim;

2° or when it has caused death and has been committed without intent to cause it.

The order of a superior or an authority cannot justify the offense provided for in the first paragraph.

(The state of necessity cannot justify the offense provided for in the first paragraph.) <L 2006-05-18/53, art. 2, 060; En vigueur : 11-12-2006>

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 13, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

(2)<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 23, 114; En vigueur : 29-02-2016>

Art. 417quater.<inséré par L 2002-06-14/42, art. 5; En vigueur : 24-08-2002> Whoever subjects a person to inhuman treatment shall be punished with imprisonment of five years to ten years.

The offense referred to in the first paragraph shall be punished with ten years to fifteen years of imprisonment in the following cases:

1° when it has been committed:

- a) either by a public officer or official, a depository or an agent of the public force acting in the performance of their duties;
- b) either against a person [ whose situation of vulnerability by reason of age, a state of pregnancy, an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental deficiency or by reason of their precarious situation was apparent or known to the perpetrator of the acts] ;
- c) either against a minor;

2° or when the act has caused an apparently incurable disease, an [ incapacity for personal work of more than four months] , the complete loss of an organ or the use of an organ, or a serious mutilation.

The offense referred to in the first paragraph shall be punished with fifteen years to twenty years of imprisonment:

1° when it has been committed against a minor or against a person who, by reason of their physical or mental condition, was not able to provide for their own maintenance, by their father, mother or other ascendants, any other person having authority over them or having their custody, or any adult person who cohabitates occasionally or habitually with the victim;

2° or when it has caused death and has been committed without intent to cause it.

The order of a superior or an authority cannot justify the offense provided for in the first paragraph.

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 14, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

(2)<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 23, 114; En vigueur : 29-02-2016>

Art. 417quinquies.<inséré par L 2002-06-14/42, art. 5; En vigueur : 24-08- 2002>Whoever subjects a person to degrading treatment shall be punished with imprisonment of fifteen days to two years and a fine of 50 EUR to 300 EUR or with one of these penalties only.

[ If the degrading treatment is committed against a person whose situation of vulnerability by reason of age, a state of pregnancy, an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental deficiency was apparent or known to the perpetrator of the acts, the minimum penalty provided for in paragraph 1 shall be doubled.] (1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 15, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

##### CHAPITRE II. - OF INVOLUNTARY HOMICIDE AND BODILY INJURIES.

###### Art. 418. Guilty of involuntary homicide or injury is the person who has caused the

harm through lack of foresight or precaution, but without intent to harm the person of another.

###### Art. 419. Whoever will have involuntarily caused the death of a person will be

punished with imprisonment of three months to two years and a fine of fifty [euros] to one thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01- (When death is the consequence of a traffic accident, the imprisonment will be three months to five years and the fine 50 euros to 2000 euros.) <L 2005-07-20/52, art. 28, 052; En vigueur : 31-03-2006>

Art. 419bis. (Repealed) <L 2005-07-20/52, art. 30, 052; En vigueur : 31-03-2006>

###### Art. 420. If only blows or injuries resulted from the lack of foresight or precaution

the guilty party (will be punished with imprisonment of eight days to six months) and a fine of fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros], or one of these penalties only. <L 31-03-1936, art. 1> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

(When the blows or injuries are the consequence of a traffic accident, the imprisonment will be eight days to one year and the fine 50 euros to 1,000 euros.) <L 2005-07-20/52, art. 29, 052; En vigueur : 31-03-2006> Art. 420bis. (Repealed) <L 2005-07-20/52, art. 30, 052; En vigueur : 31-03-2006>

###### Art. 421. Will be punished with imprisonment of eight days to one year and a fine

of twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros], or one of these penalties only, whoever will have involuntarily caused another person illness or incapacity for personal work, by administering substances that are of a nature to cause death or to seriously impair health. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-

###### Art. 422. When a railway train will have suffered an accident of a nature

to endanger the persons who were there, whoever will have been involuntarily the cause thereof will be punished with imprisonment of eight days to two months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros], or one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

If bodily injuries resulted from the accident, the guilty party will be punished with imprisonment of one month to three years and a fine of fifty [euros] to three hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

If the accident caused the death of a person, the imprisonment will be six months to five years and the fine one hundred [euros] to six hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

OF CERTAIN CULPABLE OMISSIONS.

Art. 422bis.<L 06-01-1961, art. 1> Will be punished with imprisonment of eight days to (one year) and a fine of fifty to five hundred [euros] or one of these penalties only, whoever refrains from coming to the aid of or procuring aid for a person exposed to serious danger, whether he has personally observed the situation of that person or that situation is described to him by those requesting his intervention. <L 1995-04-13/33, art. 4, 1°, 015; En vigueur : 05-05-1995> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

The offense requires that the person who refrained could intervene without serious danger to himself or to others. When he has not personally observed the danger to which the person to be assisted was exposed, the person who refrained cannot be punished when the circumstances in which he was called upon to intervene could lead him to believe in the lack of seriousness of the call or the existence of risks.

(The penalty provided for in paragraph 1 is increased to two years when the person exposed to serious danger is a minor [ or is a person whose situation of vulnerability due to age, a state of pregnancy, an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental disability was apparent or known to the perpetrator] .) <L 1995-04-13/33, art. 4, 2°, 015; En vigueur (1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 16, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

Art. 422ter. <L 06-01-1964, art. 1> Will be punished with the penalties provided for in the preceding article whoever, being able to do so without serious danger to himself or to others, refuses or neglects to provide to a person in danger the assistance of which he is legally required; whoever, being able to do so, refuses or neglects to perform the work, the service, or to provide the assistance of which he will have been required in circumstances of accidents, tumults, shipwreck, flood, fire or other calamities, as well as in cases of brigandage, pillaging, flagrant offense, public outcry or judicial execution.

Art. 422quater.<L 2007-05-10/35, art. 35, 064; En vigueur : 09-06-2007> In the cases provided for by articles 422bis and 422ter, the minimum of the correctional penalties imposed by these articles may be doubled, when one of the motives of the crime or offense is hatred, contempt or hostility toward a person because of their alleged race, their skin color, their ancestry, their national or ethnic origin, their nationality, their sex, their sexual orientation, their civil status, their birth, their age, their fortune, their religious or philosophical conviction, their current or future state of health, a disability, their language, their political conviction, [ their trade union conviction,] a physical or genetic characteristic or their social origin.

(1)<L 2009-12-30/01, art. 111, 074; En vigueur : 31-12-2009>

##### CHAPITRE III. - (Of offenses against minors, [ vulnerable persons] and the

family.) <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 31, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> (1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 17, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

###### Section I.

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 31; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> Of the abandonment and desertion of children or [ vulnerable persons] in need. (1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 17, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

###### Art. 423.

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 31, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> § 1er. Will be punished with imprisonment of one month to three years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to three hundred [euros], or one of these penalties only, those who will have abandoned or caused to be abandoned, in any place, a minor or [ a person whose situation of vulnerability due to age, a state of pregnancy, an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental disability was apparent or known to the perpetrator] . <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-

###### § 2. If as a result of the abandonment, the person referred to in § 1er remained

seriously mutilated, suffers from an illness appearing incurable or has lost the absolute use of an organ, the guilty parties will be punished with imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of fifty [euros] to three hundred [euros], or one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### § 3. If the abandonment caused the death of the person referred to in § 1er, the

guilty parties will be punished with imprisonment of five years to ten years. (1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 18, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

###### Art. 424.Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to six months and a

fine of fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros], or one of these penalties only, without prejudice, if applicable, to the application of more severe criminal provisions: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

Fathers or mothers or adoptive parents who abandon their child in need, even though he has not been left alone, who refuse to take him back or who refuse to pay for his maintenance when they have entrusted him to a third party or he has been entrusted to a third party by judicial decision.

[ Direct descendants who abandon their father, mother, adoptive parent or other ascendant in need, even though he has not been left alone, who refuse to take him back or who refuse to pay for his maintenance when they have entrusted him to a third party or he has been entrusted to a third party by judicial decision.] In case of a second conviction for one of the offenses provided for in this article, committed within five years from the first, the penalties may be doubled.

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 19, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

###### Section II.

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 31, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> Deprivations of food or care inflicted on minors or [ vulnerable persons] .

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 20, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

###### Art. 425.

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 31, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> § 1er. Shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to three years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to three hundred [euros], or one of these penalties only, whoever shall have deliberately deprived of food or care, to the point of compromising his health, a minor or [ a person whose situation of vulnerability by reason of age, a state of pregnancy, an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental deficiency was apparent or known to the perpetrator and who] , was not able to provide for his maintenance. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### § 2. If the deprivations of food or care have caused either an apparently

incurable illness, or the complete loss of the use of an organ, or a serious mutilation, the offender shall be punished by imprisonment of five years to ten years.

###### § 3. If the deprivations of food or care made deliberately, without intent

to cause death, have nevertheless caused it, the offender shall be punished by imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years.

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 21, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

###### Art. 426.

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 31, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> § 1er. Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to two months and a fine of fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros] or one of these penalties only, without prejudice, if applicable, to the application of more severe criminal provisions, whoever having the custody of a minor or of a person [ vulnerable by reason of his age, a state of pregnancy, an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental deficiency and who is not able to provide for his maintenance] , shall have neglected the maintenance of this minor or this person to the point of compromising his health. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### 1 §

2. If the negligence has resulted in the death of the minor or [ of a person referred to in § 1er and who was not able to provide for his maintenance] , the offender shall be punished by imprisonment of three months to two years and a fine of fifty [euros] to one thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01- (1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 22, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

###### Section III.

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 31, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> Provision common to sections Ier and II.

###### Art. 427.

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 31, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> In the cases provided for in articles 423, 425 and 426, if the offender has committed the acts towards his father, mother, adoptive parents or other ascendants, the minimum penalty provided for by these articles shall be doubled if it is imprisonment, and increased by two years if it is detention.

It shall be the same when the offender is the father, the mother or the adoptive parent of the victim, or any other person having authority over him or having his custody.

[ The penalty provided for in article 33 may, in addition, be applied.]

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 23, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

###### Section IV.

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 31, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> Abduction and concealment of minors [ and vulnerable persons] . (1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 24, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

###### Art. 428.

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 31, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> § 1er.

Whoever shall have abducted or caused to be abducted a minor under twelve years of age shall be punished by imprisonment of five years to ten years, even though the minor may have followed his abductor voluntarily.

###### § 2. Whoever shall have, by violence, cunning or threat, abducted or caused to be abducted a

minor over twelve years of age [ or any person whose situation of vulnerability by reason of age, a state of pregnancy, an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental deficiency was apparent or known to the perpetrator,] shall be punished by imprisonment of five years to ten years.

###### § 3.

(...) <L 2002-06-14/42, art. 6, 036; En vigueur : 24-08-2002>

###### § 4. The penalty shall be imprisonment of fifteen years to twenty years if the abduction or

detention of the abducted minor [ or of the person referred to in § 2] has caused either an apparently incurable illness, or a [ personal incapacity to work for more than four months] , or the complete loss of the use of an organ, or a serious mutilation.

###### § 5. If the abduction or detention has caused death, the penalty shall be imprisonment

of twenty years to thirty years.

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 25, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012> (2)<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 23, 114; En vigueur : 29-02-2016>

###### Art. 429.

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 31, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> Shall be punished by the same penalties as the perpetrator of the abduction, whoever shall keep a minor [ or a vulnerable person, referred to in article 428, § 2, whom he knows to have been abducted] . (1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 26, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

###### Art. 430.

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 31, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> In the cases referred to in articles 428 and 429, with the exception of the cases referred to in [ article 428, §§ 4 et 5] , the penalty shall be imprisonment of two years to five years and a fine of two hundred [euros] to five hundred [euros], if within five days of the abduction, the abductor or the person referred to in article 429 has voluntarily returned the abducted minor [ or the abducted vulnerable person] . <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 27, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

(2)<L 2014-05-05/09, art. 15, 106; En vigueur : 18-07-2014>

###### Section V.

Failure to present children. <L 2000-11-28/35, art. 31, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001>

###### Art. 431.

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 31, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to one year and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to one hundred [euros] or one of these penalties only, those who, being entrusted with a minor under twelve years of age, shall not present him to the persons who have the right to claim him. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002> If the offender conceals this minor for more than five days from those who have the right to claim him or if he unlawfully retains this minor outside the territory of the Kingdom, he shall be punished by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros] or one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42,

###### art. 2

, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 432.

<L 2000-11-28/35, art. 31, 029; En vigueur : 27-03-2001> § 1er. Shall be punished by imprisonment from eight days to one year and by a fine from twenty-six [euros] to one thousand [euros], or by one of these penalties only: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002> the father or mother who removes or attempts to remove his or her minor child from proceedings instituted against him or her under legislation relating to the protection of youth or assistance to youth, who removes or attempts to remove him or her from the custody of the persons to whom the competent authority has entrusted him or her, who fails to present him or her to those who have the right to claim him or her, abducts him or her or has him or her abducted, even with his or her consent.

If the offender has been deprived of parental authority in whole or in part, the imprisonment may be increased to three years.

###### § 2. If the offender conceals the minor child for more than five days from those who

have the right to claim him or her or if he or she unlawfully retains the minor child outside the territory of the Kingdom, he or she shall be punished by imprisonment from one year to five years and by a fine from fifty [euros] to one thousand [euros], or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

If the offender has been deprived of parental authority in whole or in part, the imprisonment shall be at least three years.

###### § 3. In cases where a decision has been made on the custody of the minor child either during

or following proceedings for divorce or judicial separation, or in other circumstances provided for by law, the penalties provided for in §§ 1 and 2 shall be applied to the father or mother who removes or attempts to remove his or her minor child from the custody of those to whom he or she has been entrusted by virtue of the decision, who fails to present him or her to those who have the right to claim him or her, abducts him or her or has him or her abducted, even with his or her consent.

###### § 4. Where the custody of the minor child has been the subject of a settlement agreement

prior to proceedings by mutual consent, the penalties provided for in §§ 1 and 2 shall be applied to the father or mother who, from the date of registration of the divorce by mutual consent, removes or attempts to remove his or her minor child from the custody of those to whom he or she has been entrusted by virtue of the decision or settlement agreement, who fails to present him or her to those who have the right to claim him or her, abducts him or her or has him or her abducted, even with his or her consent.

###### Section VI. - On the use of minors [ and vulnerable persons] for

criminal or delictual purposes. <insérée par L 2005-08-10/62, art. 3 ; En vigueur : 02- (1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 28, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

###### Art. 433.

<L 2005-08-10/62, art. 4, 054 ; En vigueur : 02-09-2005> Subject to the application of Article 433quinquies, any person who, directly or through an intermediary, has attracted or used a minor [ or a person whose situation of vulnerability by reason of age, a state of pregnancy, an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental deficiency was apparent or known to the perpetrator,] with a view to committing, in one of the ways provided for in Article 66, a crime or an offence, shall be punished by the penalties provided for that crime or offence, the minimum custodial sentence for which shall be increased by one month where the maximum imprisonment penalty provided for is one year, by two months where it is two years, by three months where it is three years, by five months where it is five years, and by two years in the case of imprisonment for a fixed term, and the minimum amount of the fine, if any, shall be doubled. The minimum penalty provided for in paragraph 1 is further increased in the same proportion where:

1° the minor is under sixteen years of age, or

2° the person referred to in paragraph 1 abuses the particularly vulnerable position in which the minor finds himself or herself, or

3° the person referred to in paragraph 1 is the father, mother, another ascendant, the adopter, or any other person having authority over the minor or having custody of the minor, or

4° the action of attracting minors or using them with a view to committing a crime or an offence constitutes a habitual activity.

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 29, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

###### Section VII.

On the invasion of privacy of the minor. <insérée par L 2005-08- 10/62, art. 5 ; En vigueur : 02-09-2005>

Art. 433bis. <inséré par L 2005-08-10/62, art. 6 ; En vigueur : 02-09-2005> The publication and dissemination by means of books, by press, by cinematography, by radio, by television or by any other means, of reports of proceedings before the youth court, before the investigating judge and before the chambers of the court of appeal competent to rule on appeals lodged against their decisions, are prohibited. Only the grounds and the operative part of the judicial decision pronounced in public hearing are excepted, subject to the application of paragraph 3.

The publication and dissemination, by any process, of texts, drawings, photographs or images likely to reveal the identity of a person prosecuted or who is the subject of a measure provided for (in Articles 37, 39, 43, 49, 52, 52quater and 57bis) of the Act of 8 April 1965 on the protection of youth or in the Act of 1 March 2002 on the provisional placement of minors who have committed an act classified as an offence, are also prohibited. The same applies to the person who is the subject of a measure taken within the framework of the procedure referred to in Article 63bis of the Act of 8 April 1965 on the protection of youth (on the care of minors who have committed an act classified as an offence and on the reparation of damage caused by that act). <L 2006-05-15/35, art. 22, 056; En vigueur : 01-10- Offences against this Article are punishable by imprisonment from two months to two years and by a fine from three hundred euros to three thousand euros or by one of these penalties only.

###### Section VIII. - [ On the luring of minors by means of information

and communication technologies for criminal or delictual purposes.] (1)<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 26, 114; En vigueur : 29-02-2016>

Art. 433bis/1. [ Shall be punished by imprisonment from three months to five years, the adult person who communicates by means of information and communication technologies with an established or supposed minor, with a view to facilitating the perpetration against him or her of a crime or an offence:

1° if he or she has concealed or lied about his or her identity or age or status; 2° if he or she has insisted on discretion to be observed regarding their exchanges; 3° if he or she has offered or held out the prospect of a gift or any advantage; 4° if he or she has used any other manoeuvre.]

This excerpt sits under: CODE PENAL > Livre II > TITRE VIII. - DES CRIMES ET DES DELITS CONTRE LES PERSONNES. > CHAPITRE III. - (Des atteintes aux mineurs, [ aux personnes vulnérables] et à la

###### art. 6;

In force: 12-09-2005>

Art. 433ter.<Inserted by L 2005-08-10/61, art. 7; In force: 12-09-2005> Shall be punished by imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine from five hundred euros to twenty-five thousand euros:

1° whoever has recruited, led, diverted or detained a person with a view to delivering them to begging, has incited them to beg or to continue doing so, or has made them available to a beggar so that they may use them to arouse public commiseration;

2° whoever has, in any manner whatsoever, exploited the begging of another.

The attempt to commit the offences referred to in paragraph 1 shall be punished by imprisonment from one month to two years and a fine from one hundred euros to two thousand euros.

[ The fine shall be applied as many times as there are victims.]

(1)<L 2013-06-24/24, art. 3, 097; In force: 02-08-2013>

Art. 433quater.<Inserted by L 2005-08-10/61, art. 8; In force: 12-09-2005> The offence referred to in article 433ter, paragraph 1, shall be punished by imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine from five hundred euros to fifty thousand euros when it has been committed:

1° against a minor;

2° by abusing the [ vulnerable situation in which a person finds themselves due to their illegal or precarious administrative situation, their precarious social situation, their age, a state of pregnancy, an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental deficiency] , in such a way that the person in fact has no other real and acceptable choice but to submit to this abuse;

3° by making use, directly or indirectly, of fraudulent maneuvers, violence, threats or any form of coercion.

[ The fine shall be applied as many times as there are victims.]

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 30, 084; In force: 02-02-2012>

CHAPTER IIIter. - Trafficking in human beings <Inserted by L 2005-08-10/61, art. 9; In force: 12-09-2005>

Art. 433quinquies.<Inserted by L 2005-08-10/61, art. 10; In force: 12-09-2005>

###### 1 §

1. [ The offence of trafficking in human beings is constituted by recruiting, transporting, transferring, housing, receiving a person, taking or transferring control exercised over them:

1° for the purposes of exploiting prostitution or other forms of sexual exploitation;

2° for the purposes of exploiting begging;

3° for the purposes of work or services, in conditions contrary to human dignity;

4° for the purposes of removing organs in violation of the law of 13 June 1986 on the removal and transplantation of organs, or of human bodily material in violation of the law of 19 December 2008 relating to obtaining and using human bodily material intended for human medical applications or for scientific research purposes;

5° or in order to make this person commit a crime or an offence, against their will.]

Except in the case referred to in 5, the consent of the person referred to in paragraph 1 to the contemplated or actual exploitation is irrelevant.

###### § 2. The offence provided for in § 1 shall be punished by imprisonment from one year to five

years and a fine from five hundred euros to fifty thousand euros.

###### § 3. The attempt to commit the offence referred to in § 1 shall be punished by

imprisonment from one year to three years and a fine from one hundred euros to ten thousand euros.

[ § 4. The fine shall be applied as many times as there are victims.]

(1)<L 2013-04-29/15, art. 2, 096; In force: 02-08-2013>

(2)<L 2013-06-24/24, art. 5, 097; In force: 02-08-2013>

Art. 433sexies.<Inserted by L 2005-08-10/61, art. 11; In force: 12-09-2005> The offence provided for in article 433quinquies, § 1, shall be punished by imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine from seven hundred and fifty euros to seventy-five thousand euros when the offence has been committed:

1° by a person who has authority over the victim, or by a person who has abused the authority or facilities conferred upon them by their functions;

2° by an officer or a public official, a depositary or an agent of the public force acting on the occasion of the exercise of their functions.

[ The fine shall be applied as many times as there are victims.]

(1)<L 2013-06-24/24, art. 6, 097; In force: 02-08-2013>

Art. 433septies.<Inserted by L 2005-08-10/61, art. 12; In force: 12-09-2005> The offence provided for in article 433quinquies, § 1, shall be punished by imprisonment from ten years to fifteen years and a fine from one thousand euros to one hundred thousand euros in the following cases:

1° when the offence has been committed against a minor;

2° when it has been committed by abusing the [ vulnerable situation in which a person finds themselves due to their illegal or precarious administrative situation, their precarious social situation, their age, a state of pregnancy, an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental deficiency] , in such a way that the person in fact has no other real and acceptable choice but to submit to this abuse;

3° when it has been committed by making use, directly or indirectly, of fraudulent maneuvers, violence, threats or any form of coercion [ , or by resorting to abduction, abuse of authority or deception] ;

[ 3bis° when it has been committed by means of the offer or acceptance of payments or any advantages to obtain the consent of a person having authority over the victim;]

4° when the life of the victim has been deliberately or through serious negligence endangered;

5° when the offence has caused an apparently incurable illness, an [ incapacity for personal work of more than four months] , the complete loss of an organ or the use of an organ, or a serious mutilation;

6° when the activity concerned constitutes a habitual activity;

7° when it constitutes an act of participation in the main or accessory activity of an association, whether or not the offender has the status of leader.

[ The fine shall be applied as many times as there are victims.]

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 31, 084; In force: 02-02-2012>

(2)<L 2013-06-24/24, art. 7, 097; In force: 02-08-2013>

(3)<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 23, 114; In force: 29-02-2016>

(4)<L 2016-05-31/02, art. 8, 118; In force: 18-06-2016>

Art. 433octies.<Inserted by L 2005-08-10/61, art. 13; In force: 12-09-2005> The offence provided for in article 433quinquies, § 1, shall be punished by imprisonment from fifteen years to twenty years and a fine from one thousand euros to one hundred and fifty thousand euros in the following cases:

1° when the offence has caused the death of the victim without intent to cause it; 2° when it constitutes an act of participation in the main or accessory activity of a criminal organization, whether or not the offender has the status of leader.

[ The fine shall be applied as many times as there are victims.]

(1)<L 2013-06-24/24, art. 8, 097; In force: 02-08-2013>

Art. 433novies.<Inserted by L 2005-08-10/61, art. 14; In force: 12-09-2005> [ § 1.] In the cases referred to in articles [ 433quinquies to 433octies] , the offenders shall in addition be sentenced to disqualification from the rights set out in

[ article 31, paragraph 1] .

[ § 2. The courts may pronounce the disqualifications referred to in articles 382,

###### § 2, and 382bis against persons convicted for acts referred to in

articles 433quinquies to 433octies, for a term from one year to twenty years.] [ § 3. Article 382quater applies to persons convicted for acts referred to in articles 433quinquies to 433octies.

###### § 4. Without regard to the status of natural or legal person of

the operator, owner, tenant or manager, the court may order the temporary or permanent, partial or total closure of the establishment in which the offences referred to in articles 433quinquies to 433octies were committed, in accordance with the procedures provided for in article 382, § 3, paragraphs 2 to 5.

5. Article 389 applies to paragraphs 1, 2 and 4.]

CHAPITRE IIIquater. - Abuse of the vulnerability of others by selling, renting or making available property with a view to making an abnormal profit <Inséré par L 2005-08-10/61, art. 15; En vigueur : 12-09-2005>

Art. 433decies.<Inséré par L 2005-08-10/61, art. 15; En vigueur : 12-09-2005> Shall be punished by imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine of five hundred euros to twenty-five thousand euros, whoever has abused, either directly or through an intermediary, the [ situation of vulnerability in which a person finds themselves due to their illegal or precarious administrative situation, their precarious social situation, their age, a state of pregnancy, an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental disability] , by selling, renting or making available, with the intention of making an abnormal profit, a movable property, part thereof, an immovable property, a room or another space referred to in article 479 of the Penal Code in conditions incompatible with human dignity [ ...] . The fine shall be applied as many times as there are victims.
