Art. 433undecies. <Inséré par L 2005-08-10/61, art. 17; En vigueur : 12-09-2005> The offence referred to in article 433decies shall be punished by imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine of one thousand euros to one hundred thousand euros in the following cases:

1° when the activity concerned constitutes a habitual activity;

2° when it constitutes an act of participation in the principal or accessory activity of an association, whether or not the offender has the status of leader.

The fine shall be applied as many times as there are victims.

Art. 433duodecies. <Inséré par L 2005-08-10/61, art. 18; En vigueur : 12-09- 2005>The offence referred to in article 433decies shall be punished by imprisonment from five years to ten years and a fine of one thousand euros to one hundred and fifty thousand euros if it constitutes an act of participation in the principal or accessory activity of a criminal organisation, whether or not the offender has the status of leader. The fine shall be applied as many times as there are victims.

Art. 433terdecies.<Inséré par L 2005-08-10/61, art. 19; En vigueur : 12-09-2005> In the cases referred to in articles 433undecies and 433duodecies, the offenders shall also be sentenced to disqualification from the rights specified in [ article 31, paragraph 1] .

The special confiscation provided for in article 42, 1°, is applied to those guilty of the offence referred to in article 433decies, even when the property of the things to which it relates does not belong to the convicted person, without this confiscation being able to prejudice the rights of third parties over the property that may be subject to confiscation. It must also be applied, in the same circumstances, to the movable property, to part thereof, to the immovable property, to the room or to any other space referred to in that article.

(It may also be applied to the counter-value of such movable or immovable property disposed of between the commission of the offence and the final judicial decision.) <L 2006-02-09/33, art. 2, 055 ; En vigueur : 10-03-2006>

(1)<L 2009-04-14/01, art. 20, 073; En vigueur : 15-04-2009>

Art. 433quaterdecies.<Inséré par L 2005-08-10/61, art. 20; En vigueur : 12-09- 2005> As the case may be, the public prosecutor or the investigating judge may seize the movable property, part thereof, the immovable property, the room or any other space referred to in article 433decies. If a decision is made to carry out the seizure, the movable property, part thereof, the immovable property, the room or any other space referred to in article 433decies must be sealed or, with the written consent of the owner or the lessor, be made available to the C.P.A.S. in order to be restored and temporarily rented. The decision of the public prosecutor or the investigating judge, as the case may be, to proceed with the seizure shall be notified to the owner or the lessor. In the event of seizure of immovable property, the decision must also be notified within twenty-four hours at the latest and be presented for transcription to the [ competent office of the General Administration of Patrimonial Documentation] of the place where the property is located. The day of transcription taken into account is that of the notification of the seizure decision. The seizure remains valid until the final judicial decision by which either confiscation has been ordered, or the lifting of the seizure is ordered. A lifting of the seizure may previously be granted at any time, as the case may be, by the public prosecutor or by the investigating judge after the latter has notified the public prosecutor. The person whose property has been seized may only bring the remedies provided for in articles 28sexies and 61quater of the Code of Criminal Procedure after a period of one year from the date of the seizure.

(1)<L 2018-07-11/07, art. 69, 133; En vigueur : 30-07-2018>

Art. 433quinquiesdecies. <Inséré par L 2005-08-10/61, art. 21; En vigueur : 12- 09-2005> In the cases referred to in article 433decies, the victims may be, where appropriate, accommodated or rehoused on the decision, as the case may be, of the competent minister, the competent authority or the officials designated by them, in consultation with the competent services in this matter. The accommodation costs shall be borne by the accused. When the accused is acquitted, the costs shall be borne, as the case may be, by the State or the competent C.P.A.S.

##### CHAPITRE IV. - OFFENCES AGAINST PERSONAL LIBERTY AND THE INVIOLABILITY

OF THE HOME, COMMITTED BY PRIVATE INDIVIDUALS.


This excerpt sits under: CODE PENAL > Livre II > TITRE VIII. - DES CRIMES ET DES DELITS CONTRE LES PERSONNES. > CHAPITRE III. - (Des atteintes aux mineurs, [ aux personnes vulnérables] et à la

###### Art. 434. Shall be punished with imprisonment of three months to two years and a

fine of twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros], those who, without order of the constituted authorities and outside the cases where the law permits or orders the arrest or detention of individuals, shall have arrested or caused to be arrested, detained or caused to be detained any person whatsoever. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 435. The imprisonment shall be from six months to three years and the fine from

fifty [euros] to three hundred [euros], if the illegal and arbitrary detention has lasted more than ten days. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 436. If the illegal and arbitrary detention has lasted more than one month, the guilty

party shall be condemned to imprisonment of one year to five years and to a fine of one hundred [euros] to five hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 437. (See NOTE under TITLE) The penalty of (confinement from five to ten years) shall be

pronounced, if the arrest was executed, either on a false order of the public authority, or with the costume or under the name of one of its agents, or if the person arrested or detained was threatened with death. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 74, 041; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 438. (Repealed)

<L 2002-06-14/42, art. 7, 036; En vigueur : 24-08-2002>

Art. 438bis.<L 2007-05-10/35, art. 36, 064; En vigueur : 09-06-2007> In the cases provided by the present chapter, the minimum of the penalties carried by these articles may be doubled if they are correctional penalties and increased by two years if it concerns confinement, when one of the motives of the crime or the offense is hatred, contempt or hostility towards a person because of their supposed race, their skin color, their ancestry, their national or ethnic origin, their nationality, their sex, their sexual orientation, their marital status, their birth, their age, their fortune, their religious or philosophical conviction, their current or future state of health, a disability, their language, their political conviction, [ their trade union conviction,] a physical or genetic characteristic or their social origin.

(1)<L 2009-12-30/01, art. 112, 074; En vigueur : 31-12-2009>

###### Art. 439.Shall be punished with imprisonment of fifteen days to two years and a

fine of twenty-six [euros] to three hundred [euros], whoever, without order of the authority and outside the cases where the law permits entry into the domicile of individuals against their will, [ either shall have penetrated into a house, an apartment, a room or a dwelling inhabited by others, or their dependencies, by means of threats or violence against persons, by means of breaking and entering, climbing or false keys, or shall occupy this property, or shall stay there without authorization of the inhabitants.] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

(1)<L 2017-10-18/08, art. 2, 126; En vigueur : 16-11-2017>

###### Art. 440. The imprisonment shall be from six months to five years and the fine from one hundred

[euros] to five hundred [euros], if the act was committed, either on a false order of the public authority, or with the costume, or under the name of one of its agents, or with the combination of the following three circumstances: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

If the act was executed at night;

If it was executed by two or more persons;

If the guilty parties or one of them were carrying weapons.

The guilty parties may, in addition, be condemned to disqualification, in accordance with article 33 (...). <L 09-04-1930, art. 32>

###### Art. 441. The attempt of the offense provided by the preceding article shall be punished with

imprisonment of one month to one year and a fine of fifty [euros] to three hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 442. Shall be punished with imprisonment of fifteen days to two years and a

fine of twenty-six [euros] to three hundred [euros], whoever shall have entered, without the consent of the owner or the tenant, into the places designated in article 439, and shall have been found there at night. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-

###### Art. 442

/1. [ § 1er. Shall be punished with imprisonment of eight days to one month and a fine of twenty-six euros to one hundred euros or one of these penalties only, whoever, either without order of the authority, or without authorization of a person possessing a title or a right that gives access to the property concerned or that permits to use it or to stay in the property and outside the cases where the law authorizes it, shall have penetrated into the house, apartment, room or uninhabited dwelling of another, or their dependencies or any other premises or the uninhabited movable property of another that may or may not serve as dwelling, or shall occupy it, or shall stay there in any manner whatsoever, without being oneself holder of the aforementioned right or title.

###### § 2. Shall be punished with imprisonment of eight days to one year and a fine of

twenty-six euros to two hundred euros or one of these penalties only, whoever, within the fixed deadline, shall not comply with the evacuation order referred to in article 12, § 1er, of the law of 18 October 2017 relating to illegal penetration, occupation or stay in another's property or with the expulsion referred to in article 1344decies of the Judicial Code.

###### § 3. The offense referred to in paragraph 1 may only be prosecuted upon complaint

of a person holding a title or right over the property concerned.] (1)<Inséré par L 2017-10-18/08, art. 3, 126; En vigueur : 16-11-2017> CHAPTER IVbis. - (inserted by <L 1998-10-30/34, art. 2, In force: 27-12-1998>)

HARASSMENT.

Art. 442bis.(inserted by <L 1998-10-30/34, art. 2, In force: 27-12-1998>) Whoever has harassed a person when they knew or should have known that this behavior would seriously affect the peace of mind of the person concerned, shall be punished by imprisonment of fifteen days to two years and a fine of fifty [euros] to three hundred [euros], or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

[ If the acts referred to in paragraph 1 are committed against a person whose situation of vulnerability due to age, pregnancy, illness, infirmity or physical or mental deficiency was apparent or known to the perpetrator, the minimum penalty provided for in paragraph 1 shall be doubled.]

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 34, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

(2)<L 2016-03-25/07, art. 2, 116; En vigueur : 15-04-2016>

Art. 442ter.<L 2007-05-10/35, art. 37, 064; En vigueur : 09-06-2007> In the cases provided for by Article 442bis, the minimum of the correctional penalties imposed by that article may be doubled, when one of the motives for the offense is hatred, contempt or hostility towards a person on the basis of their alleged race, skin color, ancestry, national or ethnic origin, nationality, sex, sexual orientation, civil status, birth, age, fortune, religious or philosophical conviction, current or future state of health, disability, language, political conviction, [ trade union conviction,] physical or genetic characteristic or social origin.

(1)<L 2009-12-30/01, art. 113, 074; En vigueur : 31-12-2009>

CHAPTER IVter. [ - Abuse of the situation of weakness of persons] (1)<Inséré par L 2011-11-26/19, art. 35, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012> Art. 442quater.[ § 1. Whoever has, while knowing the situation of physical or psychological weakness of a person, seriously impairing that person's capacity for discernment, fraudulently abused that weakness to lead that person to an act or an omission seriously affecting their physical or mental integrity or their property, shall be punished by a penalty of one month to two years' imprisonment and a fine of one hundred euros to one thousand euros or by one of these penalties only.

###### § 2. The penalties shall be imprisonment of one month to four years and a

fine of two hundred euros to two thousand euros or one of these penalties only in the following cases:

1° if the act or omission referred to in § 1 results from being placed in a state of physical or psychological subjection through the exercise of serious or repeated pressure or techniques capable of impairing the capacity for discernment;

2° if the abuse referred to in § 1 was committed against a minor;

3° if the act or omission referred to in § 1 has resulted in either an apparently incurable illness, or [ personal incapacity for work of more than four months] , or complete loss of use of an organ, or serious mutilation; 4° if the abuse referred to in § 1 constitutes an act of participation in the main or ancillary activity of an association.

###### § 3. The penalty shall be imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years if the act or omission of

the person has caused their death.

###### § 4. The court may, in application of §§ 1 and 2, prohibit the convicted person from all

or part of the rights listed in Article 31, paragraph 1, for a term of five years to ten years.

###### § 5. The court may order that the judgment or a summary thereof be

published, at the convicted person's expense, in one or more daily newspapers, or in any other manner whatsoever.]

##### CHAPTER V. - ATTACKS ON THE HONOR OR REPUTATION

OF PERSONS.

###### Art. 443. Anyone who, in the cases indicated below, has maliciously imputed to a

person a specific fact which is of a nature to attack the honor of that person or to expose them to public contempt, and of which legal proof is not provided, is guilty of slander when the law admits proof of the imputed fact, and of defamation when the law does not admit such proof. (When the imputed fact is having, during hostilities, made a pact with the enemy, either by providing them with assistance in soldiers, men, money, food, weapons, ammunition or any materials whatsoever, or by procuring or facilitating by any means their entry, maintenance or stay on the territory, without having been compelled or required to do so, proof thereof shall always be admissible and may be made by all legal means.

If such proof is sufficiently provided, the imputation shall not give rise to any criminal prosecution.) <L 11-10-1919, art. unique>

###### Art. 444. The offender shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to one year and

a fine of twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros], when the imputations have been made: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002> Either in public meetings or places;

Or in the presence of several individuals, in a non-public place, but open to a certain number of persons having the right to assemble or frequent it; Or in any place, in the presence of the offended person and before witnesses;

Or by printed or unprinted writings, images or emblems posted, distributed or sold, offered for sale or exposed to public view;

Or finally by writings not made public, but addressed or communicated to several persons.

###### Art. 445. Shall be punished by imprisonment of fifteen days to six months and a

fine of fifty [euros] to one thousand [euros] :<L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur Anyone who has made a slanderous denunciation in writing to the authority; Anyone who has addressed in writing to a person slanderous imputations against their subordinate.

###### Art. 446. Slander and defamation towards any constituted body shall be punished

in the same manner as slander or defamation directed against individuals.

###### Art. 447. The accused of an offense of slander for imputations directed, by reason

of facts relating to their functions, either against the depositaries or agents of authority or against any person having a public character, or against any constituted body, shall be permitted to make, by all ordinary means, proof of the imputed facts, subject to counter-proof by the same means.

If it concerns a fact that falls within private life, the author of the imputation may not invoke, for their defense, any proof other than that resulting from a judgment or any other authentic act.

If the imputed fact is the subject of a criminal prosecution or a denunciation on which no decision has been made, the action for slander shall be suspended until the final judgment, or until the final decision of the competent authority. (In the case where the public action or disciplinary action relating to the imputed fact is extinguished, the file concerning that action is joined to the file of the action for slander and the action for slander is resumed.

In the case of a decision to file without further action or a decision of no case to answer regarding the action relating to the imputed fact, the action for slander is resumed, without prejudice to a suspension of that action if the investigation relating to the imputed fact undergoes new judicial developments.) <L 2001-07-04/55, art. 2, 032; En vigueur

###### Art. 448. Whoever shall have insulted a person either through acts, or through

writings, images or emblems, in one of the circumstances indicated in article 444, shall be punished by imprisonment from eight days to two months and a fine from twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros], or by only one of these penalties. <L 2000- 06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

(Shall be punished by the same penalties whoever, in one of the circumstances indicated in article 444, shall have insulted by words, in their capacity or by reason of their functions, a person who is a depositary of authority or of the public force, or having a public character.) <L 27-07-1934, art. 3>

###### Art. 449. When there exists at the time of the offense legal proof of the imputed facts,

if it is established that the accused made the imputation without any motive of public or private interest and with the sole purpose of causing harm, he shall be punished, as guilty of malicious disclosure, by imprisonment from eight days to two months and a fine from twenty-six [euros] to four hundred [euros], or by only one of these penalties. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 450. The offenses provided for by this chapter, committed against

private individuals, with the exception of false accusation, may only be prosecuted upon the complaint of the person who claims to be offended. If the person has died without having filed a complaint or without having renounced it, or if the calumny or defamation was directed against a person after their death, prosecution may only take place upon the complaint of their spouse, descendants or (legal) heirs up to and including the third degree. <L 31- 03-1987, art. 98>

###### Art. 451. No one may allege as grounds for justification or excuse that

the writings, printed materials, images or emblems that are the subject of the prosecution are merely the reproduction of publications made in Belgium or in foreign countries.

###### Art. 452.

<L 10-10-1967, art. 141> Speeches delivered or writings produced before the courts shall not give rise to any criminal prosecution when such speeches or writings relate to the case or to the parties. Calumnious, insulting or defamatory imputations unrelated to the case or to the parties may give rise either to public action or to civil action by the parties or by third parties.

SPECIAL PROVISION.

###### Art. 453. Shall be punished by imprisonment from one month to one year and a fine from

twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros], whoever shall have been guilty of violation of tombs or graves. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01- Art. 453bis.<L 2007-05-10/35, art. 38, 064; In force: 09-06-2007> In the cases provided for by this chapter, the minimum of the correctional penalties provided by these articles may be doubled, when one of the motives of the offense is hatred, contempt or hostility towards a person by reason of their alleged race, skin color, ancestry, national or ethnic origin, nationality, sex, sexual orientation, civil status, birth, age, fortune, religious or philosophical conviction, current or future state of health, disability, language, political conviction, [ trade union conviction,] physical or genetic characteristic or social origin.

(1)<L 2009-12-30/01, art. 114, 074; In force: 31-12-2009>

##### CHAPTER VI. - OF CERTAIN OTHER OFFENSES AGAINST PERSONS.

###### Art. 454. Whoever shall have mixed or caused to be mixed, either with foodstuffs or

beverages, or with any food substances or goods, intended to be sold or distributed, materials that are of a nature to cause death or to seriously damage health, shall be punished by imprisonment from six months to five years and a fine from two hundred [euros] to two thousand [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42,

###### art. 2

, In force: 01-01-2002>.

###### Art. 455. Shall be punished by the penalties provided in the preceding article:

Whoever sells, distributes or exposes for sale any foodstuffs, beverages, food substances or goods, knowing that they contain materials of a nature to cause death or to seriously damage health; Whoever shall have sold or provided these materials, knowing that they were to be used to adulterate food substances or goods.

###### Art. 456. Shall be punished by imprisonment from three months to three years and a

fine from one hundred [euros] to one thousand [euros], whoever shall have in their store, shop or any other place, foodstuffs, beverages, goods or food substances, intended to be sold or distributed, knowing that they contain materials of a nature to cause death or to seriously damage health. <L 2000-06- 26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 457. The mixed foodstuffs, beverages, goods or food substances

shall be seized, confiscated and rendered unusable.

(Paragraph 2 repealed) <L 29-10-1919, art. 90>

He may, in addition, be sentenced to prohibition, in accordance with article 33. The court shall order that the judgment be posted in the places it designates and inserted in full or by extract in the newspapers it indicates; all at the expense of the convicted person.

###### Art. 458.Physicians, surgeons, health officers, pharmacists, midwives

and all other persons who, by status or profession, are depositaries of secrets confided to them, who, except in cases where they are called to give evidence in court (or before a parliamentary commission of inquiry) and [the case where the law, decree or ordinance obliges or authorizes them] to disclose these secrets, have revealed them, shall be punished by imprisonment [of one year to three years and a fine of one hundred euros to one thousand euros or by only one of these penalties]. <L 1996-06-30/34, art. 10, 017; En vigueur : 26-07-1996> <L 2000- 06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

(1)<L 2017-07-06/24, art. 312, 124; En vigueur : 03-08-2017>

Art. 458bis.[ Any person who, by status or profession, is a depositary of secrets and has knowledge thereby of an offense provided for [in articles 371/1] [to 377, 377quater, 379, 380, 383bis, §§ 1er and 2, 392 to 394, 396 to 405ter, 409, 423, 425, 426 and 433quinquies], which has been committed against a minor or against a person who is vulnerable by reason of age, pregnancy, [violence between partners,] [acts of violence perpetrated in the name of culture, custom, religion, tradition or so-called "honor,"] illness, infirmity or physical or mental disability may, without prejudice to the obligations imposed by article 422bis, inform the King's Prosecutor thereof, either when there exists a serious and imminent danger to the physical or mental integrity of the minor or vulnerable person referred to, and he or she is not able, alone or with the help of third parties, to protect that integrity, or when there are indications of a serious and real danger that other minors or vulnerable persons referred to may be victims of the offenses provided for in the aforementioned articles and he or she is not able, alone or with the help of third parties, to protect that integrity.]

(NOTE : par son arrêt n°127/2013 du 26-09-2013, la Cour constitutionnelle a annulé larticle 6 de la loi du 30 novembre 2011, en ce quil sapplique à lavocat dépositaire de confidences de son client, auteur de linfraction qui a été commise au sens de cet article, lorsque ces informations sont susceptibles dincriminer ce client.)

(1)<L 2011-11-30/28, art. 6, 083; En vigueur : 30-01-2012>

(2)<L 2012-02-23/08, art. 2, 085; En vigueur : 01-03-2013>

(3)<L 2014-04-10/24, art. 6, 102; En vigueur : 10-05-2014>

(4)<L 2016-02-01/09, art. 14, 115; En vigueur : 29-02-2016>

(5)<L 2016-05-31/02, art. 11, 118; En vigueur : 18-06-2016>

(6)<L 2018-06-18/09, art. 2, 134; En vigueur : 01-11-2018>

Art. 458ter. [ § 1er. There is no offense when a person who, by status or profession, is a depositary of secrets, communicates them in the context of a consultation organized either by or pursuant to a law, decree or ordinance, or by means of a reasoned authorization from the King's Prosecutor. This consultation may exclusively be organized either with a view to protecting the physical and mental integrity of the person or of third parties, or with a view to preventing the offenses referred to in Title Iter of Book II or offenses committed within the framework of a criminal organization, as defined in article 324bis. The law, decree or ordinance, or the reasoned authorization from the King's Prosecutor, referred to in paragraph 1, shall determine at least who may participate in the consultation, for what purpose and according to what procedures the consultation will take place.

###### § 2. Participants are bound to secrecy with regard to secrets communicated

during the consultation. Any person violating this secrecy shall be punished by the penalties provided for in article 458.

Secrets that are communicated during this consultation may only give rise to criminal prosecution for those offenses for which the consultation was organized.]

(1)<Inséré par L 2017-07-06/24, art. 313, 124; En vigueur : 03-08-2017> Art. 458quater. [ Articles 458bis and 458ter are not applicable to lawyers with regard to the communication of confidential information from their client where such information is likely to expose their client to criminal prosecution.]

(1)<Inséré par L 2017-07-06/24, art. 314, 124; En vigueur : 03-08-2017>

###### Art. 459. Shall be punished by the same penalties employees or agents of the pawn-

shop who have disclosed to persons other than police officers or the judicial authority the names of persons who have deposited or caused to be deposited objects at the establishment.

###### Art. 460. Whoever is convicted of having suppressed a letter entrusted (to a

postal operator), or of having opened it to violate its secrecy, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to one month and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros], or by only one of these penalties, without prejudice to more severe penalties if the guilty party is an official or a government agent or (a member of the personnel of a postal operator or any person acting on its behalf). <AR 1999-06-09/57, art. 27, 026; En vigueur : 18-08-1999> <L 2000- 06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

Art. 460bis.<L 14-01-1928, art. 4> (Shall be punished by the same penalties, whoever has suppressed a copy of writ of which he or she was in possession by application of article 68bis of the Code of Civil Procedure or who has opened, to violate its secrecy, the envelope containing this copy, unless, in the latter case, it concerns the father or mother of a minor child, or the spouse, guardian, administrator, [or curator] of the person concerned.)

(1)<L 2013-03-17/14, art. 151, 101; En vigueur : 01-09-2014 (L 2014-05-12/02,

###### art. 22)>

Art. 460ter.<inséré par L 1998-03-12/39, art. 44 ; En vigueur : 1998-10-02> Any use [ ...] of information obtained by [consulting or obtaining a copy of the] file, which has had the purpose and effect of obstructing the course of the investigation, of infringing the privacy, physical or mental integrity or property of a person cited in the file, is punished by imprisonment of eight days to one year or a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>.

(1)<L 2012-12-27/29, art. 27, 090; En vigueur : 10-02-2013>

#### TITLE IX. - CRIMES AND OFFENSES AGAINST PROPERTY.

##### CHAPTER I. - THEFTS AND EXTORTIONS.

###### Art. 461. Whoever has fraudulently removed a thing that does not belong to

him or her is guilty of theft.

(Assimilated to theft is the act of fraudulently removing another person's thing for momentary use.) <L 25-06-1964, art. 1>

###### Art. 462.Shall give rise only to civil reparations, thefts committed by spouses to the

detriment of their spouses; by a widower or widow, with regard to things that had belonged to the deceased spouse; by descendants to the detriment of their ascendants, by ascendants to the detriment of their descendants, or by relatives by marriage in the same degrees.

[Paragraph 1 is not applicable if these thefts were committed to the detriment of a person vulnerable by reason of age, pregnancy, illness, infirmity or physical or mental disability.]

Any other person who has participated in these thefts or concealed all or part of the stolen objects shall be punished as if [paragraph 1] did not exist.

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 37, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

###### SECTION I. - THEFTS COMMITTED WITHOUT VIOLENCE OR THREATS.

###### Art. 463.Thefts not specified in this chapter shall be punished by

imprisonment of one month to five years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>. (However, in the case provided for by article 461, paragraph 2, the term of imprisonment shall not exceed three years.) <L 25-06-1964, art. 2> [ The minimum sentence shall be three months' imprisonment and a fine of fifty euros if the theft was committed against a person whose particularly vulnerable situation due to age, pregnancy, illness or physical or mental deficiency or infirmity was apparent or known to the perpetrator.]

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 38, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

###### Art. 464. The imprisonment shall be at least three months, if the thief is a

domestic servant or a paid servant, even when he committed the theft against persons whom he did not serve, but who were either in the master's house or in the house to which he accompanied him, or if he is a worker, journeyman or apprentice, in the house, workshop or store of his master, or an individual habitually working in the dwelling where he committed the theft.

###### Art. 465. In the cases of the preceding articles, the guilty parties may, in addition,

be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33 (...). <L 09-04-1930,

###### art. 32>

###### Art. 466. Attempts of the thefts mentioned in the preceding articles shall be

punished by imprisonment of eight days to three years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to three hundred [euros] <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-

###### Art. 467. (Voir NOTE sous TITRE) Theft shall be punished by (imprisonment of five years to ten

years) : <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 75, 041; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

If it was committed by means of breaking and entering, scaling or false keys; If it was committed by a public official by means of his office; If the guilty parties or one of them assumed the title or insignia of a public official, or alleged a false order from the public authority.

###### SECTION II. - THEFTS COMMITTED BY MEANS OF VIOLENCE OR THREATS AND

EXTORTIONS.

###### Art. 468. (Voir NOTE sous TITRE) Whoever commits a theft by means of

violence or threats shall be punished by (imprisonment of five years to ten years). <L 2003- 01-23/42, art. 75, 041; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 469. Assimilated to theft committed by means of violence or threats is the case where

the thief, caught in the act, used violence or made threats, either to maintain possession of the stolen objects or to ensure his escape.

###### Art. 470. Shall be punished by the penalties provided for in article 468, as if he had committed a

theft with violence or threats, whoever extorted, by means of violence or threats, either funds, securities, movable objects, obligations, notes, promises, receipts, or the signature or delivery of any document containing or effecting an obligation, disposition or discharge.

###### Art. 471.(Voir NOTE sous TITRE)

<L 2001-12-11/50, art. 3, 033; En vigueur : 17- 02-2002> In the cases provided for in articles 468, 469 and 470, the penalty shall be that of imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years: if the offence was committed with breaking and entering, scaling or false keys; if the offence was committed by a public official by means of his office; if the guilty parties, or one of them, assumed the title or insignia of a public official or alleged a false order from the public authority; if the offence was committed at night; if the offence was committed by two or more persons; if the guilty party used a vehicle or any other motorized or non-motorized device to facilitate the offence or to ensure his escape;

[ if the offence was committed against a person whose particularly vulnerable situation due to age, pregnancy, illness or physical or mental deficiency or infirmity was apparent or known to the perpetrator.]

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 39, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

###### Art. 472. (Voir NOTE sous TITRE)

<L 2001-12-11/50, art. 4, 033; En vigueur : 17- 02-2002> In the cases provided for in articles 468, 469 and 470, the penalty shall be that of imprisonment of fifteen years to twenty years: if the offence was committed with two of the circumstances mentioned in article if weapons or objects resembling them were used or shown, or if the guilty party made it believed that he was armed; if the guilty party used inhibiting or toxic substances to commit the offence or ensure his escape; if, to facilitate the offence or to ensure his escape, the guilty party used a vehicle or any other motorized or non-motorized device, obtained by means of a crime or an offence; if, to facilitate the offence or to ensure his escape, the guilty party used a motor vehicle or any other motorized device equipped with insignia or devices capable of creating confusion with a motor vehicle or any other motorized device belonging to law enforcement.

###### Art. 473.(Voir NOTE sous TITRE)

<L 02-07-1975, art. 4> In the cases provided for in articles 468, 469, 470 and 471, the penalty shall be that (of imprisonment) of fifteen years to twenty years, if the violence or threats caused either an apparently incurable illness, or [ incapacity for personal work of more than four months] , or the complete loss of use of an organ, or serious mutilation. <L 2002-06- 14/42, art. 8, 036; En vigueur : 24-08-2002>

The same penalty shall apply if the malefactors subjected the persons to (acts referred to in article 417ter, paragraph one); <L 2002-06-14/42, art. 8, 036; En vigueur : 24-08-2002>

In the cases provided for in article 472, the penalty shall be increased (to imprisonment of twenty years to thirty years). <L 2002-06-14/42, art. 8, 036; En vigueur : 24-08-2002> (1)<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 23, 114; En vigueur : 29-02-2016>

###### Art. 474. (Voir NOTE sous TITRE) If the violence or threats exercised without

intention to cause death nevertheless caused it, the guilty parties shall be sentenced (to imprisonment of twenty years to thirty years). <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 76, 041; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

(Alinéa 2 abrogé) <L 02-07-1975, art. 6>

###### Art. 475. Murder committed to facilitate theft or extortion, or to

ensure impunity therefor, shall be punished (by life imprisonment). <L 1996-07-10/42, art.

15, 018; En vigueur : 11-08-1996>

###### Art. 476.The penalties provided for by [ articles 473 to 475] shall be applied, even

if the consummation of the theft or extortion was prevented by circumstances independent of the will of the guilty parties.

(1)<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 28, 114; En vigueur : 29-02-2016>

SECTION IIbis. - (THEFTS AND EXTORTIONS OF NUCLEAR MATERIALS). <L 17-04- 1986, art. 2>

###### Art. 477. (Voir NOTE sous TITRE)

<L 17-04-1986, art. 2> Theft of nuclear materials is punished by (imprisonment of five years to ten years). <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 77, 041; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

Art. 477bis. (Voir NOTE sous TITRE) <L 17-04-1986, art. 2> Theft of nuclear materials is punished (by imprisonment) of ten years to fifteen years : <L 2003-01-23/42,

###### art. 78

, 041; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

1° if it was committed with the aid of violence or threats;

2° if it was committed with breaking and entering, scaling or false keys;

3° if it was committed by a public official by means of his functions;

4° if the culprits or one of them took the title or insignia of a public official or alleged a false order of the public authority.

Art. 477ter. (Voir NOTE sous TITRE) <L 17-04-1986, art. 2> The extortion of nuclear materials with the aid of violence or threats is punished (de la réclusion) by ten to fifteen years. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 78, 041; En vigueur : 13-03-2003> Art. 477quater. <L 17-04-1986, art. 2> Assimilated to the theft or extortion of nuclear materials committed with the aid of violence or threats is the case where the thief or extorter, caught in the act, exercised violence or made threats, either to maintain possession of the nuclear materials taken, or to ensure his escape.

Art. 477quinquies. (Voir NOTE sous TITRE) <L 17-04-1986, art. 2> The theft or extortion of nuclear materials with the aid of violence or threats, as well as the act referred to in Article 477quater, are punished (de la réclusion) by fifteen to twenty years: <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 78, 041; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

1° if they were committed with breaking and entering, scaling or false keys;

2° if they were committed by a public official by means of his functions; 3° if the culprits, or one of them, took the title or insignia of a public official or alleged a false order of the public authority;

4° if they were committed at night;

5° if they were committed by two or more persons;

6° if the culprit used a vehicle or any other motorized or non-motorized device to facilitate the extortion or to ensure his escape.

Art. 477sexies.(Voir NOTE sous TITRE) <L 17-04-1986, art. 2> § 1. The theft or extortion of nuclear materials with the aid of violence or threats, as well as the act referred to in Article 477quater, are punished (de la réclusion de vingt ans à trente ans): <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 79, 041;En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

1° if they were committed with two of the circumstances mentioned in Article 477quinquies;

2° if weapons or objects resembling them were used or displayed or if the culprit led others to believe that he was armed;

3° if the culprit used inhibiting or toxic substances to commit the act or to ensure his escape;

4° if the culprit used a stolen vehicle or any other motorized or non-motorized device that was stolen to facilitate the act or to ensure his escape;

5° if, to facilitate the act or to ensure his escape, the culprit used a motor vehicle or any other motorized device fitted with insignia or devices likely to create confusion with a motor vehicle or any other motorized device belonging to the law enforcement forces.

###### § 2. The same acts are punished by the same penalty:

1° if the violence or threats caused either an apparently incurable disease, or an [incapacity for personal work of more than four months], or the complete loss of use of an organ, or a serious mutilation;

2° if the malefactors (have practiced on persons acts referred to in Article 417ter, first paragraph;) <L 2002-06-14/42, art. 9, 036; En vigueur : 24-08-2002> 3° if the violence or threats exercised without intent to cause death, nevertheless caused it.

###### § 3. The penalty provided for in § 2 is applied even when the completion of the

theft or extortion was prevented by circumstances independent of the will of the culprits.

(1)<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 23, 114; En vigueur : 29-02-2016>

###### SECTION III. - (ON THE MEANING OF CERTAIN TERMS USED IN THIS

CODE). <L 02-07-1975, art. 5>

###### Art. 478. Theft committed during the night is theft committed more than one hour before

sunrise and more than one hour after sunset.

###### Art. 479. Deemed an inhabited house is any building, any apartment, any

dwelling, any lodge, any cabin, even mobile, or other place used for habitation.

###### Art. 480. Deemed dependencies of an inhabited house are the courtyards, lower

courtyards, gardens and all other enclosed land, as well as the barns, stables and all other buildings enclosed therein, whatever their use, even when they form a particular enclosure within the general enclosure.

###### Art. 481. Mobile pens intended to contain livestock in the countryside, in

whatever manner they are made, are deemed dependencies of an inhabited house when they are established on the same piece of land with the mobile cabins or other shelters intended for the guardians.

###### Art. 482. Included in the word weapons are the objects designated in Article 135 of

this Code.

###### Art. 483. By violence the law means acts of physical coercion exercised on

persons.

By threats the law means all means of moral coercion through the fear of an imminent harm.

###### Art. 484. Breaking and entering consists in forcing, breaking, degrading, demolishing or removing

any kind of external or internal enclosure of a house, building, any construction or its dependencies, a boat, a railway car, a vehicle; in forcing wardrobes or closed furniture intended to remain in place and to protect the effects they contain.

###### Art. 485. Assimilated to theft with breaking and entering are:

The removal of the furniture referred to in the preceding article;

Theft committed by means of breaking seals.

###### Art. 486. Qualified as scaling is:

Any entry into houses, buildings, courtyards, lower courtyards, any edifices, gardens, parks, enclosures, executed over the walls, doors, roofs or any other kind of enclosure;

Entry through an underground opening other than that which was established to serve as an entrance.

###### Art. 487. Qualified as false keys are:

All hooks, lock picks, skeleton keys, imitated, counterfeited or altered keys; Keys that were not intended by the owner, tenant, innkeeper or lodger for the locks, padlocks or any fastenings to which the culprit has applied them;

Keys that are lost, mislaid or stolen that will have served to commit the theft. However, the use of false keys will constitute an aggravating circumstance only if it took place to open objects whose breaking and entering would have led to an aggravation of penalty.

Art. 487bis.[ § 1er. By nuclear materials is meant the nuclear materials referred to in Article 1, 8th indent, of the Act of 15 April 1994 on the protection of the population and the environment against the dangers resulting from ionizing radiation and on the Federal Agency for Nuclear Control.

###### § 2. By radioactive material is meant any nuclear material or other

radioactive substance containing nuclides that disintegrate spontaneously, a process accompanied by the emission of one or more types of ionizing radiation such as alpha, beta, gamma and neutron radiation, and which could, due to their radiological or fissile properties, cause death, serious bodily harm or substantial damage to property or the environment.

###### § 3. By nuclear installation is meant:

a) any nuclear reactor, including a reactor on board a ship, a vehicle, an aircraft or a spacecraft as a source of energy used to propel said ship, vehicle, aircraft or spacecraft, or for any other purpose;

b) any device or transport equipment for the purpose of producing, storing, reprocessing or transporting radioactive materials.


This excerpt sits under: CODE PENAL > Livre II > TITRE IX. - CRIMES ET DELITS CONTRE LES PROPRIETES. > CHAPITRE I. - DES VOLS ET DES EXTORSIONS.

###### § 4. By device is meant:

a) any nuclear explosive device; or

b) any radioactive material dispersal device or any radiation-emitting device which, by virtue of its radiological properties, causes death, serious bodily injury or substantial damage to property or the environment.

###### § 5.

By operator of a facility where nuclear materials are produced, processed, used, handled, stored or permanently disposed of, is meant any natural or legal person who assumes responsibility for such a facility.

###### § 6. By person external to a facility where nuclear materials are

produced, processed, used, handled, stored or permanently disposed of, is meant the natural person who is not bound, directly or indirectly, by an employment contract, an internship or training agreement or a contract for the provision of work or services, to a facility where nuclear materials are produced, processed, used, handled, stored or permanently disposed of.]

(1)<L 2013-05-23/09, art. 3, 093; En vigueur : 16-06-2013>

SPECIAL PROVISION.

###### Art. 488. Anyone who has fraudulently counterfeited or altered keys shall be

sentenced to imprisonment of three months to two years and to a fine of twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-
If the offender is a locksmith by profession, he shall be punished by imprisonment of two years to five years and a fine of two hundred [euros] to one thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

CHAPITRE Ibis. - [ Physical protection of nuclear materials and other radioactive materials]

(1)<L 2013-05-23/09, art. 4, 093; En vigueur : 16-06-2013>

Art. 488bis.(Voir NOTE sous TITRE) <L 17-04-1986, art. 4> § 1. Anyone who, intentionally and without being authorized by the competent authority or outside the conditions provided for upon authorization, receives, acquires, possesses, uses, alters, transfers, abandons, transports or disperses nuclear materials is punished by (imprisonment of five years to ten years). <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 80, 041; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

###### § 2. The penalty is (imprisonment) of ten years to fifteen years if the act has caused to

another: <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 80, 041; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

1° either an apparently incurable disease, a [ personal incapacity to work of more than four months] , the loss of absolute use of an organ or serious mutilation;

2° The destruction in whole or in part of buildings, bridges, dikes, roads, railways, locks, warehouses, yards, sheds, ships, boats, aircraft or other engineering works, or constructions belonging to another.

###### § 3. If the act committed without intention to cause death has nevertheless caused it, the

offender is punished by (imprisonment) of fifteen to twenty years. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 80, 041; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

[ § 4. Is punished by imprisonment of fifteen to twenty years, anyone who, intentionally and without being authorized by the competent authority or outside the conditions provided for upon authorization, commits an act directed against nuclear materials or against a facility in which nuclear materials are produced, processed, used, handled, stored or permanently disposed of, or an act disrupting the operation of such a facility, if, by these acts and as a result of exposure to radiation or the release of radioactive substances:

1° he intentionally causes or knows that he may cause death or serious injury to another or substantial damage to property or the environment; or

2° he intentionally compels a natural or legal person, an international organization or a government to perform an act or to refrain from doing so.]

(1)<L 2013-05-23/09, art. 5, 093; En vigueur : 16-06-2013>

(2)<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 20, 114; En vigueur : 29-02-2016>

Art. 488ter. [ Is punished by imprisonment of ten to fifteen years, anyone who, intentionally and without being authorized by the competent authority or outside the conditions provided for upon authorization, possesses, manufactures, uses in any manner radioactive materials other than nuclear or radioactive devices or commits an act directed against radioactive materials other than nuclear or radioactive devices, if, by these acts:

1° he intentionally causes or knows that he may cause death or serious injury to another or substantial damage to property or the environment; or

2° he intentionally compels a natural or legal person, an international organization or a government to perform an act or to refrain from doing so.]

(1)<Inséré par L 2013-05-23/09, art. 6, 093; En vigueur : 16-06-2013>

Art. 488quater. [ Is punished by imprisonment of five to ten years, anyone who demands, intentionally and without being authorized by the competent authority or outside the conditions provided for upon authorization, the delivery of radioactive materials or devices or nuclear facilities by resorting to a threat, in circumstances that make it credible, or to the use of force.]

(1)<Inséré par L 2013-05-23/09, art. 7, 093; En vigueur : 16-06-2013>

Art. 488quinquies. [ Is punished by imprisonment of one month to two years and a fine of one hundred euros to fifty thousand euros or one of these penalties only, any person external to a facility where nuclear materials are produced, processed, used, handled, stored or permanently disposed of who, without order of the authority and outside the cases where the law permits it, enters or attempts to enter the parts of such a facility for which access is limited to the persons referred to in Article 8bis, §§ 1 to 4, of the law of 11 December 1998 on classification and security clearances, certificates and opinions, either without having been authorized by the operator or his agent, or by resorting to fraudulent maneuvers likely to mislead the operator or his agent about his legitimacy to enter these parts of the facility.]

(1)<Inséré par L 2013-05-23/09, art. 8, 093; En vigueur : 16-06-2013>

##### CHAPITRE II. - FRAUDS.

###### SECTION I. [ - Offences related to insolvency.]

(1)<L 2017-08-11/14, art. 4, 128; En vigueur : 01-05-2018>

###### Art. 489.(See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 1997-08-08/80, art. 118, 019; In force: 01-01-1998> Are punished with imprisonment from one month to one year and a fine from one hundred [euros] to one hundred thousand [euros] or one of these penalties only, the

[ enterprises referred to in [ Article I.1, paragraph 1, 1°] , of the Code of Economic Law or the directors, de jure or de facto, of companies or legal entities] in a state of bankruptcy, who have: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002> 1° entered into, for the benefit of third parties, without sufficient consideration, excessively large commitments given the financial situation of the enterprise;

2° without legitimate impediment, failed to perform the obligations prescribed by Article [ XX.146 of the Code of Economic Law] .

(1)<L 2017-08-11/14, art. 5, 128; In force: 01-05-2018>

(2)<L 2018-04-15/14, art. 4, 135; In force: 01-11-2018>

Art. 489bis.<inserted by L 1997-08-08/80, art. 119, 019; In force: 01-01-1998> Are punished with imprisonment from one month to two years and a fine from one hundred [euros] to five hundred thousand [euros] or one of these penalties only, the persons referred to in Article 489, who have: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01- 1° with the intention of delaying the declaration of bankruptcy, made purchases to resell below market price or who have resorted to loans, circulations of bills and other ruinous means of procuring funds;

2° fabricated expenses or losses or have been unable to justify the existence or use of all or part of the assets, as they appear in the accounting documents and books at the date of cessation of payment and of all property of any nature whatsoever obtained subsequently;

3° with the intention of delaying the declaration of bankruptcy, paid or favoured a creditor to the detriment of the general body of creditors;

4° with the same intention, failed to make the confession of bankruptcy within the period prescribed by Article [ XX.102 of the Code of Economic Law] ; knowingly failed to provide, on the occasion of the confession of bankruptcy, the information required by Article [ XX.103 of the same Code] ; knowingly provided inaccurate information on the occasion of the confession of bankruptcy or subsequently to requests made by the examining judge or by the trustees.

(1)<L 2017-08-11/14, art. 6, 128; In force: 01-05-2018>

Art. 489ter.<inserted by L 1997-08-08/80, art. 120, 019; In force: 01-01-1998> Are punished with imprisonment from one month to five years and a fine from one hundred [euros] to five hundred thousand [euros], the persons referred to in Article 489 who, with fraudulent intent or with intent to harm, have:

1° misappropriated or concealed part of the assets; <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

2° removed, in whole or in part, the accounting books or documents referred to in [ Book III, Chapter 2, of the Code of Economic Law] ; the attempt of these offences is punished with imprisonment from one month to three years and a fine from one hundred [euros] to five hundred thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01- Those guilty of these offences or their attempt may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with Article 33.

(1)<L 2017-08-11/14, art. 7, 128; In force: 01-05-2018>

Art. 489quater.<inserted by L 1997-08-08/80, art. 121, 019; In force: 01-01- 1998> The public prosecution relating to the offences referred to in Articles 489, 489bis and 489ter is pursued independently of any action that could be pursued before the commercial court. The state of bankruptcy may not, however, be contested before the criminal judge, if this state has been the subject of a decision of the commercial court or the Court of Appeal, having become final, at the end of proceedings to which the accused was a party, either personally or as representative of [ the enterprise] in bankruptcy.

(1)<L 2017-08-11/14, art. 8, 128; In force: 01-05-2018>

Art. 489quinquies.<inserted by L 1997-08-08/80, art. 122, 019; In force: 01-01- 1998> Are punished with imprisonment from one month to two years and a fine from one hundred [euros] to five hundred thousand [euros] or one of these penalties only, those who, fraudulently, have: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002> 1° in the interest [ of the enterprise in bankruptcy] even in the absence of intervention by [ the latter or the directors, de jure or de facto, of this company or legal entity] , removed, concealed or received all or part of the assets; 2° presented in the bankruptcy and affirmed, either in their own name or through interposed persons, fictitious or exaggerated claims.

(1)<L 2017-08-11/14, art. 9, 128; In force: 01-05-2018>

Art. 489sexies. <inserted by L 1997-08-08/80, art. 123, 019; In force: 01-01- 1998> Is punished with imprisonment from one month to five years and a fine from one hundred [euros] to five hundred thousand [euros], the trustee who has been guilty of embezzlement in his management. He is, in addition, sentenced to restitution and damages owed to the general body of creditors. The guilty party may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with Article 33. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 490.

<L 1997-08-08/80, art. 124, 019; En vigueur : 01-01-1998> The courts pronouncing a conviction to a sentence of imprisonment pursuant to articles 489, 489bis, and 489ter, shall order that their decisions be published, by extract, at the expense of the convicted person, in the Belgian Official Gazette.

This extract contains:

1° the surname, first names, place and date of birth as well as the address and [enterprise] number of the convicted persons and, where applicable, the business name or denomination and registered office of the [enterprises] declared bankrupt of which they are the de jure or de facto directors;

2° the date of the judgment or decision of conviction and the court that pronounced it;

3° the offences that gave rise to the convictions and the sentences pronounced; when, by reason of unity of intention, a single sentence has been pronounced for one of the aforementioned offences and other offences, all offences punished by that single sentence shall be mentioned.

(1)<L 2009-12-30/14, art. 2, 077; En vigueur : 25-01-2010>

(2)<L 2017-08-11/14, art. 10, 128; En vigueur : 01-05-2018>

Art. 490bis. <L 1997-08-08/50, art. 141, 019; En vigueur : 01-01-1998> Shall be punished by imprisonment from one month to two years and a fine from one hundred [euros] to five hundred thousand [euros], or by one of these penalties only, anyone who fraudulently organized their insolvency and has not fulfilled the obligations with which they are charged. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002> The organization of their insolvency by the debtor may be inferred from any circumstance of a nature to reveal their intention to render themselves insolvent.

With regard to the third party co-author or accomplice of the offence, the public prosecution is extinguished if they restore the property that had been handed over to them.

Art. 490ter. [ The debtor shall be punished by imprisonment from one month to two years and a fine from five euros to one hundred twenty-five thousand euros or by one of these penalties only:

1° if, in order to obtain or facilitate the judicial reorganization procedure, they have, in any manner whatsoever, voluntarily concealed part of their assets or liabilities, or exaggerated those assets or minimized those liabilities;

2° if they have knowingly and voluntarily caused or allowed one or more supposed creditors or creditors whose claims have been exaggerated to intervene in the deliberations;

3° if they have knowingly and voluntarily omitted one or more creditors from the list of creditors;

4° if they have knowingly and voluntarily made or allowed to be made to the court or to an officer of justice inaccurate or incomplete statements on the state of their affairs or on the prospects for reorganization.]

(1)<Inséré par L 2017-08-11/14, art. 11, 128; En vigueur : 01-05-2018>

Art. 490quater. [ Shall be punished by imprisonment from one month to two years and a fine from 5 euros to 125,000 euros, (i) those who fraudulently, without being creditors, took part in the vote provided for in article XX.78 of the Code of Economic Law, (ii) those who, being creditors, exaggerated their claims and (iii) those who stipulated, either with the debtor or with any other persons, particular advantages to influence the direction of their vote on the reorganization plan or who concluded a particular agreement by virtue of which an advantage would result in their favor at the expense of the debtor's assets.]

(1)<Inséré par L 2017-08-11/14, art. 12, 128; En vigueur : 01-05-2018>

###### SECTION II. - BREACH OF TRUST.

###### Art. 491. Anyone who has fraudulently either misappropriated or dissipated, to the detriment of others, effects, monies, goods, notes, receipts, writings of any nature containing or effecting an obligation or discharge and which had been handed over to them on condition of returning them or making a specified use or employment thereof, shall be punished by imprisonment from one month to five years and a fine from twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002> The offender may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33.

###### Art. 492. The provision of article 462 shall be applicable to the offence provided for by the preceding article.

Art. 492bis. <inséré par L 1997-08-08/80, art. 142, En vigueur : 01-01-1998> Shall be punished by imprisonment from one month to five years and a fine from one hundred [euros] (to five hundred thousand [euros]), the de jure or de facto directors of commercial and civil companies as well as non-profit associations who, with fraudulent intent and for personal purposes, directly or indirectly, made use of the property or credit of the legal entity in a manner they knew to be significantly detrimental to its proprietary interests and to those of its creditors or members. <Erratum, voir M.B. 07.02.2001, p. 3294 > <L 2000-06- 26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

The offenders may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33.

###### Art. 493.(Shall be punished by imprisonment from three months to five years and a fine from twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros], anyone who has taken advantage of the needs, weaknesses, (passions or ignorance) of a minor [ or of a person whose situation of vulnerability by reason of age, a state of pregnancy, an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental deficiency was apparent or known to the perpetrator of the acts,] to cause them to subscribe, to their detriment, obligations, receipts, discharges, negotiable instruments or any other binding instruments, in whatever form this transaction was made or disguised.) <AR148 18-03-1935, art. 1> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002> The offender may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33.

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 40, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

###### Art. 494.

<AR148 18-03-1935, art. 2> Is punished by imprisonment from one month to one year and a fine from one thousand [euros] to ten thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only, anyone who, habitually taking advantage of the weaknesses or passions of the borrower, causes to be promised to them, by reason of a loan of a sum of money, contracted in whatever form, for themselves or for others, an interest or other advantages exceeding the legal interest. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur Is punished by the same penalties, anyone who, habitually taking advantage of the needs or ignorance of the borrower, causes to be promised to them, by reason of a loan of a sum of money, contracted in whatever form, for themselves or for others, an interest or other advantages manifestly exceeding the normal interest and the coverage of the risks of that loan.

In the cases provided for in this article, the judge, at the request of any injured party, reduces their obligations in accordance with article 1907ter of the Civil Code.

###### Art. 495.

Anyone who, after having produced, in a judicial dispute, any title, document or memorandum, has maliciously or fraudulently misappropriated it, in any manner whatsoever, shall be punished by a fine from twenty-six [euros] to three hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

This penalty shall be pronounced by the court seized of the dispute. Art. 495bis. <L 10-10-1967, art. 144> Shall be punished by imprisonment from eight days to two years and a fine from twenty-six [euros] to one thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only anyone who, being in possession of a document the production of which in court has been ordered by a judgment, has fraudulently destroyed, altered or concealed that document. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur

###### SECTION III. - FRAUD AND DECEIT.

###### Art. 496.Whoever, with the aim of appropriating a thing belonging to another,

shall have obtained the delivery or transfer of funds, movable property, bonds, receipts, discharges, either by making use of false names or false qualities, or by employing fraudulent maneuvers to persuade of the existence of false enterprises, of an imaginary power or credit, to give rise to the hope or fear of a success, an accident or any other chimerical event, or to otherwise abuse confidence or credulity, shall be punished by imprisonment from one month to five years and by a fine from twenty-six [euros] to three thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

[ If the acts referred to in the preceding paragraph were committed to the detriment of a person whose situation of vulnerability by reason of age, a state of pregnancy, an illness, an infirmity or a physical or mental deficiency was apparent or known to the perpetrator, the latter shall be punished by imprisonment from six months to five years and by a fine from twenty-six euros to three thousand euros.]

(The attempt of the offense provided for in paragraph 1 shall be punished by imprisonment from eight days to three years and by a fine from twenty-six [euros] to two thousand [euros].) <L 1993-06-16/35, art. 1, 1°, 009; En vigueur : 03-08-1993> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

(In the cases provided for in the preceding paragraphs, the offender may, in addition, be sentenced to prohibition, in accordance with Article 33.) <L 1993-06-16/35, art. 1, 2°, 009; En vigueur : 03-08-1993>

(1)<L 2011-11-26/19, art. 41, 084; En vigueur : 02-02-2012>

###### Art. 497. Shall be punished by imprisonment from eight days to three years and by a

fine from fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros] : <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

(Those who shall have, with fraudulent intent, given or attempted to give to a coin having legal tender in Belgium or abroad the appearance of a coin of higher value;

Those who shall have issued or attempted to issue coins to which the appearance of coins of a higher value has been given or who, with the aim of putting them into circulation, shall have introduced them into the country or attempted to introduce them.) <L 12-07-1932, art. 1, 12°>

Those who shall have issued or attempted to issue as coins pieces of metal bearing no monetary stamp.

Art. 497bis. <L 12-07-1932, art. 1, 13°> Shall be punished by imprisonment from eight days to six months and by a fine from twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros], those who, with the aim of putting them into circulation, shall have received or obtained coins to which the appearance of coins of a higher value has been given. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

The attempt shall be punished by imprisonment from eight days to three months and by a fine from twenty-six [euros] to one thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur

###### Art. 498. Shall be punished by imprisonment from one month to one year and by a fine from

fifty [euros] to one thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only, whoever shall have deceived the purchaser:

As to the identity of the thing sold, by fraudulently delivering a thing other than the determined object on which the transaction bore; <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

As to the nature or origin of the thing sold by selling or delivering a thing similar in appearance to that which he purchased or which he believed he was purchasing.

###### Art. 499.

<L 17-06-1896, art. unique> Shall be sentenced to imprisonment from eight days to one year and to a fine from twenty-six [euros] to one thousand [euros] or to one of these penalties only, those who, by fraudulent maneuvers, shall have deceived: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2,En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

1° The purchaser or the seller as to the quantity of things sold;

2° The parties engaged in a contract for work or one of them, either as to the quantity, or as to the quality of work provided, when, in this second case, the determination of the quality of work must serve to fix the amount of wages.

###### Art. 500. Shall be punished by imprisonment from eight days to one year and by a

fine from fifty [euros] to one thousand [euros], or by one of these penalties only: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

Those who shall have adulterated or caused to be adulterated (foodstuffs) intended to be sold or retailed;

Those who shall have sold, retailed or exposed for sale these items, knowing that they were adulterated; <L 24-01-1977, art. 24, 1°>

Those who, by posters or by notices, printed or not, shall have maliciously or fraudulently propagated or disclosed processes of adulteration of these same items.

###### Art. 501. Shall be punished by imprisonment from eight days to six months and by a

fine from twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros], or by one of these penalties only, the person in whose possession (foodstuffs) intended to be sold or retailed are found, and who knows that they are adulterated. <L 24-01-1977, art. 24, 2°> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

Art. 501bis. <L 20-06-1964, art. 15> Shall be punished by imprisonment from eight days to three months and by a fine from twenty-six to three hundred [euros] or by one of these penalties only whoever, without the fraudulent intent, required by Article 500, shall have sold, retailed, or exposed for sale adulterated (foodstuffs). <L 24-01-1977, art. 24, 3°> <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 502. In the cases provided for (by Articles 500 and 501), the court may

order that the judgment be posted in the places it shall designate and inserted, in whole or in extract, in the newspapers it shall indicate; all at the expense of the convicted person. <L 24-01-1977, art. 24, 4°>

(Paragraph 2 repealed) <L 29-10-1919, art. 90>

###### Art. 503.

<L 24-01-1977, art. 25> The adulterated foodstuffs found in the possession of the offender shall be seized and confiscated.

However, when the adulteration has the effect of rendering these foodstuffs unfit for consumption and when by reason of their nature or condition, they are not susceptible of preservation, they shall be destroyed or denatured after sampling by the reporting officer, assisted by an official provided for by Article 11 of the law relating to the protection of consumer health with regard to foodstuffs and other products, who shall jointly sign the reports of seizure and destruction or denaturing of these foodstuffs. Confiscation shall be pronounced in any event.

The foodstuffs which, notwithstanding the adulteration, remain fit for consumption may be handed over to a social welfare institution dependent on a subordinate administration, either immediately after sampling if they are foodstuffs not susceptible of preservation, or, if they are susceptible of preservation, after judicial decision pronouncing confiscation.

###### Art. 504. The provision of article 462 shall be applicable to the offences provided for by

articles 496, 498 and 499.

SECTION IIIBIS. - <Insérée par L 1999-02-10/39, art. 5; En vigueur : 02-04-1999> Private corruption.

Art. 504bis.<Inséré par L 1999-02-10/39, art. 5; En vigueur : 02-04-1999> § 1er. Passive private corruption is constituted by the act of a person who has the capacity of director or manager of a legal entity, agent or employee of a legal or natural person, [to solicit, accept or receive], directly or through intermediaries, an offer, a promise or an advantage of any nature, for himself or for a third party, to do or to refrain from doing an act of his function or facilitated by his function, without the knowledge and without the authorization, as the case may be, of the Board of Directors or the General Meeting, the principal or the employer.

###### § 2. Active private corruption is constituted by the act of proposing, directly or

through intermediaries, to a person who has the capacity of director or manager of a legal entity, agent or employee of a legal or natural person, an offer, a promise or an advantage of any nature, for himself or for a third party, to do or to refrain from doing an act of his function or facilitated by his function, without the knowledge and without the authorization, as the case may be, of the Board of Directors or the General Meeting, the principal or the employer.

(1)<L 2016-02-05/11, art. 29, 114; En vigueur : 29-02-2016>

Art. 504ter. <Inséré par L 1999-02-10/39, art. 5; En vigueur : 02-04-1999> § 1er. In the case of private corruption, the penalty shall be imprisonment of six months to two years and a fine of 100 [euros] to 10,000 [euros] or one of these penalties. <L 2000- 06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur :01-01-2002>

###### § 2. In the case where the solicitation referred to in article 504bis, § 1er, is followed by a

proposal referred to in article 504bis, § 2, as well as in the case where the proposal referred to in article 504bis, § 2, is accepted, the penalty shall be imprisonment of six months to three years and a fine of 100 [euros] to 50,000 [euros] or one of these penalties. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002> SECTION IIIbis. - Computer fraud. <inséré par L 2000-11-28/34, art. 5; En vigueur : 13-02-2001>

Art. 504quater. <inséré par L 2000-11-28/34, art. 5; En vigueur : 13-02-2001>§ 1er. (Any person who seeks to procure, for himself or for another, with fraudulent intent, an illegal economic advantage) by introducing into a computer system, by modifying or deleting data that is stored, processed or transmitted by a computer system, or by modifying by any technological means (the normal use) of data in a computer system, is punished by imprisonment of six months to five years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to one hundred thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002> <L 2006-05-15/46,

###### art. 4

, 059; En vigueur : 22-09-2006>

###### § 2. The attempt to commit the offence referred to in § 1er is punished by imprisonment

of six months to three years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to fifty thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### § 3. The penalties provided for in §§ 1er and 2 are doubled if an offence against one

of these provisions is committed within five years following the pronouncement of a conviction for one of these offences or for one of the offences referred to in articles 210bis, 259bis, 314bis or in Title IXbis.

###### SECTION IV. - (Receiving and other operations relating to things obtained from

an offence.) <L 1990-07-17/30, art. 4, 004; En vigueur : 25-08-1990>

###### Art. 505.

<L 1995-04-07/57, art. 7, 004; En vigueur : 20-05-1995> Shall be punished by imprisonment of fifteen days to five years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to one hundred thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only: <L 2000-06-26/42,

###### art. 2

, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

1° those who have received, in whole or in part, things removed, misappropriated or obtained by means of a crime or an offence;

2° (those who have purchased, received in exchange or gratuitously, possessed, kept or managed things referred to in article 42, 3°, when they knew or should have known the origin of these things at the beginning of these operations;) <L 2007-05-10/63,

###### art. 2

, 071; En vigueur : 01-09-2007>

3° those who have converted or transferred things referred to in article 42, 3°, with the purpose of concealing or disguising their illicit origin or helping any person who is involved in the commission of the offence from which these things originate, to escape the legal consequences of their acts;

4° (those who have concealed or disguised the nature, origin, location, disposition, movement or ownership of the things referred to in article 42, 3°, when they knew or should have known the origin of these things at the beginning of these operations.) <L 2007-05-10/63, art. 2, 071; En vigueur : 01-09-2007>

(The offences referred to in paragraph 1, 3° and 4°, exist even if their perpetrator is also the perpetrator, co-perpetrator or accomplice of the offence from which the things referred to in article 42, 3° originate. The offences referred to in paragraph 1, 1° and 2°. exist even if their perpetrator is also the perpetrator, co-perpetrator or accomplice of the offence from which the things referred to in article 42, 3° originate, when this offence was committed abroad and cannot be prosecuted in Belgium.) <L 2007-05-10/63, art. 2, 071; En vigueur : 01-09-2007>

(Except with regard to the perpetrator, co-perpetrator or accomplice of the offence from which the things referred to in article 42, 3° originate, the offences referred to in paragraph 1, 2° and 4°, relate exclusively, in tax matters, to acts committed in the context [ of serious tax fraud, organized or not] .

The organizations and persons referred to in articles 2, 2bis and 2ter of the law of 11 January 1993 on the prevention of the use of the financial system for the purposes of money laundering and terrorist financing, may rely on the preceding paragraph insofar as, with regard to the facts referred to therein, they have complied with the obligation provided for in [ article 28] of the law of 11 January 1993 which regulates the procedures for the communication of information to the Financial Information Processing Unit.) <L 2007-05-10/63, art. 2, 071; En vigueur : 01-09-2007>

The things referred to (in paragraph 1, 1°) of this article constitute the object (of the offence covered by this provision), within the meaning of article 42, 1°, and shall be confiscated, even if ownership thereof does not belong to the convicted person, without (this penalty) being able however to prejudice the rights of third parties over the property liable to be the object of confiscation. <L 2007-05-10/63, art. 2, 071; En vigueur : 01-09-2007>

(The things referred to in paragraph 1, 3° and 4°, constitute the object of the offences covered by these provisions, within the meaning of article 42, 1°, and shall be confiscated, in respect of each of the perpetrators, co-perpetrators or accomplices of these offences, even if ownership thereof does not belong to the convicted person, without this penalty being able however to prejudice the rights of third parties over the property liable to be the object of confiscation. If these things cannot be found in the estate of the convicted person, the judge shall proceed to their monetary valuation and the confiscation shall relate to a sum of money equivalent thereto. In this case, the judge may however reduce this sum in order not to subject the convicted person to an unreasonably heavy penalty.

The things referred to in paragraph 1, 2°, of this article constitute the object of the offence covered by this provision, within the meaning of article 42, 1°, and shall be confiscated, in respect of each of the perpetrators, co-perpetrators or accomplices of these offences, even if ownership thereof does not belong to the convicted person, without this penalty being able however to prejudice the rights of third parties over the property liable to be the object of confiscation. If these things cannot be found in the estate of the convicted person, the judge shall proceed to their monetary valuation and the confiscation shall relate to a sum of money which shall be proportional to the participation of the convicted person in the offence.) <L 2007-05-10/63,
