###### art. 2

, 071; En vigueur : 01-09-2007>

The attempt of the offences referred to in 2°, 3° and 4° of this article shall be punished by imprisonment from eight days to three years and a fine from twenty-six [euros] to fifty thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

Persons punished under these provisions may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33.

(1)<L 2013-07-15/02, art. 15, 094; En vigueur : 29-07-2013>

Art. 505bis. <inséré par L 2005-08-10/62, art. 7 ; En vigueur : 02-09-2005> Those who have received, in whole or in part, the things removed, misappropriated or obtained by means of the misdemeanor or crime referred to in article 433, shall be punished by the penalties provided for in article 505, paragraph 1, of which the minimum penalty is raised in the case of imprisonment to three months and in the case of a fine to one thousand euros.<L 2003-01- 23/42, art. 81, 041; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 506.

<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 81, 041; En vigueur : 13-03-2003> In the case where the penalty applicable to the perpetrators of the crime is that of imprisonment for life or imprisonment from twenty years to thirty years, (the receivers referred to in articles 505 and 505bis) shall be sentenced to imprisonment from five years to ten years, if they are convicted of having had, at the time of receiving, knowledge of the circumstances to which the law attaches either imprisonment for life or imprisonment from twenty years to thirty years. <LW 2005-08-10/62, art. 8, 054 ; En vigueur : 02-09-2005>

###### SECTION V. - OF CERTAIN OTHER FRAUDS.

###### Art. 507. Shall be punished by imprisonment from eight days to two years and a

fine from twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros], the person whose property has been seized and all those who have fraudulently destroyed or misappropriated, in his interest, objects seized from him. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

(The same provision is applicable to the spouse or to those who in his interest destroy, damage or misappropriate movable property which has been the subject of a measure provided for (in article 223 of the Civil Code) (and in articles 1253septies, second paragraph, and 1280 of the Judicial Code.)) <L 1990-04-09/35, art. 1, 003; En vigueur : 19-06-1990>

Art. 507bis. <inséré par L 1998-03-12/39, art. 45 ; En vigueur : 1998-10-02> Shall be punished by imprisonment from eight days to two years and a fine from twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros], whoever does not comply with the conditions set upon the lifting of an act of information or investigation, in accordance with articles 28sexies and 61quater of the Code of Criminal Procedure. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 508. Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to two years and a

fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros]: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

Those who, having found a movable thing belonging to another or having obtained possession of it by chance, shall have fraudulently concealed it or delivered it to third parties;

Those who, having discovered a treasure, shall have appropriated it to the prejudice of the persons to whom the law attributes a part thereof.

Art. 508bis. <L 23-03-1936, art. unique> Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three months and a fine of two hundred to fifteen hundred [euros], or one of these penalties only, whoever, knowing that he is in absolute impossibility of paying, shall have had served to him, in an establishment intended for that purpose, beverages or food which he shall have consumed in whole or in part, shall have had lodging given to him in a travelers' hotel or an inn, or shall have rented a hire vehicle. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002> In case of recidivism, the penalties may be doubled.

(Paragraph 3 repealed) <L 17-12-1963, art. 2>

Art. 508ter. <L 17-12-1963, art. 1> Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three months and a fine of two hundred to one thousand five hundred [euros], or one of these penalties only, whoever, after having had a vehicle supplied with fuel or lubricant, shall have fraudulently evaded immediate payment. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

In case of recidivism, the penalties may be doubled.

###### Art. 509. Shall be punished by imprisonment of one month to two years and a fine

of twenty-six [euros] to three thousand [euros], whoever shall have fraudulently procured funds, securities or discharges by means of a bill drawn on a person who does not exist or whom he knew not to be his debtor or not to have to be at maturity, and who had not authorized him to draw on her. <L 2000-06-26/42,

###### art. 2

, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

However, proceedings may not take place, or shall cease, if the bill has been paid, or if funds have been provided at the time the fraud was discovered, unless the drawee has filed a complaint.

In that case, the offender shall be sentenced to imprisonment of fifteen days to three months and to a fine of twenty-six [euros] to three hundred [euros], or to one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002> Art. 509bis. <L 02-05-1956, art. 28> Is punished by imprisonment of one month to two years and a fine of twenty-six to three thousand [euros]: <L 2000-06-26/42,

###### art. 2

, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

1° Whoever knowingly issues without sufficient and available funds, a postal check or a postal transfer;

2° Whoever transfers one of these instruments, knowing that the funds are not sufficient and available;

3° Whoever, having issued one of these instruments, knowingly withdraws, within six months of their issue, all or part of their funds;

4° Whoever, having issued one of these instruments, renders, with fraudulent intent or with intent to harm, all or part of the funds unavailable.

Art. 509ter. <L 31-03-1958, art. 3> Is punished by imprisonment of one month to two years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to three thousand [euros], or one of these penalties only: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002> 1° Whoever, after having endorsed an invoice, knowingly collects it for his own benefit;

2° Whoever, after having endorsed the original or a duplicate of an invoice, knowingly has funds given to him or has any advantage attributed to him, through the endorsement of another original copy or duplicate of the same invoice; 3° Whoever has funds given to him or has any advantage attributed to him by knowingly endorsing an invoice relating to a legally extinguished obligation.

Art. 509quater. <L 2007-05-15/62, art. 33, 072; En vigueur : 01-09-2007> Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three months and a fine of two hundred euros to fifteen hundred euros, or one of these penalties only, the expert who, knowing that a direct payment is not authorized, accepts it nevertheless from a party to the case.

##### CHAPTER III. - DESTRUCTIONS, DEGRADATIONS, DAMAGES.

###### SECTION I. - ON ARSON.

###### Art. 510. (See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 07-06-1963, art. 3> Shall be punished by (imprisonment) of fifteen years to twenty years those who shall have set fire to buildings, bridges, dikes, roads, railways, locks, warehouses, yards, sheds, ships, boats, carriages, wagons, aircraft or other engineering works, constructions or motor vehicles, if the perpetrator should have presumed that one or more persons were there at the time of the fire. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 82, 041; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

###### Art. 511.

<L 07-06-1963, art. 4> Shall be punished by (imprisonment) of ten years to fifteen years, those who shall have set fire to the immovable properties designated in article 510, as well as to ships, boats and aircraft, but outside the cases provided for by that article, or to forests, woods, coppices or standing crops. <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 82, 041; En vigueur : 13-03-2003>

However, if these properties belong exclusively to those who set fire to them, and if the fire was set with malicious or fraudulent intent, the offenders shall be punished by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of two hundred [euros] to one thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-

###### Art. 512.

<L 07-06-1963, art. 5> Shall be punished by imprisonment of one year to five years and a fine of one hundred [euros] to one thousand [euros], those who shall have intentionally set fire to the movable properties of another, other than ships, boats and aircraft, and on condition that the act was of a nature to cause serious prejudice to another. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur If the movable properties belong exclusively to those who set fire to them and if the fire was set with malicious or fraudulent intent, the penalties shall be imprisonment of six months to three years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-

###### Art. 513. (See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 83, 041; En vigueur : 13- 03-2003> When the fire shall have been set during the night, the penalties provided in articles 510 to 512 shall be replaced: imprisonment of fifteen years to twenty years, by imprisonment of twenty years to thirty years; imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years, by imprisonment of fifteen years to twenty years; the imprisonment and fine, provided in article 511, second paragraph, and in article 512, first paragraph, by imprisonment of five years to ten years; the imprisonment and fine, provided in article 512, second paragraph, by imprisonment of one year to four years and a fine of fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros] in article 512, second paragraph, by imprisonment of one year to four years and a fine of fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06- 26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 514. When arson carries a penalty of imprisonment, the attempt

of arson shall be punished by imprisonment of two months to two years and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

Art. 514bis.<L 2007-05-10/35, art. 39, 064; In force: 09-06-2007> In the cases provided for by articles 510 to 514, the minimum of the penalties provided for by these articles may be doubled if they are correctional penalties and increased by two years if they are imprisonment, when one of the motives for the crime or offence is hatred, contempt or hostility towards a person on account of their alleged race, skin colour, ancestry, national or ethnic origin, nationality, sex, sexual orientation, civil status, birth, age, fortune, religious or philosophical belief, current or future state of health, disability, language, political belief, [trade union belief,] physical or genetic characteristic or social origin.

(1)<L 2009-12-30/01, art. 115, 074; In force: 31-12-2009>

###### Art. 515. In the cases provided for by the preceding articles, the guilty party sentenced to

imprisonment may, in addition, be sentenced to disqualification, in accordance with article 33 (...). <L 09-04-1930, art. 32>

###### Art. 516. Any person who, with the intention of committing one of the acts provided for in

articles 510, 511 and 512, shall have set fire to any objects, placed so as to communicate it to the thing he wanted to destroy, shall be punished as if he had directly set or attempted to set fire to that latter thing.

###### Art. 517. When the fire is communicated from the object that the guilty party wanted

to burn to another object the destruction of which carries a stronger penalty, this latter penalty shall be pronounced, if the two things were placed in such a way that the fire must necessarily have been communicated from one to the other.

###### Art. 518.

<L 07-06-1963, art. 7> When the arson has caused injuries to one or more persons, and the perpetrator of the act should have presumed that they were in the premises set on fire at the time of the crime or offence, the guilty party shall be sentenced as if those injuries had been inflicted with premeditation, and the penalty that the law attaches thereto shall be applied to the guilty party if that penalty is stronger than that which he incurred by reason of the arson.

In the contrary case, this latter penalty shall be raised by two years above the maximum, if it consists of imprisonment (from fifteen years to twenty years or a lesser term). <L 2003-01-23/42, art. 84, 041; In force: 13-03-2003>

If the act has caused death, the penalty shall be (life imprisonment). <L 1996-07-10/42, art. 15, 018; In force: 11-08-1996>

###### Art. 519. Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three months and a

fine of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros], or one of these penalties only, the arson of movable or immovable property of another which has been caused either by the dilapidation or lack of repair or cleaning of ovens, chimneys, forges, nearby houses or factories, or by fires lit in fields, at less than one hundred metres from houses, buildings, forests, heaths, woods, orchards, plantations, hedges, stacks, piles of grain, straw, hay, fodder or any other deposit of combustible materials, or by fires or lights carried or left, or by fireworks lit or fired without sufficient precaution. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 520.

<L 07-06-1963, art. 8> Shall be punished by the penalties provided for by the preceding articles and according to the distinctions established therein, those who have destroyed or attempted to destroy, by the effect of an explosion, buildings, bridges, dykes, roadways, railways, locks, warehouses, yards, sheds, ships, boats, carriages, wagons, aircraft or other works of art, constructions or motor vehicles.

###### SECTION II. - DESTRUCTION OF CONSTRUCTIONS, STEAM

ENGINES AND TELEGRAPHIC APPARATUS.

###### Art. 521. (See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 07-06-1963, art. 9> Whoever has, outside the cases referred to in articles 510 to 520, destroyed, by any means whatsoever, in whole or in part, buildings, bridges, dykes, roadways, railways, locks, warehouses, yards, sheds, ships, boats, aircraft or other works of art, or constructions belonging to another, shall be punished by (imprisonment of In case of putting out of use with intent to harm, the penalty shall be imprisonment of fifteen days to three years and a fine of fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002> The penalty provided for in the second paragraph is applicable in case of destruction, in whole or in part, or putting out of use with intent to harm, of carriages, wagons and motor vehicles.

###### Art. 522. The provision of article 518 shall be applicable to the case provided for by the

preceding article.

###### Art. 523.

<L 07-06-1963, art. 10> Whoever has destroyed a machine belonging to another, intended to produce, transform or distribute motive energy or to consume it for purposes other than purely domestic, shall be sentenced to imprisonment of fifteen days to three years and to a fine of fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01- There is destruction as soon as the effects of the machine are prevented in whole or in part, whether the act relates to the motor apparatus or whether it relates to the apparatus set in motion.

###### Art. 524. (Repealed)

<L 13-10-1930, art. 31>

###### Art. 525. (See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 86, 041; In force: 13-03-2003> When the acts provided for by the two preceding articles have been committed in assembly or in a gang and by means of violence, assaults or threats, the guilty parties shall be punished by imprisonment of five years to ten years. The leaders and instigators shall be sentenced to imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years and to a fine of five hundred [euros] to five thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42,

###### art. 2

, In force: 01-01-2002>

Art. 525bis.<inserted by L 2007-05-10/35, art. 40; In force: 09-06-2007> In the cases provided for by articles 521 to 525, the minimum of the penalties provided for by these articles may be doubled if they are correctional penalties, and increased by two years if they are imprisonment, when one of the motives for the offence is hatred, contempt or hostility towards a person on account of their alleged race, skin colour, ancestry, national or ethnic origin, nationality, sex, sexual orientation, civil status, birth, age, fortune, religious or philosophical belief, current or future state of health, disability, language, political belief, [trade union belief,] physical or genetic characteristic or social origin.

(1)<L 2009-12-30/01, art. 116, 074; In force: 31-12-2009>

###### SECTION III. - DESTRUCTION OR DEFACEMENT OF TOMBS,

MONUMENTS, WORKS OF ART, TITLES, DOCUMENTS OR OTHER PAPERS.

###### Art. 526. Shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to one year and a fine

of twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros], whoever has destroyed, demolished, mutilated or defaced: <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

Tombs, commemorative signs or sepulchral stones;

Monuments, statues or other objects intended for public utility or decoration and erected by the competent authority or with its authorization;

Monuments, statues, paintings or any works of art, placed in churches, temples or other public buildings.

###### Art. 527. Anyone who has maliciously or fraudulently destroyed in any

manner whatsoever registers, minutes or original acts of public authority, titles, notes, bills of exchange, commercial or banking instruments containing or effecting obligation, disposition or discharge, shall be punished as if he had misappropriated the same documents and according to the distinctions established in the first chapter of this title.

###### SECTION IV. - DESTRUCTION OR DETERIORATION OF FOODSTUFFS,

GOODS OR OTHER MOVABLE PROPERTY.

###### Art. 528. Any destruction, any damage to the movable property of another executed

by means of violence or threats, shall be punished by imprisonment from eight days to three years and a fine from twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros], or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 529. (Voir NOTE sous TITRE)

<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 87, 041; En vigueur : 13- 03-2003> If the act was committed in assembly or in a gang, the penalty shall be imprisonment from five years to ten years.

The leaders and instigators shall be punished by imprisonment from ten years to fifteen years.

###### Art. 530. (Voir NOTE sous TITRE)

<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 88, 041; En vigueur : 13- 03-2003> The destruction or damage to the movable property of another, carried out by means of violence or threats, in an inhabited house or its outbuildings, and with one of the circumstances provided for in article 471, shall be punished by imprisonment from ten years to fifteen years.

The penalty shall not be less than twelve years if the crime was committed in assembly or in a gang.

The leaders and instigators shall be punished by imprisonment from fifteen years to twenty years.

###### Art. 531. If the violence or threats by means of which the destruction or

damage was committed have caused an illness or bodily injury of the nature of those provided for by article 400, the guilty persons shall be punished by the penalty immediately higher than that which they would have incurred under the terms of the two preceding articles.

###### Art. 532. Murder committed, either to facilitate the destruction or damage, or

to ensure its impunity, shall be punished (by life imprisonment). <L 1996-07- 10/42, art. 15, 018; En vigueur : 11-08-1996>

Art. 532bis.<L 2007-05-10/35, art. 41, 064; En vigueur : 09-06-2007> In the cases provided for by articles 528 to 532, the minimum of the penalties imposed by these articles may be doubled if they are correctional penalties, and increased by two years if they are imprisonment, when one of the motives for the offence is hatred, contempt or hostility towards a person on the grounds of their supposed race, skin colour, ancestry, national or ethnic origin, nationality, sex, sexual orientation, civil status, birth, age, fortune, religious or philosophical belief, current or future state of health, disability, language, political belief, [ trade union belief,] physical or genetic characteristic or social origin.

(1)<L 2009-12-30/01, art. 117, 074; En vigueur : 31-12-2009>

###### Art. 533. Anyone who has maliciously or fraudulently altered or deteriorated

goods or materials used for manufacture, shall be punished by imprisonment from one month to one year and a fine from twenty-six [euros] to three hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2,En vigueur : 01-01-2002> The imprisonment shall be from six months to three years and the fine from fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros], if the offence was committed by a person employed in the factory, workshop or commercial establishment. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur

###### Art. 534. Anyone who has maliciously removed, cut or destroyed the ties or

obstacles which hold a boat, a wagon or a vehicle, shall be punished by imprisonment from eight days to two years.

Section IVbis. - Graffiti and degradation of immovable property. <inséré par L 2007-01-25/39, art. 3, En vigueur : 02-03-2007>

Art. 534bis. <inséré par L 2007-01-25/39, art. 3, En vigueur : 02-03-2007> § 1er. Shall be punished by imprisonment from one month to six months and a fine from twenty-six euros to two hundred euros or by one of these penalties only, anyone who creates graffiti without authorization on movable or immovable property.

###### § 2. The maximum imprisonment is increased to one year of imprisonment in

the event of recidivism for an offence referred to in paragraph one within five years from the date of a prior judgment imposing a conviction and having become res judicata.

Art. 534ter. <inséré par L 2007-01-25/39, art. 3, En vigueur : 02-03-2007> Shall be punished by imprisonment from one month to six months and a fine from twenty-six euros to two hundred euros or by one of these penalties only, anyone who has intentionally degraded the immovable property of another.

Art. 534quater.<inséré par L 2007-05-10/35, art. 42, En vigueur : 09-06-2007> In the cases provided for by articles 534bis and 534ter, the minimum of the penalties imposed by these articles may be doubled if they are correctional penalties, and increased by two years if they are imprisonment, when one of the motives for the offence is hatred, contempt or hostility towards a person on the grounds of their supposed race, skin colour, ancestry, national or ethnic origin, nationality, sex, sexual orientation, civil status, birth, age, fortune, religious or philosophical belief, current or future state of health, disability, language, political belief, [ trade union belief,] physical or genetic characteristic or social origin.

(1)<L 2009-12-30/01, art. 118, 074; En vigueur : 31-12-2009>

###### SECTION V. - DESTRUCTION AND DEVASTATION OF CROPS, PLANTS, TREES,

GRAFTS, GRAIN AND FODDER, DESTRUCTION OF AGRICULTURAL
IMPLEMENTS.

###### Art. 535. Shall be punished by imprisonment from one month to three years and a fine

from twenty-six [euros] to five hundred [euros], anyone who has maliciously cut or devastated standing crops or plants grown naturally or by human hand. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 536. Shall be punished by imprisonment from one month to two years and a fine

from twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros], anyone who has maliciously ravaged a sown field, spread in a field darnel seed or any other harmful grass or plant, broken or put out of service agricultural implements, livestock pens or keepers' huts. <L 2000-06- 26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 537. Anyone who has maliciously felled one or more trees, cut,

mutilated or stripped these trees so as to cause them to perish, or destroyed one or more grafts, shall be punished:

For each tree, by imprisonment from eight days to three months and a fine from twenty-six [euros] to one hundred [euros]; <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

For each graft, by imprisonment from eight days to fifteen days and a fine from twenty-six [euros] to fifty [euros], or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

In no case shall the total penalty exceed three years for imprisonment, nor five hundred [euros] for the fine. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### SECTION VI. - DESTRUCTION OF ANIMALS.

###### Art. 538. Anyone who has poisoned horses or other beasts of burden or

draught, horned cattle, sheep, goats or pigs, shall be punished by imprisonment from three months to two years and a fine from twenty-six [euros] to three hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, En vigueur : 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 539. Anyone who throws into a river, canal, stream, pond,

fish pond or reservoir, substances of a nature to destroy fish and with the aim of achieving this result, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to three hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42,

###### art. 2

, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 540. Those who, without necessity, have killed one of the animals mentioned in

article 538, or have caused it serious injury, shall be punished as follows: If the offense was committed in the buildings, enclosures and outbuildings, or on the land of which the owner of the killed or injured animal was owner, tenant, settler or farmer, the penalty shall be imprisonment of one month to six months and a fine of fifty [euros] to three hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force If it was committed in places of which the offender was owner, tenant, settler or farmer, the penalty shall be imprisonment of eight days to two months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to one hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

If it was committed in any other place, the imprisonment shall be fifteen days to three months and the fine fifty [euros] to two hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 541. Anyone who, without necessity, has killed a domestic animal other than

those mentioned in article 538, or has caused it serious injury, in a place of which the person to whom this animal belongs is owner, usufructuary, user, tenant, settler or farmer, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to three months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros], or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002> The same penalties shall be imposed if these acts were maliciously committed on a tamed animal or on an animal kept in captivity, in the places where they are kept, or on a domestic animal at the moment when it was employed in the service for which it was intended and in a place where its owner had the right to be.

###### Art. 542. In the cases provided for in the preceding articles, if there has been a violation of

an enclosure, the minimum penalty shall be increased in accordance with article 266.

###### SECTION VII. - PROVISIONS COMMON TO THE PRECEDING SECTIONS.

###### Art. 543. If the acts provided for in sections V and VI of this chapter were

committed either in hatred of a public official and by reason of his functions, or during the night, the minimum penalty shall be increased in accordance with article 266.

###### Art. 544. (Repealed)

<L 09-04-1930, art. 32>

###### SECTION VIII. - DESTRUCTION OF ENCLOSURES, DISPLACEMENT OR

REMOVAL OF BOUNDARY MARKERS AND CORNER POSTS.

###### Art. 545. Anyone who has, in whole or in part, filled in ditches, cut or torn out live or dead hedges, destroyed rural or urban enclosures, of whatever materials they are made, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to six months and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to two hundred [euros], or by one of these penalties only;

displaced or removed boundary markers, corner posts or other trees planted or recognized to establish the boundaries between different properties. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 546. When the acts provided for by the preceding article were carried out with the

aim of committing land usurpation, the penalty shall be imprisonment of one month to one year and a fine of fifty [euros] to two thousand [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force:01-01-2002>

SECTION VIIIbis. [ - Intrusion into port areas.] (1)<Inserted by L 2016-05-20/04, art. 2, 117; In force: 12-06-2016>

###### Art. 546

/1. [ Anyone who enters or intrudes, without being authorized or permitted, into a port facility referred to in article 5, 6° and 7°, of the law of 5 February 2007 on maritime security, or into immovable or movable property located within the perimeter of the port within the meaning of the same law, shall be punished by imprisonment of eight days to six months and a fine of twenty-six euros to five hundred euros or by one of these penalties only.]

(1)<Inserted by L 2016-05-20/04, art. 3, 117; In force: 12-06-2016>

###### Art. 546

/2. [ § 1. The offense referred to in article 546/1 is punished by imprisonment of eight days to one year and a fine of twenty-six euros to one thousand euros or by one of these penalties only:

1° when the activity concerned constitutes a habitual activity;

2° if it was committed during the night;

3° if it was committed by two or more persons;

4° if it was committed with fraudulent intent or with intent to harm; 5° if it was committed by means of violence or threats;

6° if the person entered or intruded into a critical infrastructure within the meaning of the law of 1 July 2011 on the security and protection of critical infrastructures.

###### § 2. The attempt to commit the offense referred to in paragraph 1 of this

article is punished by imprisonment of eight days to six months and a fine of twenty-six euros to five hundred euros or by one of these penalties only.] (1)<Inserted by L 2016-05-20/04, art. 4, 117; In force: 12-06-2016>

###### Art. 546

/3. [ The penalties provided for in articles 546/1 and 546/2 are doubled if an offense against one of these provisions is committed within five years following the pronouncement of a conviction for one of these offenses.]

(1)<Inserted by L 2016-05-20/04, art. 5, 117; In force: 12-06-2016>

###### SECTION IX. - DESTRUCTION AND DAMAGE CAUSED BY FLOODING.

###### Art. 547. (See NOTE under TITLE)

<L 2003-01-23/42, art. 89, 041; In force: 13-03-2003> Anyone who has maliciously or fraudulently flooded all or part of the workings of a mine shall be punished by imprisonment of ten years to fifteen years. If, according to the circumstances, the offender should have presumed that one or more persons were in the mine at the time of the flooding, he shall be sentenced to imprisonment of fifteen years to twenty years.

###### Art. 548. The provision of article 518 shall be applicable to the act provided for by the

preceding article.

###### Art. 549. Any person who has maliciously or fraudulently flooded

another person's property, or has transmitted water to it in a damaging manner, shall be sentenced to a fine of twenty-six [euros] to three hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### Art. 550. Owners, farmers or any other persons operating mills, factories or ponds, who, by raising the spillway of their waters above the height determined by the competent authority, have flooded the roads or properties of others, shall be punished by a fine of fifty [euros] to five hundred [euros]. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

If any damage has resulted from these acts, the penalty shall be, in addition to the fine, imprisonment of eight days to one month.

TITRE IXbis. - Offenses against the confidentiality, integrity and availability of computer systems and data that are stored, processed or transmitted by these systems. <inserted by L 2000-11-28/34, art. 6; In force: 13-02-2001> Art. 550bis.<inserted by L 2000-11-28/34, art. 6; In force: 13-02-2001> § 1. Anyone who, knowing that he is not authorized, accesses a computer system or remains in it, shall be punished [ by imprisonment of six months to two years] and a fine of twenty-six [euros] to twenty-five thousand [euros] or by one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

If the offense referred to in paragraph 1 is committed with fraudulent intent,

[ the penalty of imprisonment is six months to three years] .

###### § 2. Anyone who, with fraudulent intent or with the aim of causing harm, exceeds

their access rights to a computer system, shall be punished [with imprisonment from six months to three years] and a fine from twenty-six [euros] to twenty-five thousand [euros] or one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force

###### § 3. Anyone who is in one of the situations referred to in §§ 1 and 2 and who:

1° either retrieves, in any manner whatsoever, the data stored, processed or transmitted by the computer system;

2° either makes any use whatsoever of a computer system belonging to a third party or uses the computer system to access the computer system of a third party;

3° either causes any damage whatsoever, even unintentionally, to the computer system or to the data that are stored processed or transmitted by that system or to the computer system of a third party or to the data that are stored, processed or transmitted by that system; shall be punished [with imprisonment from one to five years] and a fine from twenty-six [euros] Belgian francs to fifty thousand [euros] or one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### § 4. The attempt to commit one of the offences referred to in §§ 1 and 2 shall be

punished with the same penalties.

###### § 5. (Anyone who, unlawfully, possesses, produces, sells, obtains for use,

imports, distributes or makes available in any other form, any device, including computer data, primarily designed or adapted to enable the commission of the offences provided for in §§ 1 to 4, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine from twenty-six euros to one hundred thousand euros or one of these penalties only.) <L 2006-05-15/46, art. 5, 059; In force: 22-09-2006>

###### § 6. Anyone who orders the commission of one of the offences referred to in §§ 1 to 5

or who incites it, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to five years and a fine from one hundred [euros] to two hundred thousand [euros] or one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### § 7. Anyone who, knowing that data have been obtained through the commission

of one of the offences referred to in §§ 1 to 3, possesses them, discloses them to another person or divulges them, or makes any use of the data thus obtained, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine from twenty-six [euros] to one hundred thousand [euros] or one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### § 8. The penalties provided for in §§ 1 to 7 shall be doubled if an offence against one

of these provisions is committed within five years following the pronouncement of a conviction for one of these offences or for one of the offences referred to in articles 210bis, 259bis, 314bis, 504quater or 550ter.

(1)<L 2017-07-06/24, art. 214, 124; In force: 03-08-2017>

Art. 550ter.<inserted by L 2000-11-28/34, art. 6; In force: 13-02-2001> § 1. (Anyone who, knowing that they are not authorized to do so, directly or indirectly, introduces into a computer system, modifies or deletes data, or who modifies by any technological means the normal use of data in a computer system, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine from twenty-six euros to twenty-five thousand euros or one of these penalties only.

If the offence referred to in paragraph 1 is committed with fraudulent intent or with the aim of causing harm, the penalty of imprisonment shall be from six months to five years.) <L 2006-05-15/46, art. 6, 1°, 059; In force: 22-09-2006>

[The same penalty shall apply when the offence referred to in paragraph 1 is committed against a computer system of a critical infrastructure as referred to in article 3, 4°, of the Act of 1 July 2011 on the security and protection of critical infrastructures.]

###### § 2. Anyone who, following the commission of an offence referred to in § 1, causes

damage to data in the computer system concerned or in any other computer system, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to five years and a fine from twenty-six [euros] to seventy-five thousand [euros] or one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-2002>

###### § 3.

Anyone who, following the commission of an offence referred to in § 1, prevents, totally or partially, the proper functioning of the computer system concerned or of any other computer system, shall be punished with imprisonment from one year to five years and a fine from twenty-six [euros] to one hundred thousand [euros] or one of these penalties only. <L 2000-06-26/42, art. 2, In force: 01-01-

###### § 4. (Anyone who, unlawfully, possesses, produces, sells, obtains for use,

imports, distributes or makes available in any other form, a device including computer data, primarily designed or adapted to enable the commission of the offences provided for in §§ 1 to 3, when they know that such data can be used to cause damage to data or to prevent, totally or partially, the proper functioning of a computer system, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine from twenty-six euros to one hundred thousand euros or one of these penalties only.) <L 2006-05-15/46, art. 6, 2°, 059; In force: 22-09-2006>

###### § 5. The penalties provided for in §§ 1 to 4 shall be doubled if an offence against one

of these provisions is committed within five years following the pronouncement of a conviction for one of these offences or for one of the offences referred to in articles 210bis, 259bis, 314bis, 504quater or 550bis.

###### (§ 6. The attempt to commit the offence referred to in § 1 shall be punished with the same

penalties.) <L 2006-05-15/46, art. 6, 3°, 059; In force: 22-09-2006> (1)<L 2017-07-06/24, art. 215, 124; In force: 03-08-2017>
